




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

aṅguttaranikāyau


chak‍kanipātapāḷi


1. paṭhamapaṇṇāsakaṃ


1. āhunaiyyavaggau

1. paṭhamaāhunaiyyasuttaṃ

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau  añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi chahi dī॰ ni॰ 3.328; paṭi॰ ma॰ 3.17? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau, upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau, upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau, upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau, upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau, upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñā naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau, upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyaāhunaiyyasuttaṃ

2. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi chahi dī॰ ni॰ 3.356? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti; āvibhāvaṃ tiraubhāvaṃ; tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchati, saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karauti, saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchati, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamati, saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parimasati parāmasati (ka॰) parimaj‍jati; yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti|

‘‘dibbāya, sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇāti – dibbai ca mānusai ca, yai dūrai santikai ca|

‘‘parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti| sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittanti pajānāti, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ… vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ… samauhaṃ  vā cittaṃ… vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ… saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ… vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ… mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ… amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ… sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ… anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ… samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ… asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ… vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ… avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ avimuttaṃ cittanti pajānāti|

‘‘anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰…| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

‘‘dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ  vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā| imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti|

‘‘āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ  diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. indriyasuttaṃ

3. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ  laukassa| katamaihi chahi? saddhindriyaina, vīriyindriyaina, satindriyaina, samādhindriyaina, pañ‍ñindriyaina, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. balasuttaṃ

4. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi chahi? saddhābalaina, vīriyabalaina, satibalaina, samādhibalaina, pañ‍ñābalaina, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamaājānīyasuttaṃ

5. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau khamau hauti rūpānaṃ, khamau saddānaṃ, khamau gandhānaṃ, khamau rasānaṃ, khamau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ, vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti rūpānaṃ, khamau saddānaṃ, khamau gandhānaṃ, khamau rasānaṃ, khamau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ, khamau dhammānaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyaājānīyasuttaṃ

6. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau khamau hauti rūpānaṃ, khamau saddānaṃ, khamau gandhānaṃ, khamau rasānaṃ, khamau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ, balasampan‍nau ca hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti rūpānaṃ  …pai॰… khamau dhammānaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. tatiyaājānīyasuttaṃ

7. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau khamau hauti rūpānaṃ, khamau saddānaṃ, khamau gandhānaṃ, khamau rasānaṃ, khamau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ, javasampan‍nau ca hauti| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, chahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti rūpānaṃ…pai॰… khamau dhammānaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. anuttariyasuttaṃ

8. dī॰ ni॰ 3.327 ‘‘chayimāni, bhikkhavai, anuttariyāni| katamāni cha? dassanānuttariyaṃ, savanānuttariyaṃ, lābhānuttariyaṃ, sikkhānuttariyaṃ, pāricariyānuttariyaṃ, anussatānuttariyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cha anuttariyānī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. anussatiṭṭhānasuttaṃ

9. dī॰ ni॰ 3.327 ‘‘chayimāni, bhikkhavai, anussatiṭṭhānāni| katamāni cha? buddhānussati, dhammānussati, saṅghānussati, sīlānussati, cāgānussati, daivatānussati – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cha anussatiṭṭhānānī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. mahānāmasuttaṃ

10. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau mahānāmau sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau, khau mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yau sau, bhantai, ariyasāvakau āgataphalau viñ‍ñātasāsanau, sau katamaina vihāraina bahulaṃ viharatī’’ti?

‘‘yau  sau, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau āgataphalau  viñ‍ñātasāsanau, sau iminā vihāraina bahulaṃ viharati| a॰ ni॰ 11.11 idha, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau tathāgataṃ anussarati – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| yasmiṃ, mahānāma, samayai ariyasāvakau tathāgataṃ anussarati naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti tathāgataṃ ārabbha| ujugatacittau khau pana, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaidaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ| pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidiyati, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, mahānāma – ‘ariyasāvakau  visamagatāya pajāya samappattau viharati, sabyāpaj‍jāya pajāya  abyāpaj‍jau viharati, dhammasautaṃ samāpan‍nau buddhānussatiṃ bhāvaiti’’’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau dhammaṃ anussarati – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| yasmiṃ, mahānāma, samayai ariyasāvakau dhammaṃ anussarati naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti dhammaṃ ārabbha| ujugatacittau khau pana, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaidaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhita pāmauj‍jaṃ| pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidiyati, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, mahānāma – ‘ariyasāvakau visamagatāya pajāya samappattau viharati, sabyāpaj‍jāya pajāya abyāpaj‍jau viharati, dhammasautaṃ samāpan‍nau dhammānussatiṃ bhāvaiti’’’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau saṅghaṃ anussarati – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ujuppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ñāyappaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā aisa bhagavatau  sāvakasaṅghau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti| yasmiṃ, mahānāma, samayai ariyasāvakau saṅghaṃ anussarati naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti saṅghaṃ ārabbha| ujugatacittau khau pana, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaidaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ| pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidiyati, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, mahānāma – ‘ariyasāvakau visamagatāya pajāya samappattau viharati, sabyāpaj‍jāya pajāya abyāpaj‍jau viharati, dhammasautaṃ samāpan‍nau saṅghānussatiṃ bhāvaiti’’’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau attanau sīlāni anussarati akhaṇḍāni acchiddāni asabalāni akammāsāni bhujissāni viñ‍ñuppasatthāni aparāmaṭṭhāni samādhisaṃvattanikāni| yasmiṃ, mahānāma, samayai ariyasāvakau sīlaṃ anussarati naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti sīlaṃ ārabbha| ujugatacittau khau pana, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaidaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ| pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidiyati, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, mahānāma – ‘ariyasāvakau visamagatāya pajāya samappattau viharati, sabyāpaj‍jāya pajāya abyāpaj‍jau viharati, dhammasautaṃ samāpan‍nau sīlānussatiṃ bhāvaiti’’’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau attanau cāgaṃ anussarati – ‘lābhā vata mai, suladdhaṃ vata mai! yauhaṃ macchairamalapariyuṭṭhitāya pajāya  vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasāmi muttacāgau payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau’ti| yasmiṃ, mahānāma, samayai ariyasāvakau cāgaṃ anussarati naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti cāgaṃ ārabbha| ujugatacittau khau pana, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaidaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ| pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidiyati, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, mahānāma – ‘ariyasāvakau visamagatāya pajāya samappattau viharati, sabyāpaj‍jāya pajāya abyāpaj‍jau viharati, dhammasautaṃ samāpan‍nau cāgānussatiṃ bhāvaiti’’’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau daivatānussatiṃ bhāvaiti – ‘santi daivā cātumahārājikā cātummahārājikā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), santi daivā tāvatiṃsā, santi daivā yāmā, santi daivā tusitā, santi daivā nimmānaratinau, santi daivā paranimmitavasavattinau, santi daivā brahmakāyikā, santi daivā tatuttari tatuttariṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), taduttari (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.25; visuddhi॰ 1.162 passitabbaṃ| yathārūpāya saddhāya saman‍nāgatā tā daivatā itau cutā tattha upapan‍nā tattha uppan‍nā (sī॰), tatthūpapan‍nā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), tatthupapan‍nā (a॰ ni॰ 3.71), mayhampi tathārūpā saddhā saṃvij‍jati| yathārūpaina sīlaina saman‍nāgatā tā daivatā itau cutā tattha upapan‍nā, mayhampi tathārūpaṃ sīlaṃ saṃvij‍jati| yathārūpaina sutaina saman‍nāgatā  tā daivatā itau cutā tattha upapan‍nā, mayhampi tathārūpaṃ sutaṃ saṃvij‍jati| yathārūpaina cāgaina saman‍nāgatā tā daivatā itau cutā tattha upapan‍nā, mayhampi tathārūpau  cāgau saṃvij‍jati| yathārūpāya pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatā tā daivatā itau cutā tattha upapan‍nā, mayhampi tathārūpā pañ‍ñā saṃvij‍jatī’ti| yasmiṃ, mahānāma, samayai ariyasāvakau attanau ca tāsañ‍ca daivatānaṃ saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarati naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti tā daivatā ārabbha| ujugatacittau khau pana, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaidaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ| pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidiyati, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, mahānāma – ‘ariyasāvakau visamagatāya pajāya samappattau viharati, sabyāpaj‍jāya sabyāpajjhāya… abyāpajjhau (ka॰) pajāya abyāpaj‍jau sabyāpajjhāya… abyāpajjhau (ka॰) viharati, dhammasautaṃ samāpan‍nau daivatānussatiṃ bhāvaiti’’’|

‘‘yau sau, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau āgataphalau viñ‍ñātasāsanau, sau iminā vihāraina bahulaṃ viharatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

āhunaiyyavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

dvai āhunaiyyā indriya, balāni tayau ājānīyā|

anuttariya anussatī, mahānāmaina tai dasāti||


2. sāraṇīyavaggau

1. paṭhamasāraṇīyasuttaṃ

11. ‘‘chayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā sāraṇīyā sārāṇīyā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| katamai cha? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau maittaṃ kāyakammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau maittaṃ vacīkammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau maittaṃ manaukammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yai tai lābhā dhammikā dhammaladdhā antamasau pattapariyāpan‍namattampi tathārūpaihi lābhaihi appaṭivibhattabhaugī hauti sīlavantaihi sabrahmacārīhi sādhāraṇabhaugī, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yāni tāni sīlāni akhaṇḍāni acchiddāni asabalāni akammāsāni bhujissāni viñ‍ñuppasatthāni aparāmaṭṭhāni samādhisaṃvattanikāni  tathārūpaihi sīlaihi sīlasāmañ‍ñagatau viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi caiva rahau ca, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yāyaṃ diṭṭhi ariyā niyyānikā niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāya tathārūpāya diṭṭhiyā diṭṭhisāmañ‍ñagatau viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi caiva  rahau ca, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammā sāraṇīyā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyasāraṇīyasuttaṃ

12. ‘‘chayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā sāraṇīyā piyakaraṇā garukaraṇā saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattanti| katamai cha? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau maittaṃ kāyakammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau maittaṃ vacīkammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti…pai॰… maittaṃ manaukammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yai tai lābhā dhammikā dhammaladdhā antamasau pattapariyāpan‍namattampi tathārūpaihi lābhaihi appaṭivibhattabhaugī hauti sīlavantaihi  sabrahmacārīhi sādhāraṇabhaugī, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yāni tāni sīlāni akhaṇḍāni acchiddāni asabalāni akammāsāni bhujissāni viñ‍ñuppasatthāni aparāmaṭṭhāni samādhisaṃvattanikāni tathārūpaihi sīlaihi sīlasāmañ‍ñagatau viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi caiva rahau ca, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yāyaṃ diṭṭhi ariyā niyyānikā niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāya tathārūpāya diṭṭhiyā diṭṭhisāmañ‍ñagatau viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi caiva rahau  ca, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā  aikībhāvāya saṃvattati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammā sāraṇīyā piyakaraṇā garukaraṇā saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattantī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. nissāraṇīyasuttaṃ

13. ‘‘chayimā, bhikkhavai, nissāraṇīyā dhātuyau| katamā cha? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘maittā hi khau mai caitauvimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā; atha ca pana mai byāpādau cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī’ti| sau ‘mā haiva’ntissa vacanīyau – ‘māyasmā, aivaṃ avaca; mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, āvusau, anavakāsau yaṃ maittāya caitauvimuttiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya; atha ca  panassa byāpādau cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati ṭhassatīti (sabbattha) dī॰ ni॰ 3.326 passitabbaṃ, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| nissaraṇañhaitaṃ, āvusau, byāpādassa yadidaṃ maittācaitauvimuttī’’’ti maittācaitauvimutti (sabbattha)|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘karuṇā hi khau mai caitauvimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā; atha ca pana mai vihaisā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī’ti| sau ‘mā haiva’ntissa vacanīyau – ‘māyasmā, aivaṃ avaca; mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, āvusau, anavakāsau yaṃ karuṇāya caitauvimuttiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya; atha ca panassa vihaisā cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| nissaraṇañhaitaṃ, āvusau, vihaisāya yadidaṃ karuṇācaitauvimuttī’’’ti|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘muditā hi khau mai caitauvimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā; atha ca pana mai arati cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī’ti| sau ‘mā haiva’ntissa vacanīyau – ‘māyasmā, aivaṃ avaca; mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, āvusau, anavakāsau yaṃ muditāya caitauvimuttiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya; atha ca panassa arati cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| nissaraṇañhaitaṃ, āvusau, aratiyā yadidaṃ muditācaitauvimuttī’’’ti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘upaikkhā hi khau mai caitauvimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā; atha ca pana mai rāgau cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī’ti| sau ‘mā haiva’ntissa vacanīyau – ‘māyasmā, aivaṃ avaca; mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, āvusau, anavakāsau yaṃ upaikkhāya caitauvimuttiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya  paricitāya susamāraddhāya; atha ca panassa rāgau cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| nissaraṇañhaitaṃ, āvusau, rāgassa yadidaṃ upaikkhācaitauvimuttī’’’ti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘animittā hi khau mai caitauvimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā; atha ca pana mai nimittānusāri viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hautī’ti| sau ‘mā haiva’ntissa vacanīyau – ‘māyasmā, aivaṃ avaca; mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, āvusau, anavakāsau yaṃ animittāya caitauvimuttiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya; atha ca panassa nimittānusāri viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissati, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| nissaraṇañhaitaṃ, āvusau, sabbanimittānaṃ yadidaṃ animittācaitauvimuttī’’’ti|

‘‘idha  pana bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘asmīti khau mai vigataṃ vigatai (syā॰), ayamahamasmīti ca ayaṃ cakārau dī॰ ni॰ 3.326 natthi na samanupassāmi; atha ca pana mai vicikicchākathaṃkathāsal‍laṃ cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī’ti| sau ‘mā haiva’ntissa vacanīyau – ‘māyasmā, aivaṃ avaca; mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, āvusau, anavakāsau yaṃ asmīti vigatai ayamahamasmīti ca na samanupassatau; atha  ca panassa vicikicchākathaṃkathāsal‍laṃ cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| nissaraṇañhaitaṃ, āvusau, vicikicchākathaṃkathāsal‍lassa yadidaṃ asmīti mānasamugghātau’ti| imā  khau, bhikkhavai, cha nissāraṇīyā dhātuyau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. bhaddakasuttaṃ

14. tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau, bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau  sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘tathā tathā, āvusau, bhikkhu vihāraṃ kappaiti yathā yathāssa vihāraṃ kappayatau na bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hauti, na bhaddikā kālakiriyā kālaṃkiriyā (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 3.110| kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu tathā tathā vihāraṃ kappaiti yathā yathāssa vihāraṃ kappayatau na bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hauti, na bhaddikā kālakiriyā?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu kammārāmau hauti kammaratau kammārāmataṃ anuyuttau, bhassārāmau hauti bhassaratau bhassārāmataṃ anuyuttau, niddārāmau hauti niddāratau niddārāmataṃ anuyuttau, saṅgaṇikārāmau hauti saṅgaṇikaratau saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ anuyuttau, saṃsaggārāmau hauti saṃsaggaratau saṃsaggārāmataṃ anuyuttau, papañ‍cārāmau hauti papañ‍caratau papañ‍cārāmataṃ anuyuttau| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu tathā tathā vihāraṃ kappaiti yathā yathāssa vihāraṃ kappayatau na bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hauti, na bhaddikā kālakiriyā| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘bhikkhu sak‍kāyābhiratau nappajahāsi na pahāsi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) sak‍kāyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāya’’’|

‘‘tathā  tathāvusau, bhikkhu vihāraṃ kappaiti yathā yathāssa vihāraṃ kappayatau bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hauti, bhaddikā kālakiriyā| kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu tathā tathā vihāraṃ kappaiti yathā yathāssa vihāraṃ kappayatau bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hauti, bhaddikā kālakiriyā?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu  na kammārāmau hauti na kammaratau na kammārāmataṃ anuyuttau, na bhassārāmau hauti na bhassaratau na bhassārāmataṃ anuyuttau, na niddārāmau hauti na niddāratau  niddārāmataṃ anuyuttau, na saṅgaṇikārāmau hauti na saṅgaṇikaratau na saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ anuyuttau, na saṃsaggārāmau hauti na saṃsaggaratau na saṃsaggārāmataṃ anuyuttau, na papañ‍cārāmau hauti na papañ‍caratau na papañ‍cārāmataṃ anuyuttau| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu tathā  tathā vihāraṃ kappaiti yathā yathāssa vihāraṃ kappayatau bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hauti, bhaddikā kālakiriyā| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘bhikkhu nibbānābhiratau pajahāsi sak‍kāyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’’ti|

‘‘yau papañ‍camanuyuttau, papañ‍cābhiratau magau|

virādhayī sau nibbānaṃ, yaugakkhaimaṃ anuttaraṃ||

‘‘yau ca papañ‍caṃ hitvāna, nippapañ‍capadai ratau|

ārādhayī sau nibbānaṃ, yaugakkhaimaṃ anuttara’’nti|| catutthaṃ|

5. anutappiyasuttaṃ

15. tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘tathā tathāvusau, bhikkhu vihāraṃ kappaiti yathā yathāssa vihāraṃ kappayatau kālakiriyā anutappā hauti| kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu tathā tathā vihāraṃ kappaiti yathā yathāssa vihāraṃ kappayatau kālakiriyā anutappā hauti?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu kammārāmau hauti kammaratau kammārāmataṃ anuyuttau, bhassārāmau hauti…pai॰… niddārāmau hauti… saṅgaṇikārāmau hauti… saṃsaggārāmau hauti… papañ‍cārāmau hauti papañ‍caratau papañ‍cārāmataṃ  anuyuttau| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu tathā tathā vihāraṃ kappaiti yathā yathāssa vihāraṃ kappayatau kālakiriyā anutappā hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘bhikkhu sak‍kāyābhiratau nappajahāsi sak‍kāyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāya’’’|

‘‘tathā tathāvusau, bhikkhu vihāraṃ kappaiti yathā yathāssa vihāraṃ kappayatau kālakiriyā ananutappā  hauti| kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu tathā tathā vihāraṃ kappaiti yathā yathāssa vihāraṃ kappayatau kālakiriyā ananutappā hauti?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu na kammārāmau hauti na kammaratau na kammārāmataṃ anuyuttau, na bhassārāmau hauti…pai॰… na  niddārāmau hauti… na saṅgaṇikārāmau hauti… na saṃsaggārāmau hauti… na papañ‍cārāmau hauti na papañ‍caratau na papañ‍cārāmataṃ anuyuttau| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu tathā tathā vihāraṃ kappaiti yathā yathāssa vihāraṃ kappayatau kālakiriyā ananutappā hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘bhikkhu nibbānābhiratau pajahāsi sak‍kāyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’’ti|

‘‘yau papañ‍camanuyuttau, papañ‍cābhiratau magau|

virādhayī sau nibbānaṃ, yaugakkhaimaṃ anuttaraṃ||

‘‘yau ca papañ‍caṃ hitvāna, nippapañ‍capadai ratau|

ārādhayī sau nibbānaṃ, yaugakkhaimaṃ anuttara’’nti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. nakulapitusuttaṃ

16. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā bhaggaisu viharati susumāragirai suṃsumāragirai (sī॰ pī॰), saṃsumāragirai (katthaci) bhaisakaḷāvanai  migadāyai| taina khau pana samayaina nakulapitā gahapati ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau nakulamātā gahapatānī nakulapitaraṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘mā khau tvaṃ, gahapati, sāpaikkhau sāpaikhau (pī॰ ka॰) kālamakāsi| dukkhā, gahapati, sāpaikkhassa kālakiriyā; garahitā ca bhagavatā sāpaikkhassa kālakiriyā| siyā khau pana tai, gahapati, aivamassa – ‘na nakulamātā gahapatānī mamac‍cayaina sakkhissati na sakkhissati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), sak‍kauti (pī॰ ka॰) dārakai pausaituṃ, gharāvāsaṃ sandharitu’nti sandharitunti (ka॰), saṇṭharituṃ (syā॰)| na khau panaitaṃ, gahapati, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kusalāhaṃ, gahapati, kappāsaṃ  kantituṃ vaiṇiṃ aulikhituṃ| sak‍kaumahaṃ, gahapati, tavac‍cayaina dārakai pausaituṃ, gharāvāsaṃ  sandharituṃ| tasmātiha  tvaṃ, gahapati, mā sāpaikkhau kālamakāsi| dukkhā, gahapati, sāpaikkhassa kālakiriyā; garahitā ca bhagavatā sāpaikkhassa kālakiriyā|

‘‘siyā khau pana tai, gahapati, aivamassa – ‘nakulamātā gahapatānī mamac‍cayaina añ‍ñaṃ gharaṃ bhattāraṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), vīraṃ (sī॰) gamissatī’ti| na khau panaitaṃ, gahapati, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| tvañ‍caiva khau, gahapati, jānāsi ahañ‍ca, yaṃ nau yadā tai (sī॰), yathā (syā॰), yathā nau (pī॰) sauḷasavassāni gahaṭṭhakaṃ brahmacariyaṃ samāciṇṇaṃ samādin‍naṃ (sī॰)| tasmātiha tvaṃ, gahapati, mā sāpaikkhau kālamakāsi| dukkhā, gahapati, sāpaikkhassa kālakiriyā; garahitā ca bhagavatā sāpaikkhassa kālakiriyā|

‘‘siyā khau pana tai, gahapati, aivamassa – ‘nakulamātā gahapatānī mamac‍cayaina na dassanakāmā bhavissati bhagavatau na dassanakāmā bhikkhusaṅghassā’ti| na khau panaitaṃ, gahapati, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| ahañhi, gahapati, tavac‍cayaina dassanakāmatarā caiva bhavissāmi bhagavatau, dassanakāmatarā  ca bhikkhusaṅghassa| tasmātiha tvaṃ, gahapati, mā sāpaikkhau kālamakāsi| dukkhā, gahapati, sāpaikkhassa kālakiriyā; garahitā ca bhagavatā sāpaikkhassa kālakiriyā|

‘‘siyā khau pana tai, gahapati, aivamassa – ‘na nakulamātā gahapatānī mamac‍cayaina sīlaisu nakulamātā… na sīlaisu (sī॰ pī॰) paripūrakārinī’ti| na khau panaitaṃ, gahapati, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| yāvatā khau, gahapati, tassa bhagavatau sāvikā gihī audātavasanā sīlaisu paripūrakāriniyau, ahaṃ tāsaṃ añ‍ñatarā| yassa khau panassa kaṅkhā vā vimati vā – ayaṃ sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bhaggaisu viharati susumāragirai bhaisakaḷāvanai migadāyai – taṃ bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchatu| tasmātiha tvaṃ, gahapati, mā sāpaikkhau kālamakāsi| dukkhā, gahapati, sāpaikkhassa kālakiriyā; garahitā ca bhagavatā sāpaikkhassa kālakiriyā|

‘‘siyā  khau pana tai, gahapati, aivamassa – ‘na nakulamātā gahapatānī lābhinī nakulamātā gahapatānī na lābhinī (pī॰) ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassā’ti| na khau panaitaṃ, gahapati, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| yāvatā khau, gahapati, tassa bhagavatau sāvikā gihī audātavasanā lābhiniyau ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa, ahaṃ tāsaṃ añ‍ñatarā| yassa khau panassa kaṅkhā vā vimati vā – ayaṃ sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau  bhaggaisu viharati susumāragirai bhaisakaḷāvanai migadāyai – taṃ bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchatu| tasmātiha tvaṃ, gahapati, mā sāpaikkhau kālamakāsi| dukkhā, gahapati, sāpaikkhassa kālakiriyā; garahitā ca bhagavatā sāpaikkhassa kālakiriyā|

‘‘siyā khau pana tai, gahapati, aivamassa – ‘na nakulamātā  gahapatānī imasmiṃ dhammavinayai augādhappattā patigādhappattā assāsappattā tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vaisāraj‍jappattā aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai viharatī’ti| na khau panaitaṃ, gahapati, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| yāvatā khau, gahapati, tassa bhagavatau sāvikā gihī audātavasanā imasmiṃ dhammavinayai augādhappattā patigādhappattā assāsappattā tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vaisāraj‍jappattā aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai viharanti, ahaṃ tāsaṃ añ‍ñatarā| yassa khau panassa kaṅkhā vā vimati vā – ayaṃ sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bhaggaisu viharati susumāragirai bhaisakaḷāvanai migadāyai – taṃ bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchatu| tasmātiha tvaṃ, gahapati, mā sāpaikkhau kālamakāsi| dukkhā gahapati sāpaikkhassa kālakiriyā; garahitā ca bhagavatā sāpaikkhassa kālakiriyā’’ti|

atha khau nakulapitunau gahapatissa nakulamātarā  nakulamātāya (sī॰ syā॰), nakulamātuyā (ka॰) gahapatāniyā iminā auvādaina auvadiyamānassa sau ābādhau ṭhānasau paṭippassambhi| vuṭṭhahi vuṭṭhāti (ka॰) ca nakulapitā gahapati tamhā ābādhā; tathā pahīnau ca pana nakulapitunau gahapatissa sau ābādhau ahausi| atha khau nakulapitā gahapati gilānā vuṭṭhitau ‘‘gilānabhāvatau vuṭṭhāya ṭhitau, bhāvappadhānau hi ayaṃ niddaisau’’ti ṭīkāsaṃvaṇṇanā aciravuṭṭhitau gailañ‍ñā daṇḍamaulubbha  yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau nakulapitaraṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘lābhā tai, gahapati, suladdhaṃ tai, gahapati! yassa tai nakulamātā  gahapatānī anukampikā atthakāmā auvādikā anusāsikā| yāvatā khau, gahapati, mama sāvikā gihī audātavasanā sīlaisu paripūrakāriniyau, nakulamātā gahapatānī tāsaṃ añ‍ñatarā| yāvatā khau, gahapati, mama sāvikā gihī audātavasanā lābhiniyau ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa, nakulamātā gahapatānī tāsaṃ añ‍ñatarā| yāvatā khau, gahapati, mama sāvikā gihī audātavasanā imasmiṃ dhammavinayai augādhappattā patigādhappattā assāsappattā tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā  vaisāraj‍jappattā aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai viharanti, nakulamātā gahapatānī tāsaṃ añ‍ñatarā| lābhā tai, gahapati, suladdhaṃ tai, gahapati! yassa tai nakulamātā gahapatānī anukampikā atthakāmā auvādikā anusāsikā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sauppasuttaṃ

17. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yainupaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| āyasmāpi khau sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yainupaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| āyasmāpi khau mahāmauggal‍lānau…pai॰… āyasmāpi khau mahākassapau… āyasmāpi khau mahākac‍cāyanau… āyasmāpi khau mahākauṭṭhikau mahākauṭṭhitau (sī॰ pī॰) … āyasmāpi khau mahācundau… āyasmāpi khau mahākappinau… āyasmāpi khau anuruddhau… āyasmāpi khau raivatau… āyasmāpi khau  ānandau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yainupaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| atha khau bhagavā bahudaiva rattiṃ nisaj‍jāya vītināmaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi| taipi khau āyasmantau  acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau uṭṭhāyāsanā yathāvihāraṃ agamaṃsu| yai pana tattha bhikkhū navā acirapabbajitā adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ tai yāva sūriyuggamanā kākacchamānā supiṃsu| addasā khau bhagavā dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina tai bhikkhū yāva sūriyuggamanā kākacchamānai supantai| disvā yainupaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘kahaṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau? kahaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānau? kahaṃ mahākassapau? kahaṃ mahākac‍cāyanau? kahaṃ mahākauṭṭhikau? kahaṃ mahācundau? kahaṃ mahākappinau? kahaṃ anuruddhau? kahaṃ raivatau? kahaṃ ānandau? kahaṃ nu khau tai, bhikkhavai, thairā sāvakā gatā’’ti? ‘‘taipi khau, bhantai, āyasmantau acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau uṭṭhāyāsanā yathāvihāraṃ agamaṃsū’’ti| ‘‘kaina nau kaina nau (ka॰), kai nu (katthaci) tumhai, bhikkhavai, thairā bhikkhū nāgatāti bhikkhū navā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), bhikkhū gatāti (?) yāva sūriyuggamanā kākacchamānā supatha? taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu tumhaihi diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ  vā – ‘rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau muddhābhisittau (ka॰) yāvadatthaṃ saiyyasukhaṃ  passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau viharantau yāvajīvaṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraintau janapadassa vā piyau manāpau’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai! mayāpi khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, naiva  diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ – ‘rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau yāvadatthaṃ saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau viharantau yāvajīvaṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraintau janapadassa vā piyau manāpau’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu tumhaihi diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā – ‘raṭṭhikau…pai॰… paittaṇikau… saināpatikau… gāmagāmaṇikau gāmagāmikau (sī॰ pī॰) … pūgagāmaṇikau yāvadatthaṃ saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau viharantau yāvajīvaṃ pūgagāmaṇikattaṃ kāraintau pūgassa vā piyau manāpau’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai! mayāpi khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, naiva diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ – ‘pūgagāmaṇikau yāvadatthaṃ saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau viharantau yāvajīvaṃ pūgagāmaṇikattaṃ vā kāraintau pūgassa vā piyau manāpau’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu tumhaihi diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā – ‘samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā yāvadatthaṃ saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau indriyaisu aguttadvārau bhaujanai amattañ‍ñū jāgariyaṃ ananuyuttau avipassakau kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pubbarattāpararattaṃ baudhipakkhiyānaṃ baudhapakkhiyānaṃ (sī॰), baudhapakkhikānaṃ (pī॰) dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugaṃ ananuyuttau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantau’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai! mayāpi khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, naiva diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ  – ‘samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā yāvadatthaṃ saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau indriyaisu aguttadvārau bhaujanai amattañ‍ñū jāgariyaṃ ananuyuttau avipassakau kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pubbarattāpararattaṃ baudhipakkhiyānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugaṃ ananuyuttau  āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantau’’’ti|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘indriyaisu guttadvārā bhavissāma, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñunau, jāgariyaṃ anuyuttā, vipassakā kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ, pubbarattāpararattaṃ baudhipakkhiyānaṃ dhammānaṃ, bhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. macchabandhasuttaṃ

18. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ carati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ| addasā khau bhagavā addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau añ‍ñatarasmiṃ padaisai macchikaṃ macchabandhaṃ macchai vadhitvā vadhitvā vik‍kiṇamānaṃ| disvā maggā auk‍kamma añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, amuṃ macchikaṃ macchabandhaṃ macchai vadhitvā vadhitvā vik‍kiṇamāna’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu tumhaihi diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā – ‘macchikau macchabandhau macchai vadhitvā vadhitvā  vik‍kiṇamānau taina kammaina taina ājīvaina hatthiyāyī vā assayāyī vā rathayāyī vā yānayāyī vā bhaugabhaugī vā mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasantau’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai! mayāpi khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, naiva diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ – ‘macchikau macchabandhau macchai vadhitvā vadhitvā vik‍kiṇamānau taina kammaina taina ājīvaina hatthiyāyī vā assayāyī vā rathayāyī vā yānayāyī vā bhaugabhaugī  vā mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasantau’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? tai hi sau, bhikkhavai, macchai vajjhai vadhāyupanītai vadhāyānītai (syā॰ kaṃ॰), vadhāya nītai (ka॰) pāpakaina manasānupaikkhati, tasmā sau naiva hatthiyāyī hauti na assayāyī na rathayāyī na yānayāyī na bhaugabhaugī, na mahantaṃ bhaugakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasati|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu tumhaihi diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā – ‘gaughātakau gāvau vadhitvā vadhitvā vik‍kiṇamānau taina kammaina taina ājīvaina hatthiyāyī vā assayāyī vā rathayāyī vā yānayāyī vā bhaugabhaugī vā mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasantau’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai! mayāpi khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, naiva diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ – ‘gaughātakau gāvau vadhitvā vadhitvā vik‍kiṇamānau taina kammaina taina ājīvaina hatthiyāyī vā assayāyī vā rathayāyī vā yānayāyī vā bhaugabhaugī vā mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasantau’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? tai hi sau, bhikkhavai, gāvau vajjhai vadhāyupanītai pāpakaina  manasānupaikkhati, tasmā sau naiva hatthiyāyī hauti na assayāyī na rathayāyī na yānayāyī na bhaugabhaugī, na mahantaṃ bhaugakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasati’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu tumhaihi diṭṭhaṃ vā  sutaṃ vā – ‘aurabbhikau…pai॰… sūkarikau saukarikau (syā॰) …pai॰… sākuṇikau…pai॰… māgavikau magai migai (syā॰ kaṃ॰) vadhitvā vadhitvā vik‍kiṇamānau taina kammaina taina ājīvaina hatthiyāyī vā assayāyī vā rathayāyī vā yānayāyī  vā bhaugabhaugī vā mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasantau’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai! mayāpi khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, naiva diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ – ‘māgavikau magai vadhitvā vadhitvā vik‍kiṇamānau taina kammaina taina ājīvaina hatthiyāyī vā assayāyī vā rathayāyī vā yānayāyī vā bhaugabhaugī vā mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasantau’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? tai hi sau, bhikkhavai, magai vajjhai vadhāyupanītai pāpakaina manasānupaikkhati, tasmā sau naiva hatthiyāyī hauti na assayāyī na rathayāyī na yānayāyī  na bhaugabhaugī, na mahantaṃ bhaugakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasati| tai hi (nāma) ( ) bahūsu natthi sau, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatai pāṇai vajjhai vadhāyupanītai pāpakaina manasānupaikkhamānau manasānupaikkhati, tasmā sau (syā॰ ka॰) naiva hatthiyāyī bhavissati hauti (syā॰ ka॰) na assayāyī na rathayāyī na yānayāyī na bhaugabhaugī, na mahantaṃ bhaugakkhandhaṃ ajjhāvasissati ajjhāvasati (syā॰ ka॰)| kau pana vādau yaṃ manussabhūtaṃ vajjhaṃ vadhāyupanītaṃ pāpakaina manasānupaikkhati! tañhi tassa taṃ hissa (pī॰ ka॰), bhikkhavai, hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya| kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamamaraṇassatisuttaṃ

19. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā nātikai nādikai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 8.73 viharati giñ‍jakāvasathai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘maraṇassati, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā| bhāvaitha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, maraṇassati’’nti?

aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu  bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhāvaisi maraṇassati’’nti? ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ rattindivaṃ jīvaiyyaṃ, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti|

añ‍ñataraupi khau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahampi khau, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhāvaisi maraṇassati’’nti? ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ divasaṃ jīvaiyyaṃ, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti|

añ‍ñataraupi khau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahampi khau, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhāvaisi maraṇassati’’nti? ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ aikapiṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti|

añ‍ñataraupi khau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahampi khau, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhāvaisi maraṇassati’’nti? ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ cattārau pañ‍ca ālaupai saṅkhāditvā saṅkharitvā (ka॰) ajjhauharāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata  mai kataṃ assā’ti| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti|

añ‍ñataraupi khau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahampi  khau, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhāvaisi maraṇassati’’nti? ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ aikaṃ ālaupaṃ saṅkhāditvā saṃharitvā (ka॰) ajjhauharāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti|

añ‍ñataraupi khau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahampi khau, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhāvaisi maraṇassati’’nti? ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ assasitvā vā passasāmi passasitvā vā assasāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai bhagavā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘yau cāyaṃ yvāyaṃ (pī॰ ka॰), bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ rattindivaṃ jīvaiyyaṃ, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’’’ti|

‘‘yau cāyaṃ yaupāyaṃ (ka॰), bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ divasaṃ jīvaiyyaṃ, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’’’ti|

‘‘yau  cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ aikapiṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’’’ti|

‘‘yau cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ cattārau pañ‍ca ālaupai saṅkhāditvā ajjhauharāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| imai  vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū pamattā viharanti dandhaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvainti āsavānaṃ khayāya|

‘‘yau ca khvāyaṃ yaupāyaṃ (ka॰), bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ aikaṃ ālaupaṃ saṅkhāditvā ajjhauharāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’’’ti|

‘‘yau cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ assasitvā vā passasāmi passasitvā vā assasāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū appamattā viharanti tikkhaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvainti āsavānaṃ khayāya|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘appamattā viharissāma, tikkhaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaissāma āsavānaṃ khayāyā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyamaraṇassatisuttaṃ

20. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā nātikai viharati giñ‍jakāvasathai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘maraṇassati, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā| kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, maraṇassati kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā?

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu divasai nikkhantai rattiyā patihitāya paṭigatāya (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 8.74 iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘bahukā khau mai pac‍cayā maraṇassa – ahi vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya, vicchikau vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya, satapadī vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya; taina mai assa kālakiriyā, sau mamassa antarāyau| upakkhalitvā  vā papataiyyaṃ, bhattaṃ vā mai bhuttaṃ byāpaj‍jaiyya, pittaṃ vā mai kuppaiyya, saimhaṃ vā mai kuppaiyya, satthakā vā mai vātā kuppaiyyuṃ; taina mai assa kālakiriyā, sau mamassa antarāyau’ti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘atthi nu khau mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā, yai mai assu rattiṃ kālaṃ karauntassa antarāyāyā’’’ti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘atthi mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā, yai mai assu rattiṃ kālaṃ karauntassa antarāyāyā’ti, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ādittacailau vā ādittasīsau vā tassaiva cailassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimattaṃ chandañ‍ca vāyāmañ‍ca ussāhañ‍ca ussauḷhiñ‍ca appaṭivāniñ‍ca satiñ‍ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, taina bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ|

‘‘sacai  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘natthi mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā, yai mai assu rattiṃ kālaṃ karauntassa antarāyāyā’ti, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rattiyā nikkhantāya divasai patihitai iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘bahukā khau mai pac‍cayā maraṇassa – ahi vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya, vicchikau vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya, satapadī vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya; taina mai assa kālakiriyā sau mamassa antarāyau| upakkhalitvā vā papataiyyaṃ, bhattaṃ vā mai bhuttaṃ byāpaj‍jaiyya, pittaṃ vā mai kuppaiyya, saimhaṃ vā mai kuppaiyya, satthakā vā mai vātā kuppaiyyuṃ; taina mai assa kālakiriyā sau mamassa antarāyau’ti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ  – ‘atthi nu khau mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā, yai mai assu divā kālaṃ karauntassa antarāyāyā’’’ti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘atthi mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā, yai mai assu divā kālaṃ karauntassa antarāyāyā’ti, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ādittacailau vā ādittasīsau vā tassaiva cailassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimattaṃ chandañ‍ca vāyāmañ‍ca ussāhañ‍ca ussauḷhiñ‍ca appaṭivāniñ‍ca satiñ‍ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, taina bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī  ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ|

‘‘sacai pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘natthi mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā, yai mai assu divā kālaṃ karauntassa antarāyāyā’ti, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, maraṇassati aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

sāraṇīyavaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ  –

dvai sāraṇī nisāraṇīyaṃ, bhaddakaṃ anutappiyaṃ|

nakulaṃ sauppamacchā ca, dvai haunti maraṇassatīti||


3. anuttariyavaggau

1. sāmakasuttaṃ

21. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati sāmagāmakai paukkharaṇiyāyaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ paukkharaṇiyaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘tayaumai, bhantai, dhammā bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai tayau? kammārāmatā, bhassārāmatā, niddārāmatā – imai khau, bhantai, tayau dhammā bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| idamavauca sā daivatā| samanuñ‍ñau satthā ahausi| atha khau sā daivatā ‘‘samanuñ‍ñau mai satthā’’ti bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyi|

atha khau bhagavā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ paukkharaṇiyaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau, bhikkhavai, sā daivatā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘tayaumai, bhantai, dhammā bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai tayau? kammārāmatā, bhassārāmatā, niddārāmatā – imai khau, bhantai, tayau dhammā bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattantī’ti| idamavauca, bhikkhavai, sā daivatā| idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyi| taisaṃ vau khau (ka॰), bhikkhavai, alābhā taisaṃ dul‍laddhaṃ, yai vau daivatāpi jānanti kusalaihi dhammaihi parihāyamānai’’|

‘‘aparaipi, bhikkhavai, tayau parihāniyai dhammai daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca  – ‘‘katamai  ca, bhikkhavai, tayau parihāniyā dhammā? saṅgaṇikārāmatā, dauvacassatā, pāpamittatā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau parihāniyā dhammā’’|

‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ parihāyiṃsu kusalaihi dhammaihi, sabbaitai imaihaiva  chahi dhammaihi parihāyiṃsu kusalaihi dhammaihi| yaipi hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ parihāyissanti kusalaihi dhammaihi, sabbaitai imaihaiva chahi dhammaihi parihāyissanti kusalaihi  dhammaihi| yaipi hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi parihāyanti kusalaihi dhammaihi, sabbaitai imaihaiva chahi dhammaihi parihāyanti kusalaihi dhammaihī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. aparihāniyasuttaṃ

22. ‘‘chayimai, bhikkhavai, aparihāniyai dhammai daisaissāmi, taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamai ca, bhikkhavai, cha aparihāniyā dhammā? na kammārāmatā, na bhassārāmatā, na niddārāmatā, na saṅgaṇikārāmatā, sauvacassatā, kalyāṇamittatā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha aparihāniyā dhammā|

‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ na parihāyiṃsu kusalaihi dhammaihi, sabbaitai imaihaiva chahi dhammaihi na parihāyiṃsu kusalaihi dhammaihi| yaipi hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ na parihāyissanti kusalaihi dhammaihi, sabbaitai imaihaiva chahi dhammaihi na parihāyissanti kusalaihi dhammaihi| yaipi hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi na parihāyanti kusalaihi dhammaihi, sabbaitai imaihaiva chahi dhammaihi na parihāyanti kusalaihi dhammaihī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. bhayasuttaṃ

23. ‘‘‘bhaya’nti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ; ‘dukkha’nti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ; ‘raugau’ti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ; ‘gaṇḍau’ti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ; ‘saṅgau’ti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ; ‘paṅkau’ti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

‘‘kasmā  ca, bhikkhavai, ‘bhaya’nti kāmānamaitaṃ  adhivacanaṃ? kāmarāgarattāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, chandarāgavinibaddhau diṭṭhadhammikāpi bhayā na parimuc‍cati, samparāyikāpi bhayā na parimuc‍cati, tasmā ‘bhaya’nti kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| kasmā ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhanti…pai॰… raugauti… gaṇḍauti… saṅgauti… paṅkauti kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ? kāmarāgarattāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, chandarāgavinibaddhau diṭṭhadhammikāpi paṅkā na parimuc‍cati, samparāyikāpi paṅkā na parimuc‍cati, tasmā ‘paṅkau’ti kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacana’’nti|

‘‘bhayaṃ dukkhaṃ raugau gaṇḍau, saṅgau paṅkau ca ubhayaṃ|

aitai kāmā pavuc‍canti, yattha sattau puthuj‍janau||

‘‘upādānai  bhayaṃ disvā, jātimaraṇasambhavai|

anupādā vimuc‍canti, jātimaraṇasaṅkhayai||

‘‘tai khaimappattā sukhinau, diṭṭhadhammābhinibbutā|

sabbavairabhayātītā sabbai vairabhayātītā (syā॰), sabbadukkhaṃ upac‍cagu’’nti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. himavantasuttaṃ

24. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu himavantaṃ pabbatarājaṃ padālaiyya, kau pana vādau chavāya avij‍jāya! katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu samādhissa samāpattikusalau hauti, samādhissa ṭhitikusalau hauti, samādhissa vuṭṭhānakusalau hauti, samādhissa kal‍litakusalau kal‍latākusalau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.665 passitabbaṃ hauti, samādhissa gaucarakusalau hauti, samādhissa abhinīhārakusalau hauti| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu himavantaṃ pabbatarājaṃ padālaiyya, kau pana vādau chavāya avij‍jāyā’’ti! catutthaṃ|

5. anussatiṭṭhānasuttaṃ

25. ‘‘chayimāni, bhikkhavai, anussatiṭṭhānāni| katamāni cha? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau  tathāgataṃ anussarati – ‘itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ariyasāvakau tathāgataṃ anussarati, naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gaidhamhā| ‘gaidhau’ti khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| idampi khau, bhikkhavai, ārammaṇaṃ karitvā aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā visujjhanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau dhammaṃ anussarati – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau…pai॰… pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ariyasāvakau dhammaṃ anussarati, naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gaidhamhā| ‘gaidhau’ti khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| idampi khau, bhikkhavai, ārammaṇaṃ karitvā aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā visujjhanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau saṅghaṃ anussarati – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti| yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai  ariyasāvakau saṅghaṃ anussarati, naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ  cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gaidhamhā| ‘gaidhau’ti khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| idampi khau, bhikkhavai, ārammaṇaṃ karitvā aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā visujjhanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau attanau sīlāni anussarati akhaṇḍāni…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikāni| yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ariyasāvakau sīlaṃ anussarati, naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gaidhamhā| ‘gaidhau’ti khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| idampi khau, bhikkhavai, ārammaṇaṃ karitvā aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā visujjhanti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau attanau cāgaṃ anussarati – ‘lābhā vata mai! suladdhaṃ vata mai…pai॰… yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau’ti| yasmiṃ…pai॰… aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā visujjhanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau daivatā anussarati – ‘santi daivā cātumahārājikā, santi daivā tāvatiṃsā, santi daivā yāmā, santi daivā tusitā, santi daivā nimmānaratinau, santi daivā paranimmitavasavattinau, santi daivā brahmakāyikā, santi daivā tatuttari| yathārūpāya saddhāya saman‍nāgatā tā daivatā itau cutā tattha upapan‍nā; mayhampi tathārūpā saddhā saṃvij‍jati| yathārūpaina sīlaina… sutaina… cāgaina… pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatā tā daivatā itau cutā tattha upapan‍nā; mayhampi tathārūpā pañ‍ñā saṃvij‍jatī’’’ ti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ariyasāvakau attanau ca tāsañ‍ca daivatānaṃ saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarati naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gaidhamhā| ‘gaidhau’ti khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| idampi khau, bhikkhavai, ārammaṇaṃ karitvā aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā visujjhanti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cha anussatiṭṭhānānī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. mahākac‍cānasuttaṃ

26. tatra khau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahākac‍cānassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā mahākac‍cānau aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, āvusau; abbhutaṃ, āvusau! yāvañ‍cidaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sambādhai aukāsādhigamau anubuddhau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ  samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ cha anussatiṭṭhānāni|

‘‘katamāni cha? idhāvusau, ariyasāvakau tathāgataṃ anussarati – ‘itipi sau bhagavā  …pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| yasmiṃ, āvusau, samayai ariyasāvakau tathāgataṃ anussarati naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ  cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gaidhamhā| ‘gaidhau’ti khau, āvusau, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| sa khau sau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sabbasau ākāsasamaina caitasā viharati vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina| idampi khau, āvusau, ārammaṇaṃ karitvā aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā visuddhidhammā bhavanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, ariyasāvakau dhammaṃ anussarati – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau…pai॰… pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| yasmiṃ, āvusau, samayai ariyasāvakau dhammaṃ anussarati naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ  cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gaidhamhā| ‘gaidhau’ti khau, āvusau, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| sa khau sau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sabbasau ākāsasamaina caitasā viharati vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina| idampi khau, āvusau, ārammaṇaṃ karitvā aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā visuddhidhammā bhavanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, ariyasāvakau saṅghaṃ anussarati – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti| yasmiṃ, āvusau, samayai ariyasāvakau saṅghaṃ anussarati naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gaidhamhā| ‘gaidhau’ti khau, āvusau, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| sa khau sau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sabbasau ākāsasamaina caitasā viharati vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina| idampi khau, āvusau, ārammaṇaṃ  karitvā aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā visuddhidhammā bhavanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, ariyasāvakau attanau sīlāni anussarati akhaṇḍāni…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikāni| yasmiṃ, āvusau, samayai ariyasāvakau attanau sīlaṃ  anussarati naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gaidhamhā| ‘gaidhau’ti khau, āvusau, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| sa khau sau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sabbasau ākāsasamaina caitasā viharati vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina| idampi khau, āvusau, ārammaṇaṃ karitvā  aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā visuddhidhammā bhavanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, ariyasāvakau attanau cāgaṃ anussarati – ‘lābhā vata mai, suladdhaṃ vata mai…pai॰… yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau’ti| yasmiṃ, āvusau, samayai ariyasāvakau attanau cāgaṃ anussarati naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na  dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gaidhamhā| ‘gaidhau’ti khau, āvusau, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| sa khau sau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sabbasau ākāsasamaina caitasā viharati vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina| idampi khau, āvusau, ārammaṇaṃ karitvā aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā visuddhidhammā bhavanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, ariyasāvakau daivatā anussarati – ‘santi daivā cātumahārājikā, santi daivā…pai॰… tatuttari| yathārūpāya saddhāya saman‍nāgatā tā daivatā itau cutā tattha upapan‍nā; mayhampi tathārūpā saddhā saṃvij‍jati| yathārūpaina sīlaina…pai॰… sutaina… cāgaina… pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatā tā daivatā itau cutā tattha upapan‍nā; mayhampi tathārūpā pañ‍ñā saṃvij‍jatī’ti| yasmiṃ, āvusau, samayai ariyasāvakau attanau ca tāsañ‍ca daivatānaṃ saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarati naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti; ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti, nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gaidhamhā| ‘gaidhau’ti khau, āvusau, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| sa khau sau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sabbasau ākāsasamaina caitasā viharati vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina| idampi khau, āvusau, ārammaṇaṃ karitvā aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā visuddhidhammā bhavanti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, āvusau; abbhutaṃ, āvusau! yāvañ‍cidaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sambādhai aukāsādhigamau anubuddhau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ cha anussatiṭṭhānānī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamasamayasuttaṃ

27. atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau  sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, samayā manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamitu’’nti? ‘‘chayimai, bhikkhu, samayā manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ’’|

‘‘katamai cha? idha, bhikkhu, yasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati kāmarāgaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti tasmiṃ samayai manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā  viharāmi kāmarāgaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca  kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāmi| sādhu vata mai, āyasmā, kāmarāgassa pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaitū’ti| tassa manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu kāmarāgassa pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaiti| ayaṃ, bhikkhu, paṭhamau samayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhu, yasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu byāpādapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati byāpādaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca byāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti tasmiṃ samayai manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, byāpādapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharāmi byāpādaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca byāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāmi| sādhu vata mai, āyasmā, byāpādassa pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaitū’ti| tassa manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu byāpādassa pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaiti| ayaṃ, bhikkhu, dutiyau samayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhu, yasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati thinamiddhaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca thinamiddhassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti tasmiṃ samayai manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharāmi thinamiddhaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca thinamiddhassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāmi| sādhu vata mai, āyasmā, thinamiddhassa pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaitū’ti| tassa manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu thinamiddhassa pahānāya dhammaṃ  daisaiti| ayaṃ, bhikkhu, tatiyau samayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhu, yasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍capariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti tasmiṃ samayai manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍capariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharāmi uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa  nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāmi| sādhu vata mai, āyasmā, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaitū’ti| tassa manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaiti| ayaṃ, bhikkhu, catutthau samayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhu, yasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati vicikicchāparaitaina, uppan‍nāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti tasmiṃ samayai manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, āvusau, vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharāmi vicikicchāparaitaina, uppan‍nāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāmi| sādhu vata mai, āyasmā, vicikicchāya pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaitū’ti| tassa manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu vicikicchāya pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaiti| ayaṃ, bhikkhu, pañ‍camau samayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhu, yasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu yaṃ nimittaṃ āgamma yaṃ nimittaṃ manasikarautau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hauti taṃ nimittaṃ nappajānāti tasmiṃ samayai manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, yaṃ nimittaṃ āgamma  yaṃ nimittaṃ  manasikarautau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hauti, taṃ nimittaṃ nappajānāmi| sādhu vata mai, āyasmā, āsavānaṃ khayāya dhammaṃ daisaitū’ti| tassa manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayāya dhammaṃ daisaiti| ayaṃ, bhikkhu, chaṭṭhau samayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| imai khau, bhikkhu, cha samayā manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamitu’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyasamayasuttaṃ

28. aikaṃ  samayaṃ sambahulā thairā bhikkhū bārāṇasiyaṃ viharanti isipatanai migadāyai| atha khau taisaṃ thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ maṇḍalamāḷai san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘‘kau nu khau, āvusau, samayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamitu’’nti?

aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘yasmiṃ, āvusau, samayai manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau pādai pakkhālaitvā nisin‍nau hauti pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā, sau samayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamitu’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, āvusau, sau samayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| yasmiṃ, āvusau, samayai manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau pādai pakkhālaitvā nisin‍nau hauti pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā, cārittakilamathaupissa tasmiṃ samayai appaṭippassaddhau hauti, bhattakilamathaupissa tasmiṃ samayai appaṭippassaddhau hauti| tasmā sau asamayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| yasmiṃ, āvusau, samayai manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau vihārapacchāyāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā, sau samayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamitu’’nti|

aivaṃ  vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, āvusau, sau samayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| yasmiṃ, āvusau, samayai manaubhāvanīyau  bhikkhu sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau vihārapacchāyāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā, yadaivassa divā samādhinimittaṃ manasikataṃ hauti tadaivassa tasmiṃ samayai samudācarati| tasmā sau asamayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya  upasaṅkamituṃ| yasmiṃ, āvusau, samayai manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya nisin‍nau hauti pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā, sau samayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamitu’’nti|

aivaṃ  vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, āvusau, sau samayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| yasmiṃ, āvusau, samayai manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya nisin‍nau hauti pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā, aujaṭṭhāyissa tasmiṃ samayai kāyau hauti phāsussa hauti buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasi kātuṃ| tasmā sau asamayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamitu’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā mahākac‍cānau thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘chayimai, bhikkhu, samayā manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ’’’|

‘‘katamai cha? idha, bhikkhu, yasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati kāmarāgaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, tasmiṃ samayai manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharāmi kāmarāgaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāmi| sādhu vata  mai āyasmā kāmarāgassa pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaitū’ti| tassa manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu kāmarāgassa pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaiti| ayaṃ, bhikkhu, paṭhamau samayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhu, yasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu byāpādapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati…pai॰… thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā  viharati… uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍capariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati… vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati… yaṃ nimittaṃ āgamma  yaṃ nimittaṃ manasikarautau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hauti, taṃ nimittaṃ na jānāti na passati, tasmiṃ samayai manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, yaṃ nimittaṃ āgamma yaṃ nimittaṃ manasikarautau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hauti taṃ nimittaṃ na jānāmi na passāmi| sādhu vata mai āyasmā āsavānaṃ khayāya dhammaṃ daisaitū’ti| tassa manaubhāvanīyau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayāya dhammaṃ daisaiti| ayaṃ, bhikkhu, chaṭṭhau samayau manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ|

‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘imai khau, bhikkhu, cha samayā manaubhāvanīyassa bhikkhunau dassanāya upasaṅkamitu’’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. udāyīsuttaṃ

29. atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kati nu khau, udāyi, anussatiṭṭhānānī’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā udāyī tuṇhī ahausi| dutiyampi khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kati nu khau, udāyi, anussatiṭṭhānānī’’ti? dutiyampi khau āyasmā udāyī tuṇhī ahausi| tatiyampi khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kati nu khau, udāyi, anussatiṭṭhānānī’’ti? tatiyampi khau āyasmā udāyī tuṇhī ahausi|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā taṃ, āvusau udāyi, āmantaisī’’ti| ‘‘suṇaumahaṃ, āvusau ānanda, bhagavatau| idha, bhantai, bhikkhu anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati – saiyyathidaṃ aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰…| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| idaṃ, bhantai, anussatiṭṭhāna’’nti|

atha  khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘añ‍ñāsiṃ khau ahaṃ, ānanda – ‘naivāyaṃ udāyī maughapurisau adhicittaṃ anuyuttau viharatī’ti| kati nu khau, ānanda, anussatiṭṭhānānī’’ti?

‘‘pañ‍ca, bhantai, anussatiṭṭhānāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? idha, bhantai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ, bhantai, anussatiṭṭhānaṃ aivaṃ bhāvitaṃ aivaṃ bahulīkataṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhu ālaukasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karauti, divā sañ‍ñaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā; iti vivaṭaina caitasā  apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| idaṃ, bhantai, anussatiṭṭhānaṃ aivaṃ bhāvitaṃ aivaṃ bahulīkataṃ ñāṇadassanappaṭilābhāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā adhau kaisamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacau maṃsaṃ nhāru nahāru (sī॰ pī॰) dī॰ ni॰ 2.377; ma॰ ni॰ 1.110 aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ vak‍kaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā mutta’nti| idaṃ, bhantai, anussatiṭṭhānaṃ aivaṃ bhāvitaṃ aivaṃ bahulīkataṃ kāmarāgappahānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhu saiyyathāpi passaiyya sarīraṃ sīvathikāya chaḍḍitaṃ chaḍḍitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) aikāhamataṃ vā dvīhamataṃ vā  tīhamataṃ vā uddhumātakaṃ vinīlakaṃ vipubbakajātaṃ| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ aivaṃ aivanti idaṃ satipaṭṭhānasuttādīsu natthi upasaṃharati – ‘ayampi khau kāyau aivaṃdhammau aivaṃbhāvī aivaṃanatītau’’’ti aitaṃ anatītauti (sī॰)|

‘‘saiyyathāpi vā pana saiyyathā vā pana (syā॰) passaiyya sarīraṃ sīvathikāya chaḍḍitaṃ kākaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ kulalaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ gijjhaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ sunakhaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ siṅgālaihi sigālaihi (sī॰) vā khaj‍jamānaṃ  vividhaihi vā pāṇakajātaihi khaj‍jamānaṃ| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ aivaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘ayampi khau kāyau aivaṃdhammau aivaṃbhāvī aivaṃanatītau’’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi vā pana passaiyya sarīraṃ sīvathikāya chaḍḍitaṃ aṭṭhikasaṅkhalikaṃ samaṃsalauhitaṃ nhārusambandhaṃ…pai॰… aṭṭhikasaṅkhalikaṃ nimmaṃsalauhitamakkhitaṃ nhārusambandhaṃ… aṭṭhikasaṅkhalikaṃ apagatamaṃsalauhitaṃ nhārusambandhaṃ| aṭṭhikāni apagatasambandhāni disāvidisāvikkhittāni disāvidisāsu vikkhittāni (sī॰), añ‍ñaina hatthaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina pādaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina jaṅghaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina ūruṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina kaṭiṭṭhikaṃ kaṭaṭṭhikaṃ (sī॰) añ‍ñaina piṭṭhikaṇḍakaṃ añ‍ñaina sīsakaṭāhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), piṭṭhikaṇḍakaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina sīsakaṭāhaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) phāsukaṭṭhikaṃ  añ‍ñaina piṭṭhikaṇṭakaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina khandhaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina gīvaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina hanukaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina dantakaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina sīsakaṭāhaṃ piṭṭhikaṇḍakaṃ añ‍ñaina sīsakaṭāhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), piṭṭhikaṇḍakaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina sīsakaṭāhaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), aṭṭhikāni saitāni saṅkhavaṇṇappaṭibhāgāni saṅkhavaṇṇūpanibhāni (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) aṭṭhikāni puñ‍jakitāni puñ‍jakatāni (pī॰) tairauvassikāni aṭṭhikāni pūtīni cuṇṇakajātāni| sau imamaiva  kāyaṃ aivaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘ayampi  khau kāyau aivaṃdhammau aivaṃbhāvī aivaṃanatītau’ti| idaṃ, bhantai, anussatiṭṭhānaṃ aivaṃ bhāvitaṃ aivaṃ bahulīkataṃ asmimānasamugghātāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ, bhantai, anussatiṭṭhānaṃ aivaṃ bhāvitaṃ aivaṃ bahulīkataṃ anaikadhātupaṭivaidhāya saṃvattati| imāni khau, bhantai, pañ‍ca anussatiṭṭhānānī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu, sādhu, ānanda! taina hi tvaṃ, ānanda, idampi chaṭṭhaṃ anussatiṭṭhānaṃ dhāraihi| idhānanda, bhikkhu satauva abhik‍kamati satauva paṭik‍kamati satauva tiṭṭhati satauva nisīdati satauva saiyyaṃ kappaiti satauva kammaṃ adhiṭṭhāti| idaṃ, ānanda, anussatiṭṭhānaṃ aivaṃ bhāvitaṃ aivaṃ bahulīkataṃ satisampajañ‍ñāya saṃvattatī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. anuttariyasuttaṃ

30. ‘‘chayimāni, bhikkhavai, anuttariyāni| katamāni cha? dassanānuttariyaṃ, savanānuttariyaṃ, lābhānuttariyaṃ, sikkhānuttariyaṃ, pāricariyānuttariyaṃ, anussatānuttariyanti|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dassanānuttariyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau hatthiratanampi dassanāya gacchati, assaratanampi dassanāya gacchati, maṇiratanampi dassanāya gacchati, uc‍cāvacaṃ vā pana dassanāya gacchati, samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā micchādiṭṭhikaṃ micchāpaṭipan‍naṃ dassanāya gacchati| atthaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, dassanaṃ; naitaṃ natthīti vadāmi| tañ‍ca khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, dassanaṃ hīnaṃ gammaṃ pauthuj‍janikaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na  upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya  saṃvattati| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataṃ vā tathāgatasāvakaṃ vā dassanāya gacchati  niviṭṭhasaddhau niviṭṭhapaimau aikantagatau abhippasan‍nau, aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dassanānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ saukapariddavānaṃ (sī॰) samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya atthagamāya (sī॰) ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ tathāgataṃ vā tathāgatasāvakaṃ vā dassanāya gacchati niviṭṭhasaddhau niviṭṭhapaimau aikantagatau abhippasan‍nau| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dassanānuttariyaṃ| iti dassanānuttariyaṃ|

‘‘savanānuttariyañ‍ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau bhairisaddampi bhairisaddassapi (ka॰) aivaṃ vīṇāsaddampiic‍cādīsupi savanāya gacchati, vīṇāsaddampi savanāya gacchati, gītasaddampi savanāya gacchati, uc‍cāvacaṃ vā pana savanāya gacchati, samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā micchādiṭṭhikassa micchāpaṭipan‍nassa dhammassavanāya gacchati| atthaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, savanaṃ; naitaṃ natthīti vadāmi| tañ‍ca khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, savanaṃ hīnaṃ gammaṃ pauthuj‍janikaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa vā tathāgatasāvakassa vā dhammassavanāya gacchati niviṭṭhasaddhau niviṭṭhapaimau aikantagatau abhippasan‍nau, aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhikkhavai, savanānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ tathāgatassa vā tathāgatasāvakassa vā dhammassavanāya gacchati  niviṭṭhasaddhau niviṭṭhapaimau aikantagatau abhippasan‍nau| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, savanānuttariyaṃ| iti dassanānuttariyaṃ, savanānuttariyaṃ|

‘‘lābhānuttariyañ‍ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puttalābhampi labhati, dāralābhampi labhati, dhanalābhampi labhati, uc‍cāvacaṃ vā pana  lābhaṃ labhati, samaṇai vā brāhmaṇai  vā micchādiṭṭhikai micchāpaṭipan‍nai saddhaṃ paṭilabhati| atthaisau, bhikkhavai, lābhau; naisau natthīti vadāmi| sau ca khau aisau, bhikkhavai, lābhau hīnau gammau pauthuj‍janikau anariyau anatthasaṃhitau, na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatai vā tathāgatasāvakai vā  saddhaṃ paṭilabhati niviṭṭhasaddhau niviṭṭhapaimau aikantagatau abhippasan‍nau, aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhikkhavai, lābhānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ tathāgatai vā tathāgatasāvakai vā saddhaṃ paṭilabhati niviṭṭhasaddhau niviṭṭhapaimau aikantagatau abhippasan‍nau| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, lābhānuttariyaṃ| iti dassanānuttariyaṃ, savanānuttariyaṃ, lābhānuttariyaṃ|
