‘‘sikkhānuttariyañ‍ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau hatthismimpi sikkhati, assasmimpi sikkhati, rathasmimpi sikkhati, dhanusmimpi sikkhati, tharusmimpi sikkhati, uc‍cāvacaṃ vā pana sikkhati, samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā micchādiṭṭhikassa micchāpaṭipan‍nassa micchāpaṭipattiṃ (ka॰) sikkhati| atthaisā, bhikkhavai, sikkhā; naisā natthīti  vadāmi| sā ca khau aisā, bhikkhavai, sikkhā hīnā gammā pauthuj‍janikā anariyā anatthasaṃhitā, na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai adhisīlampi sikkhati, adhicittampi sikkhati, adhipañ‍ñampi sikkhati niviṭṭhasaddhau niviṭṭhapaimau aikantagatau abhippasan‍nau, aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhikkhavai, sikkhānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai adhisīlampi sikkhati, adhicittampi sikkhati, adhipañ‍ñampi sikkhati, niviṭṭhasaddhau niviṭṭhapaimau aikantagatau abhippasan‍nau| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sikkhānuttariyaṃ| iti dassanānuttariyaṃ, savanānuttariyaṃ, lābhānuttariyaṃ, sikkhānuttariyaṃ|

‘‘pāricariyānuttariyañ‍ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau khattiyampi paricarati, brāhmaṇampi paricarati, gahapatimpi paricarati, uc‍cāvacaṃ vā pana paricarati, samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā micchādiṭṭhikaṃ micchāpaṭipan‍naṃ paricarati| atthaisā, bhikkhavai, pāricariyā; naisā natthīti  vadāmi| sā ca khau aisā, bhikkhavai, pāricariyā hīnā gammā pauthuj‍janikā anariyā anatthasaṃhitā, na nibbidāya…pai॰… na nibbānāya saṃvattati| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataṃ vā tathāgatasāvakaṃ vā paricarati niviṭṭhasaddhau niviṭṭhapaimau aikantagatau abhippasan‍nau, aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhikkhavai, pāricariyānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ  atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ tathāgataṃ vā tathāgatasāvakaṃ vā paricarati niviṭṭhasaddhau niviṭṭhapaimau aikantagatau abhippasan‍nau| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pāricariyānuttariyaṃ| iti dassanānuttariyaṃ, savanānuttariyaṃ, lābhānuttariyaṃ, sikkhānuttariyaṃ, pāricariyānuttariyaṃ|

‘‘anussatānuttariyañ‍ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puttalābhampi anussarati, dāralābhampi anussarati, dhanalābhampi anussarati, uc‍cāvacaṃ vā pana lābhaṃ anussarati, samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā micchādiṭṭhikaṃ micchāpaṭipan‍naṃ anussarati| atthaisā, bhikkhavai, anussati; naisā natthīti vadāmi| sā ca khau aisā, bhikkhavai, anussati hīnā gammā pauthuj‍janikā anariyā anatthasaṃhitā, na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataṃ vā  tathāgatasāvakaṃ vā anussarati niviṭṭhasaddhau niviṭṭhapaimau aikantagatau abhippasan‍nau, aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhikkhavai, anussatīnaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ tathāgataṃ vā tathāgatasāvakaṃ vā anussarati niviṭṭhasaddhau niviṭṭhapaimau aikantagatau abhippasan‍nau| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, anussatānuttariyaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cha anuttariyānī’’ti|

‘‘yai dassanānuttaraṃ  laddhā yai dassanavaraṃ laddhā (sī॰ pī॰), dassanānuttariyaṃ laddhā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), savanañ‍ca anuttaraṃ|

lābhānuttariyaṃ laddhā, sikkhānuttariyai ratā anuttariyaṃ tathā (ka॰)||

‘‘upaṭṭhitā pāricariyā, bhāvayanti anussatiṃ|

vivaikappaṭisaṃyuttaṃ, khaimaṃ amatagāminiṃ||

‘‘appamādai  pamuditā, nipakā sīlasaṃvutā|

tai vai kālaina pac‍cainti pac‍canti (syā॰ ka॰), yattha dukkhaṃ nirujjhatī’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

anuttariyavaggau sāmakavaggau (ka॰) tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sāmakau  aparihāniyau, bhayaṃ himavānussati|

kac‍cānau dvai ca samayā, udāyī anuttariyaināti||


4. daivatāvaggau

1. saikhasuttaṃ

31. ‘‘chayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai cha? kammārāmatā, bhassārāmatā, niddārāmatā, saṅgaṇikārāmatā, indriyaisu aguttadvāratā, bhaujanai amattañ‍ñutā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘chayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai cha? na kammārāmatā, na bhassārāmatā, na niddārāmatā, na saṅgaṇikārāmatā, indriyaisu guttadvāratā, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paṭhamaaparihānasuttaṃ

32. atha khau añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘chayimai, bhantai, dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai cha? satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā, saṅghagāravatā, sikkhāgāravatā, appamādagāravatā, paṭisanthāragāravatā paṭisandhāragāravatā (ka॰) – imai khau, bhantai, cha dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| idamavauca sā daivatā| samanuñ‍ñau satthā ahausi| atha khau sā daivatā ‘‘samanuñ‍ñau mai satthā’’ti bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyi|

atha  khau bhagavā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau, bhikkhavai, sā daivatā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘chayimai, bhantai, dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai cha? satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā, saṅghagāravatā, sikkhāgāravatā, appamādagāravatā, paṭisanthāragāravatā – imai  khau, bhantai, cha dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’ti| idamavauca, bhikkhavai, sā daivatā| idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyī’’ti|

‘‘satthugaru  dhammagaru, saṅghai ca tibbagāravau|

appamādagaru bhikkhu, paṭisanthāragāravau|

abhabbau parihānāya, nibbānassaiva santikai’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. dutiyaaparihānasuttaṃ

33. ‘‘imaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau, bhikkhavai, sā daivatā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘chayimai, bhantai, dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai cha? satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā, saṅghagāravatā, sikkhāgāravatā, hirigāravatā, auttappagāravatā – imai khau, bhantai, cha dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’ti| idamavauca, bhikkhavai, sā daivatā| idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyī’’ti|

‘‘satthugaru dhammagaru, saṅghai ca tibbagāravau|

hiriauttappasampan‍nau, sappatissau sagāravau|

abhabbau parihānāya, nibbānassaiva santikai’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. mahāmauggal‍lānasuttaṃ

34. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa rahaugatassa  paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘katamaisānaṃ daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘sautāpan‍nā nāma sautāpan‍nāmhā (sī॰), sautāpan‍nāmha (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’’ti? taina khau pana samayaina tissau nāma bhikkhu adhunākālaṅkatau  añ‍ñataraṃ brahmalaukaṃ upapan‍nau hauti| tatrapi naṃ aivaṃ jānanti – ‘‘tissau brahmā mahiddhikau mahānubhāvau’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ sammiñ‍jitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaivaṃ – jaitavanai antarahitau tasmiṃ brahmalaukai pāturahausi| addasā khau tissau brahmā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna; svāgataṃ sāgataṃ (sī॰), mārisa mauggal‍lāna; cirassaṃ khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna; imaṃ pariyāyamakāsi, yadidaṃ idhāgamanāya| nisīda, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, idamāsanaṃ pañ‍ñatta’’nti| nisīdi khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau pañ‍ñattai āsanai| tissaupi khau brahmā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau tissaṃ brahmānaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca –

‘‘katamaisānaṃ khau, tissa, daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘sautāpan‍nā nāma avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’’ti? ‘‘cātumahārājikānaṃ khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘sautāpan‍nā nāma avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’’ti|

‘‘sabbaisañ‍ñaiva nu khau, tissa, cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘sautāpan‍nā nāma avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’’ti? ‘‘na khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, sabbaisaṃ cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘sautāpan‍nā nāma avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’ti| yai  khau tai, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, cātumahārājikā  daivā buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina asaman‍nāgatā dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina asaman‍nāgatā saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina asaman‍nāgatā ariyakantaihi sīlaihi  asaman‍nāgatā na taisaṃ daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ  hauti – ‘sautāpan‍nā nāma avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’ti| yai ca khau tai, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, cātumahārājikā daivā buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā, saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatā, taisaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘sautāpan‍nā nāma avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’’ti|

‘‘cātumahārājikānañ‍ñaiva nu khau, tissa, daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘sautāpan‍nā nāma avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’ti udāhu tāvatiṃsānampi daivānaṃ…pai॰… yāmānampi daivānaṃ… tusitānampi daivānaṃ… nimmānaratīnampi daivānaṃ… paranimmitavasavattīnampi daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘sautāpan‍nā nāma avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’’ti? ‘‘paranimmitavasavattīnampi khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘sautāpan‍nā nāma avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’’ti|

‘‘sabbaisañ‍ñaiva nu khau, tissa, paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘sautāpan‍nā nāma avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’’ti? ‘‘na khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, sabbaisaṃ paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘sautāpan‍nā nāma avinipātadhammā niyatā  sambaudhiparāyaṇā’ti| yai khau tai, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, paranimmitavasavattī daivā buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina asaman‍nāgatā, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina asaman‍nāgatā, saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina asaman‍nāgatā, ariyakantaihi sīlaihi asaman‍nāgatā, na taisaṃ daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘sautāpan‍nā nāma avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’ti| yai ca khau tai, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, paranimmitavasavattī daivā buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā, saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā, ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatā taisaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘sautāpan‍nā nāma avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tissassa brahmunau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā  – ‘‘saiyyathāpi nāma balavā  purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaivaṃ – ‘brahmalaukai antarahitau jaitavanai pāturahausī’’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. vij‍jābhāgiyasuttaṃ

35. ‘‘chayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā vij‍jābhāgiyā| katamai cha? anic‍casañ‍ñā, anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā, pahānasañ‍ñā, virāgasañ‍ñā, niraudhasañ‍ñā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammā vij‍jābhāgiyā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. vivādamūlasuttaṃ

36. dī॰ ni॰ 3.325; ma॰ ni॰ 3.44; cūḷava॰ 216 ‘‘chayimāni, bhikkhavai, vivādamūlāni| katamāni cha? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti upanāhī| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti upanāhī sau sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi agāravau  viharati appatissau, saṅghaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī hauti| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satthari agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammai agāravau viharati appatissau, saṅghai agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāya na paripūrakārī sau saṅghai vivādaṃ janaiti, yau hauti vivādau bahujanāhitāya bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| aivarūpaṃ cai tumhai, bhikkhavai, vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā samanupassaiyyātha| tatra tumhai, bhikkhavai, tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānāya vāyamaiyyātha| aivarūpaṃ cai tumhai, bhikkhavai, vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā na samanupassaiyyātha, tatra tumhai, bhikkhavai, tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavāya  paṭipaj‍jaiyyātha| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānaṃ hauti| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu makkhī hauti paḷāsī…pai॰… issukī hauti maccharī… saṭhau hauti māyāvī… pāpicchau hauti micchādiṭṭhi… sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī  duppaṭinissaggī| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī, sau sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, saṅghaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī hauti| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satthari agāravau  viharati appatissau, dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāya na paripūrakārī, sau saṅghai vivādaṃ janaiti, yau hauti vivādau bahujanāhitāya  bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| aivarūpaṃ cai tumhai, bhikkhavai, vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā samanupassaiyyātha| tatra tumhai, bhikkhavai, tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānāya vāyamaiyyātha| aivarūpaṃ cai tumhai, bhikkhavai, vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā na samanupassaiyyātha| tatra tumhai, bhikkhavai, tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavāya paṭipaj‍jaiyyātha| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānaṃ hauti| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavau hauti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cha vivādamūlānī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. chaḷaṅgadānasuttaṃ

37. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina vaiḷukaṇḍakī vaiḷukaṇḍakiyā (a॰ ni॰ 7.53; 2.134; saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.173) nandamātā upāsikā sāriputtamauggal‍lānappamukhai bhikkhusaṅghai chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ dakkhiṇaṃ patiṭṭhāpaiti| addasā khau bhagavā dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina vaiḷukaṇḍakiṃ nandamātaraṃ upāsikaṃ sāriputtamauggal‍lānappamukhai bhikkhusaṅghai chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ dakkhiṇaṃ patiṭṭhāpaintiṃ| disvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘aisā, bhikkhavai, vaiḷukaṇḍakī nandamātā upāsikā sāriputtamauggal‍lānappamukhai bhikkhusaṅghai chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ dakkhiṇaṃ patiṭṭhāpaiti’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgatā dakkhiṇā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, dāyakassa tīṇaṅgāni haunti, paṭiggāhakānaṃ tīṇaṅgāni| katamāni dāyakassa tīṇaṅgāni? idha, bhikkhavai, dāyakau pubbaiva dānā sumanau hauti, dadaṃ cittaṃ pasādaiti, datvā attamanau hauti| imāni dāyakassa tīṇaṅgāni|

‘‘katamāni  paṭiggāhakānaṃ tīṇaṅgāni? idha, bhikkhavai, paṭiggāhakā vītarāgā vā haunti rāgavinayāya vā paṭipan‍nā, vītadausā vā haunti dausavinayāya vā paṭipan‍nā, vītamauhā vā haunti mauhavinayāya vā paṭipan‍nā| imāni paṭiggāhakānaṃ tīṇaṅgāni| iti dāyakassa tīṇaṅgāni, paṭiggāhakānaṃ tīṇaṅgāni| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgatā dakkhiṇā hauti|

‘‘aivaṃ  chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgatāya, bhikkhavai, dakkhiṇāya na sukaraṃ puñ‍ñassa pamāṇaṃ gahaituṃ – ‘aittakau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattatī’ti| atha khau asaṅkhyaiyyau asaṅkhaiyyau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) appamaiyyau mahāpuñ‍ñakkhandhautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai na sukaraṃ udakassa pamāṇaṃ  gahaituṃ – ‘aittakāni udakāḷhakānīti vā aittakāni udakāḷhakasatānīti vā aittakāni udakāḷhakasahassānīti vā aittakāni udakāḷhakasatasahassānī’ti vā| atha khau asaṅkhyaiyyau appamaiyyau mahāudakakkhandhautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgatāya dakkhiṇāya na sukaraṃ puñ‍ñassa pamāṇaṃ gahaituṃ – ‘aittakau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattatī’ti| atha  khau asaṅkhyaiyyau appamaiyyau mahāpuñ‍ñakkhandhautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchatī’’ti|

pai॰ va॰ 305 paitavatthumhipi ‘‘pubbaiva dānā sumanau, dadaṃ cittaṃ pasādayai|

datvā attamanau hauti, aisā yañ‍ñassa puñ‍ñassa (ka॰) sampadā||

‘‘vītarāgā vītarāgau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) aivaṃ anantarapadattayaipi vītadausā, vītamauhā anāsavā|

khaittaṃ yañ‍ñassa sampan‍naṃ, sañ‍ñatā brahmacārayau brahmacārinau (syā॰ kaṃ॰)||

‘‘sayaṃ ācamayitvāna, datvā sakaihi pāṇibhi|

attanau paratau caisau, yañ‍ñau hauti mahapphalau||

a॰ ni॰ 4.40 ‘‘aivaṃ  yajitvā maidhāvī, saddhau muttaina caitasā|

abyāpaj‍jaṃ sukhaṃ laukaṃ, paṇḍitau upapaj‍jatī’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. attakārīsuttaṃ

38. atha khau añ‍ñatarau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘ahañhi, bhau gautama, aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘natthi attakārau, natthi parakārau’’’ti| ‘‘māhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aivaṃvādiṃ aivaṃdiṭṭhiṃ addasaṃ vā assausiṃ vā| kathañhi nāma  sayaṃ abhik‍kamantau, sayaṃ paṭik‍kamantau aivaṃ vakkhati – ‘natthi attakārau, natthi parakārau’’’ti!

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa, atthi ārabbhadhātū’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’| ‘‘ārabbhadhātuyā sati ārabbhavantau sattā pañ‍ñāyantī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’| ‘‘yaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, ārabbhadhātuyā sati ārabbhavantau sattā pañ‍ñāyanti, ayaṃ sattānaṃ attakārau ayaṃ parakārau’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa, atthi nik‍kamadhātu…pai॰… atthi parak‍kamadhātu… atthi thāmadhātu… atthi ṭhitidhātu… atthi upak‍kamadhātū’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’| ‘‘upak‍kamadhātuyā sati upak‍kamavantau sattā pañ‍ñāyantī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’| ‘‘yaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, upak‍kamadhātuyā sati upak‍kamavantau sattā pañ‍ñāyanti, ayaṃ sattānaṃ  attakārau ayaṃ parakārau’’|

‘‘māhaṃ, brāhmaṇa taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi brāhmaṇa māhaṃ (ka॰), aivaṃvādiṃ aivaṃdiṭṭhiṃ addasaṃ vā assausiṃ vā| kathañhi nāma sayaṃ abhik‍kamantau sayaṃ paṭik‍kamantau aivaṃ vakkhati – ‘natthi attakārau natthi parakārau’’’ti|

‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti! aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. nidānasuttaṃ

39. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāya| katamāni tīṇi? laubhau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya, dausau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya, mauhau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya| na, bhikkhavai, laubhā alaubhau samudaiti; atha khau, bhikkhavai, laubhā laubhauva samudaiti| na, bhikkhavai, dausā adausau samudaiti; atha khau, bhikkhavai, dausā dausauva samudaiti| na, bhikkhavai, mauhā amauhau samudaiti; atha khau, bhikkhavai, mauhā mauhauva samudaiti| na, bhikkhavai, laubhajaina  kammaina dausajaina kammaina mauhajaina kammaina daivā pañ‍ñāyanti, manussā pañ‍ñāyanti, yā vā panañ‍ñāpi kāci sugatiyau| atha khau, bhikkhavai, laubhajaina kammaina dausajaina kammaina mauhajaina kammaina  nirayau pañ‍ñāyati tiracchānayauni pañ‍ñāyati paittivisayau pañ‍ñāyati, yā vā panañ‍ñāpi kāci duggatiyau| imāni  khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāya|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāya| katamāni tīṇi? alaubhau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya, adausau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya, amauhau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya| na, bhikkhavai, alaubhā laubhau samudaiti; atha khau, bhikkhavai, alaubhā alaubhauva samudaiti| na, bhikkhavai, adausā dausau samudaiti; atha khau, bhikkhavai, adausā adausauva samudaiti| na, bhikkhavai, amauhā mauhau samudaiti; atha khau, bhikkhavai, amauhā amauhauva samudaiti| na, bhikkhavai, alaubhajaina kammaina adausajaina kammaina amauhajaina kammaina nirayau pañ‍ñāyati tiracchānayauni pañ‍ñāyati paittivisayau pañ‍ñāyati, yā vā panañ‍ñāpi kāci duggatiyau| atha khau, bhikkhavai, alaubhajaina kammaina adausajaina kammaina amauhajaina kammaina daivā pañ‍ñāyanti, manussā pañ‍ñāyanti, yā vā panañ‍ñāpi kāci sugatiyau| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāyā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. kimilasuttaṃ

40. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kimilāyaṃ viharati niculavanai| atha khau āyasmā kimilau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā kimilau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau  nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau na ciraṭṭhitikau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, kimila, tathāgatai parinibbutai bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau upāsakā upāsikāyau satthari agāravā viharanti appatissā, dhammai agāravā viharanti  appatissā, saṅghai agāravā viharanti appatissā, sikkhāya agāravā viharanti appatissā, appamādai agāravā viharanti appatissā, paṭisanthārai paṭisandhārai (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 5.201; 7.59 agāravā viharanti appatissā| ayaṃ khau, kimila, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau na ciraṭṭhitikau hauti’’|

‘‘kau  pana, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau ciraṭṭhitikau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, kimila, tathāgatai parinibbutai bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau upāsakā upāsikāyau satthari sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, dhammai sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, saṅghai sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, sikkhāya sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, appamādai sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, paṭisanthārai sagāravā viharanti sappatissā| ayaṃ khau, kimila, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau ciraṭṭhitikau hautī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. dārukkhandhasuttaṃ

41. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā sāriputtau rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasa añ‍ñatarasmiṃ padaisai mahantaṃ dārukkhandhaṃ| disvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘passatha nau, āvusau, tumhai amuṃ mahantaṃ dārukkhandha’’nti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti|

‘‘ākaṅkhamānau, āvusau, bhikkhu iddhimā caitauvasippattau amuṃ dārukkhandhaṃ pathavītvaiva adhimuc‍caiyya| taṃ kissa  haitu? atthi, āvusau, amumhi dārukkhandhai pathavīdhātu, yaṃ nissāya bhikkhu iddhimā caitauvasippattau amuṃ dārukkhandhaṃ pathavītvaiva adhimuc‍caiyya| ākaṅkhamānau, āvusau, bhikkhu iddhimā caitauvasippattau amuṃ dārukkhandhaṃ āpautvaiva adhimuc‍caiyya …pai॰… taijautvaiva adhimuc‍caiyya… vāyautvaiva adhimuc‍caiyya… subhantvaiva adhimuc‍caiyya… asubhantvaiva adhimuc‍caiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? atthi, āvusau, amumhi dārukkhandhai asubhadhātu, yaṃ nissāya bhikkhu iddhimā caitauvasippattau amuṃ dārukkhandhaṃ asubhantvaiva adhimuc‍caiyyā’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. nāgitasuttaṃ

42. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina icchānaṅgalaṃ nāma kausalānaṃ brāhmaṇagāmau tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā icchānaṅgalai viharati  icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍai| assausuṃ khau icchānaṅgalakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau icchānaṅgalaṃ anuppattau icchānaṅgalai viharati icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍai| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau…pai॰… buddhau bhagavā’ti| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ…pai॰… arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti| atha khau icchānaṅgalakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pahūtaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ ādāya yaina icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bahidvārakauṭṭhakai aṭṭhaṃsu uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā|

taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā nāgitau bhagavatau upaṭṭhākau hauti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ nāgitaṃ āmantaisi  – ‘‘kai pana tai, nāgita, uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā kaivaṭṭā mañ‍ñai macchavilaupai’’ti? ‘‘aitai, bhantai, icchānaṅgalakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā pahūtaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ ādāya bahidvārakauṭṭhakai ṭhitā bhagavantaṃyaiva uddissa bhikkhusaṅghañ‍cā’’ti| ‘‘māhaṃ, nāgita, yasaina samāgamaṃ, mā ca mayā yasau| yau khau, nāgita, nayimassa naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhī assa akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, yassāhaṃ naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, sau taṃ mīḷhasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukasukhaṃ sādiyaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘adhivāsaitu  dāni, bhantai, bhagavā; adhivāsaitu, sugatau; adhivāsanakālau dāni, bhantai, bhagavatau| yaina yainaiva dāni, bhantai, bhagavā gamissati, tan‍nin‍nāva bhavissanti brāhmaṇagahapatikā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, thul‍laphusitakai daivai vassantai yathānin‍naṃ udakāni pavattanti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhantai, yaina yainaiva dāni bhagavā gamissati, tan‍nin‍nāva bhavissanti brāhmaṇagahapatikā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi, bhantai, bhagavatau sīlapañ‍ñāṇa’’nti|

‘‘māhaṃ, nāgita, yasaina samāgamaṃ, mā ca mayā yasau| yau khau, nāgita, nayimassa naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa  nikāmalābhī assa akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, yassāhaṃ  naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, sau taṃ mīḷhasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukasukhaṃ sādiyaiyya|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, nāgita, bhikkhuṃ passāmi gāmantavihāriṃ samāhitaṃ  nisin‍naṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, nāgita, aivaṃ hauti – ‘idānimaṃ āyasmantaṃ ārāmikau vā ghaṭṭaissati samaṇuddaisau vā, taṃ tamhā (ka॰ sī॰ pī॰) ārāmikau vā ghaṭṭaissati samaṇuddaisau vā, sau taṃ tamhā (ka॰ sī॰) a॰ ni॰ 8.86 passitabbaṃ ārāmikau vā upaṭṭhahissati samaṇuddaisau vā taṃ tamhā ārāmikau vā ghaṭṭaissati samaṇuddaisau vā, taṃ tamhā (ka॰ sī॰ pī॰) ārāmikau vā ghaṭṭaissati samaṇuddaisau vā, sau taṃ tamhā (ka॰ sī॰) a॰ ni॰ 8.86 passitabbaṃ samādhimhā cāvaissatī’ti| taināhaṃ, nāgita, tassa bhikkhunau na attamanau haumi gāmantavihāraina| idha panāhaṃ, nāgita, bhikkhuṃ passāmi ārañ‍ñikaṃ arañ‍ñai pacalāyamānaṃ nisin‍naṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, nāgita, aivaṃ hauti – ‘idāni ayamāyasmā imaṃ niddākilamathaṃ paṭivinaudaitvā arañ‍ñasañ‍ñaṃyaiva manasi karissati aikatta’nti| taināhaṃ, nāgita, tassa bhikkhunau attamanau haumi arañ‍ñavihāraina|

‘‘idha panāhaṃ, nāgita, bhikkhuṃ passāmi ārañ‍ñikaṃ arañ‍ñai asamāhitaṃ nisin‍naṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, nāgita, aivaṃ hauti – ‘idāni ayamāyasmā asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ samādahissati, samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ anurakkhissatī’ti| taināhaṃ, nāgita, tassa bhikkhunau attamanau haumi arañ‍ñavihāraina|

‘‘idha panāhaṃ, nāgita, bhikkhuṃ passāmi ārañ‍ñikaṃ arañ‍ñai samāhitaṃ nisin‍naṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, nāgita, aivaṃ hauti – ‘idāni ayamāyasmā avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ vimaucaissati, vimuttaṃ vā  cittaṃ anurakkhissatī’ti| taināhaṃ, nāgita, tassa bhikkhunau attamanau haumi arañ‍ñavihāraina|

‘‘idha panāhaṃ, nāgita, bhikkhuṃ passāmi gāmantavihāriṃ lābhiṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| sau taṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ  nikāmayamānau riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ riñ‍cati arañ‍ñavanapatthāni  pantāni saināsanāni; gāmanigamarājadhāniṃ ausaritvā vāsaṃ kappaiti| taināhaṃ, nāgita, tassa bhikkhunau na attamanau haumi gāmantavihāraina|

‘‘idha  panāhaṃ, nāgita, bhikkhuṃ passāmi ārañ‍ñikaṃ lābhiṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| sau taṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ paṭipaṇāmaitvā na riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ na riñ‍cati arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni| taināhaṃ, nāgita, tassa bhikkhunau attamanau haumi arañ‍ñavihāraina| yasmāhaṃ, nāgita, samayai addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau na kañ‍ci na kiñ‍ci (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) passāmi puratau vā pacchatau vā, phāsu mai, nāgita, tasmiṃ samayai hauti antamasau uc‍cārapassāvakammāyā’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

daivatāvaggau saikkhaparihāniyavaggau (syā॰) catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

saikhā dvai aparihāni, mauggal‍lāna vij‍jābhāgiyā|

vivādadānattakārī nidānaṃ, kimiladārukkhandhaina nāgitauti||


5. dhammikavaggau

1. nāgasuttaṃ

43. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘āyāmānanda, yaina pubbārāmau migāramātupāsādau  tainupasaṅkamissāma divāvihārāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi|

atha khau bhagavā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ yaina pubbārāmau migāramātupāsādau tainupasaṅkami| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘āyāmānanda, yaina pubbakauṭṭhakau tainupasaṅkamissāma gattāni parisiñ‍citu’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti  khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| atha khau bhagavā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ yaina pubbakauṭṭhakau tainupasaṅkami gattāni parisiñ‍cituṃ| pubbakauṭṭhakai gattāni parisiñ‍citvā pac‍cuttaritvā aikacīvarau aṭṭhāsi gattāni pubbāpayamānau|

taina khau pana samayaina rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa saitau nāma nāgau mahātūriya mahāturiya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) tāḷitavāditaina pubbakauṭṭhakā pac‍cuttarati| apissu taṃ janau disvā aivamāha – ‘‘abhirūpau vata, bhau, rañ‍ñau nāgau; dassanīyau vata, bhau, rañ‍ñau nāgau; pāsādikau vata, bhau, rañ‍ñau nāgau; kāyupapan‍nau vata, bhau, rañ‍ñau nāgau’’ti! aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā udāyī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘hatthimaiva nu khau, bhantai, mahantaṃ brahantaṃ mahantaṃ bruhantaṃ (sī॰), mahattaṃ brahmattaṃ (ka॰) kāyupapan‍naṃ janau disvā aivamāha – ‘nāgau vata, bhau, nāgau’ti, udāhu añ‍ñampi kañ‍ci kiñ‍ci (ka॰) mahantaṃ brahantaṃ kāyupapan‍naṃ  janau disvā aivamāha – ‘nāgau vata, bhau, nāgau’’’ti? ‘‘hatthimpi khau, udāyi, mahantaṃ brahantaṃ kāyupapan‍naṃ janau disvā aivamāha – ‘nāgau vata, bhau, nāgau’ti! assampi khau, udāyi, mahantaṃ brahantaṃ…pai॰… gauṇampi khau, udāyi, mahantaṃ brahantaṃ…pai॰… uragampi nāgampi (ka॰) khau, udāyi, mahantaṃ brahantaṃ…pai॰… rukkhampi khau, udāyi, mahantaṃ brahantaṃ…pai॰… manussampi khau, udāyi, mahantaṃ brahantaṃ kāyupapan‍naṃ janau disvā aivamāha – ‘nāgau vata, bhau, nāgau’ti! api ca, udāyi, yau sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya āguṃ na karauti kāyaina vācāya manasā, tamahaṃ ‘nāgau’ti brūmī’’ti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāva subhāsitaṃ cidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā – api ca, udāyi, yau sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya āguṃ na karauti kāyaina vācāya manasā, tamahaṃ ‘nāgau’ti brūmī’’ti| idañ‍ca panāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā subhāsitaṃ imāhi gāthāhi anumaudāmi –

‘‘manussabhūtaṃ sambuddhaṃ, attadantaṃ samāhitaṃ|

iriyamānaṃ brahmapathai, cittassūpasamai rataṃ||

‘‘yaṃ  manussā namassanti, sabbadhammāna pāraguṃ|

daivāpi taṃ naṃ (sī॰ pī॰) namassanti, iti mai arahatau sutaṃ||

‘‘sabbasaṃyaujanātītaṃ, vanā nibbana nibbāna (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) māgataṃ|

kāmaihi naikkhammarataṃ naikkhammai rataṃ (ka॰ sī॰), muttaṃ sailāva kañ‍canaṃ||

‘‘sabbai ac‍carucī nāgau, himavāñ‍ñai siluc‍cayai|

sabbaisaṃ  nāganāmānaṃ, sac‍canāmau anuttarau||

‘‘nāgaṃ vau tai (ka॰) kittayissāmi, na hi āguṃ karauti sau|

saurac‍caṃ avihiṃsā ca, pādā nāgassa tai duvai||

‘‘tapau ca brahmacariyaṃ, caraṇā nāgassa tyāparai|

saddhāhatthau mahānāgau, upaikkhāsaitadantavā||

‘‘sati  gīvā sirau pañ‍ñā, vīmaṃsā dhammacintanā|

 dhammakucchisamātapau, vivaikau tassa vāladhi||

‘‘sau jhāyī assāsaratau, ajjhattaṃ susamāhitau ajjhattupasamāhitau (syā॰ ka॰)|

gacchaṃ samāhitau nāgau, ṭhitau nāgau samāhitau||

‘‘saiyyaṃ samāhitau nāgau, nisin‍naupi samāhitau|

sabbattha  saṃvutau nāgau, aisā nāgassa sampadā||

‘‘bhuñ‍jati anavaj‍jāni, sāvaj‍jāni na bhuñ‍jati|

ghāsamacchādanaṃ laddhā, san‍nidhiṃ parivaj‍jayaṃ||

‘‘saṃyaujanaṃ aṇuṃ thūlaṃ, sabbaṃ chaitvāna bandhanaṃ|

yaina yainaiva gacchati, anapaikkhauva gacchati||

‘‘yathāpi udakai jātaṃ, puṇḍarīkaṃ pavaḍḍhati|

nupalippati na upalippati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), nupalimpati (ka॰) tauyaina, sucigandhaṃ manauramaṃ||

‘‘tathaiva laukai sujātau, buddhau laukai viharati|

nupalippati laukaina, tauyaina padumaṃ yathā||

‘‘mahāginīva  jalitau mahāggini paj‍jalitau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), anāhārūpasammati|

saṅkhāraisūpasantaisu  aṅgāraisu ca santaisu (ka॰), nibbutauti pavuc‍cati||

‘‘atthassāyaṃ viñ‍ñāpanī, upamā viñ‍ñūhi daisitā|

viñ‍ñassanti viñ‍ñissanti (ka॰) mahānāgā, nāgaṃ nāgaina daisitaṃ||

‘‘vītarāgau  vītadausau, vītamauhau anāsavau|

sarīraṃ vijahaṃ nāgau, parinibbissati parinibbāti (pī॰ ka॰) anāsavau’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. migasālāsuttaṃ

44. atha khau āyasmā ānandau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina migasālāya upāsikāya nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau migasālā migasāṇā (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 10.75 upāsikā yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau migasālā upāsikā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kathaṃ kathaṃ nāmāyaṃ, bhantai ānanda, bhagavatā dhammau daisitau añ‍ñaiyyau, yatra hi nāma brahmacārī ca abrahmacārī ca ubhau samasamagatikā bhavissanti abhisamparāyaṃ? pitā mai, bhantai, purāṇau brahmacārī ahausi ārācārī viratau maithunā gāmadhammā| sau kālaṅkatau bhagavatā byākatau sakadāgāmisattau sakadāgāmipattau (ka॰ syā॰ pī॰) tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nauti| paittaiyyaupi paittayyau piyau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), pitu piyau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) mai, bhantai, isidattau abrahmacārī ahausi sadārasantuṭṭhau| saupi kālaṅkatau bhagavatā byākatau sakadāgāmipattau tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nauti| kathaṃ kathaṃ nāmāyaṃ, bhantai ānanda, bhagavatā dhammau daisitau añ‍ñaiyyau, yatra hi nāma brahmacārī ca abrahmacārī ca  ubhau samasamagatikā bhavissanti abhisamparāya’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhagini, bhagavatā byākata’’nti|

atha  khau āyasmā ānandau migasālāya upāsikāya nivaisanai piṇḍapātaṃ gahaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau āyasmā ānandau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina migasālāya upāsikāya nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdiṃ| atha khau, bhantai, migasālā upāsikā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nā khau, bhantai, migasālā upāsikā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘kathaṃ kathaṃ nāmāyaṃ, bhantai ānanda, bhagavatā dhammau daisitau añ‍ñaiyyau, yatra hi nāma brahmacārī ca abrahmacārī ca ubhau samasamagatikā bhavissanti abhisamparāyaṃ| pitā mai, bhantai, purāṇau brahmacārī ahausi ārācārī viratau maithunā gāmadhammā| sau kālaṅkatau bhagavatā byākatau sakadāgāmipattau tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nauti| paittaiyyaupi mai, bhantai, isidattau abrahmacārī ahausi sadārasantuṭṭhau| saupi kālaṅkatau bhagavatā byākatau sakadāgāmipattau  tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nauti| kathaṃ kathaṃ nāmāyaṃ, bhantai ānanda, bhagavatā dhammau daisitau añ‍ñaiyyau, yatra hi nāma brahmacārī ca abrahmacārī  ca ubhau samasamagatikā bhavissanti abhisamparāya’nti? aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ, bhantai, migasālaṃ upāsikaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘aivaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhagini, bhagavatā byākata’’’nti|

‘‘kā cānanda, migasālā upāsikā bālā abyattā ammakā ammakasañ‍ñā ambakā ambakapañ‍ñā (sī॰ pī॰), ambakā ambakasañ‍ñā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) a॰ ni॰ 10.75 passitabbaṃ, kai ca purisapuggalaparaupariyañāṇai? chayimai, ānanda, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘katamai cha? idhānanda, aikac‍cau puggalau sauratau hauti sukhasaṃvāsau, abhinandanti sabrahmacārī aikattavāsaina| tassa savanainapi akataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi akataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi appaṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ na labhati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ  maraṇā hānāya paraiti nau visaisāya, hānagāmīyaiva hauti nau visaisagāmī|

‘‘idha panānanda, aikac‍cau puggalau sauratau hauti sukhasaṃvāsau, abhinandanti sabrahmacārī aikattavāsaina| tassa savanainapi kataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi kataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi paṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ labhati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā visaisāya paraiti nau hānāya, visaisagāmīyaiva hauti nau hānagāmī|

‘‘tatrānanda, pamāṇikā pamiṇanti – ‘imassapi taiva dhammā aparassapi taiva dhammā, kasmā taisaṃ aikau hīnau aikau paṇītau’ti! tañhi taisaṃ, ānanda, hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya|

‘‘tatrānanda, yvāyaṃ puggalau sauratau hauti sukhasaṃvāsau, abhinandanti sabrahmacārī aikattavāsaina, tassa savanainapi kataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi kataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi paṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ labhati| ayaṃ, ānanda, puggalau amunā purimaina puggalaina abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| taṃ kissa haitu? imaṃ hānanda, puggalaṃ dhammasautau nibbahati, tadantaraṃ kau jānaiyya añ‍ñatra tathāgataina! tasmātihānanda, mā puggalaisu pamāṇikā ahuvattha; mā puggalaisu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhittha| khañ‍ñati hānanda, puggalaisu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhantau| ahaṃ vā, ānanda, puggalaisu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhaiyyaṃ, yau vā panassa mādisau|

‘‘idha panānanda, aikac‍cassa puggalassa kaudhamānau adhigatau avigatau (ka॰) hauti, samayaina samayañ‍cassa laubhadhammā uppaj‍janti| tassa savanainapi akataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi akataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi appaṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ na labhati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā hānāya paraiti nau visaisāya, hānagāmīyaiva hauti nau visaisagāmī|

‘‘idha panānanda, aikac‍cassa puggalassa kaudhamānau adhigatau hauti, samayaina samayañ‍cassa laubhadhammā uppaj‍janti| tassa savanainapi kataṃ hauti…pai॰… nau hānagāmī|

‘‘tatrānanda, pamāṇikā pamiṇanti…pai॰… yau vā panassa mādisau|

‘‘idha, panānanda, aikac‍cassa puggalassa kaudhamānau adhigatau hauti, samayaina samayañ‍cassa vacīsaṅkhārā uppaj‍janti| tassa savanainapi akataṃ hauti…pai॰… sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ na labhati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā hānāya paraiti nau visaisāya, hānagāmīyaiva hauti nau visaisagāmī|

‘‘idha panānanda, aikac‍cassa puggalassa kaudhamānau adhigatau hauti, samayaina samayañ‍cassa vacīsaṅkhārā uppaj‍janti| tassa savanainapi kataṃ  hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi kataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi paṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ labhati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā visaisāya  paraiti nau hānāya, visaisagāmīyaiva hauti nau hānagāmī|

‘‘tatrānanda, pamāṇikā pamiṇanti – ‘imassapi taiva dhammā, aparassapi taiva dhammā| kasmā taisaṃ aikau hīnau, aikau paṇītau’ti? tañhi taisaṃ, ānanda, hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya|

‘‘tatrānanda, yassa puggalassa kaudhamānau adhigatau hauti, samayaina samayañ‍cassa vacīsaṅkhārā uppaj‍janti, tassa savanainapi kataṃ hauti, bāhusac‍cainapi kataṃ hauti, diṭṭhiyāpi paṭividdhaṃ hauti, sāmāyikampi vimuttiṃ labhati| ayaṃ, ānanda, puggalau amunā purimaina puggalaina abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| taṃ kissa haitu? imaṃ hānanda, puggalaṃ dhammasautau nibbahati| tadantaraṃ kau jānaiyya añ‍ñatra tathāgataina! tasmātihānanda, mā puggalaisu pamāṇikā ahuvattha; mā puggalaisu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhittha| khañ‍ñati hānanda, puggalaisu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhantau| ahaṃ vā, ānanda, puggalaisu pamāṇaṃ gaṇhaiyyaṃ, yau vā panassa mādisau|

‘‘kā cānanda, migasālā upāsikā bālā abyattā ammakā ammakasañ‍ñā, kai ca purisapuggalaparaupariyañāṇai! imai khau, ānanda, cha puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘yathārūpaina, ānanda, sīlaina purāṇau saman‍nāgatau ahausi, tathārūpaina sīlaina isidattau saman‍nāgatau abhavissa| nayidha purāṇau isidattassa gatimpi añ‍ñassa| yathārūpāya ca, ānanda, pañ‍ñāya  isidattau saman‍nāgatau ahausi, tathārūpāya pañ‍ñāya purāṇau saman‍nāgatau  abhavissa| nayidha isidattau purāṇassa gatimpi añ‍ñassa| iti khau, ānanda, imai puggalā ubhau aikaṅgahīnā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. iṇasuttaṃ

45. ‘‘dāliddiyaṃ dāḷiddiyaṃ (sī॰), bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ laukasmiṃ kāmabhauginau’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, daliddau daḷiddau (sī॰) assakau  anāḷhikau anaddhikau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) iṇaṃ ādiyati, iṇādānampi, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ laukasmiṃ kāmabhauginau’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, daliddau assakau anāḷhikau iṇaṃ ādiyitvā vaḍḍhiṃ paṭissuṇāti, vaḍḍhipi, bhikkhavai, dukkhā laukasmiṃ kāmabhauginau’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, daliddau assakau anāḷhikau vaḍḍhiṃ paṭissuṇitvā  kālābhataṃ kālagataṃ (ka॰) vaḍḍhiṃ na daiti, caudaintipi naṃ; caudanāpi, bhikkhavai, dukkhā laukasmiṃ kāmabhauginau’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, daliddau assakau anāḷhikau caudiyamānau na daiti, anucarantipi naṃ; anucariyāpi, bhikkhavai, dukkhā laukasmiṃ kāmabhauginau’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, daliddau assakau anāḷhikau anucariyamānau na daiti, bandhantipi naṃ; bandhanampi, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ laukasmiṃ kāmabhauginau’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, dāliddiyampi dukkhaṃ laukasmiṃ kāmabhauginau, iṇādānampi dukkhaṃ laukasmiṃ kāmabhauginau, vaḍḍhipi dukkhā laukasmiṃ kāmabhauginau, caudanāpi dukkhā laukasmiṃ kāmabhauginau, anucariyāpi dukkhā laukasmiṃ kāmabhauginau, bandhanampi dukkhaṃ laukasmiṃ kāmabhauginau; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci saddhā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, hirī natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, auttappaṃ natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, vīriyaṃ natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, pañ‍ñā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyassa vinayai daliddau assakau anāḷhikau|

‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, daliddau assakau anāḷhikau saddhāya asati kusalaisu dhammaisu, hiriyā asati kusalaisu dhammaisu, auttappai asati kusalaisu dhammaisu, vīriyai asati kusalaisu dhammaisu, pañ‍ñāya asati  kusalaisu dhammaisu, kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| idamassa iṇādānasmiṃ vadāmi|

‘‘sau tassa kāyaduc‍caritassa paṭicchādanahaitu pāpikaṃ icchaṃ paṇidahati padahati (ka॰)| ‘mā maṃ jañ‍ñū’ti icchati, ‘mā maṃ jañ‍ñū’ti saṅkappati, ‘mā maṃ jañ‍ñū’ti vācaṃ bhāsati, ‘mā maṃ jañ‍ñū’ti kāyaina parak‍kamati| sau tassa vacīduc‍caritassa paṭicchādanahaitu…pai॰… sau tassa manauduc‍caritassa paṭicchādanahaitu…pai॰… ‘mā maṃ jañ‍ñū’ti kāyaina parak‍kamati| idamassa vaḍḍhiyā vadāmi|

‘‘tamainaṃ paisalā sabrahmacārī aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ayañ‍ca sau āyasmā aivaṃkārī aivaṃsamācārau’ti| idamassa caudanāya vadāmi|

‘‘tamainaṃ  arañ‍ñagataṃ vā rukkhamūlagataṃ vā suñ‍ñāgāragataṃ vā vippaṭisārasahagatā pāpakā akusalavitak‍kā samudācaranti| idamassa anucariyāya  vadāmi|

‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, daliddau assakau anāḷhikau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā nirayabandhanai vā bajjhati tiracchānayaunibandhanai vā| nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñaṃ aikabandhanampi samanupassāmi aivaṃdāruṇaṃ aivaṃkaṭukaṃ aivaṃdukkhaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) aivaṃantarāyakaraṃ anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya, yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayabandhanaṃ vā tiracchānayaunibandhanaṃ vā’’ti|

‘‘dāliddiyaṃ dukkhaṃ laukai, iṇādānañ‍ca vuc‍cati|

daliddau iṇamādāya, bhuñ‍jamānau vihañ‍ñati||

‘‘tatau anucaranti naṃ, bandhanampi nigacchati|

aitañhi bandhanaṃ dukkhaṃ, kāmalābhābhijappinaṃ||

‘‘tathaiva ariyavinayai, saddhā yassa na vij‍jati|

ahirīkau  anauttappī, pāpakammavinibbayau||

‘‘kāyaduc‍caritaṃ katvā, vacīduc‍caritāni ca|

manauduc‍caritaṃ katvā, ‘mā maṃ jañ‍ñū’ti icchati||

‘‘sau  saṃsappati saṅkappati (ka॰) kāyaina, vācāya uda caitasā|

pāpakammaṃ pavaḍḍhaintau, tattha tattha punappunaṃ||

‘‘sau pāpakammau dummaidhau, jānaṃ duk‍kaṭamattanau|

daliddau iṇamādāya, bhuñ‍jamānau vihañ‍ñati||

‘‘tatau  anucaranti naṃ, saṅkappā mānasā dukhā|

gāmai  vā yadi vārañ‍ñai, yassa vippaṭisārajā||

‘‘sau pāpakammau dummaidhau, jānaṃ duk‍kaṭamattanau|

yaunimañ‍ñataraṃ gantvā, nirayai vāpi bajjhati||

‘‘aitañhi bandhanaṃ dukkhaṃ, yamhā dhīrau pamuc‍cati|

dhammaladdhaihi bhaugaihi, dadaṃ cittaṃ pasādayaṃ||

‘‘ubhayattha kaṭaggāhau, saddhassa gharamaisinau|

diṭṭhadhammahitatthāya, samparāyasukhāya ca|

aivamaitaṃ gahaṭṭhānaṃ, cāgau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati||

‘‘tathaiva ariyavinayai, saddhā yassa patiṭṭhitā|

hirīmanau ca auttappī, pañ‍ñavā sīlasaṃvutau||

‘‘aisau khau ariyavinayai, ‘sukhajīvī’ti vuc‍cati|

nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ laddhā, upaikkhaṃ adhitiṭṭhati||

‘‘pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai hitvā, nic‍caṃ āraddhavīriyau|

jhānāni upasampaj‍ja, aikaudi nipakau satau||

‘‘aivaṃ ñatvā yathābhūtaṃ, sabbasaṃyaujanakkhayai|

sabbasau anupādāya, sammā cittaṃ vimuc‍cati||

‘‘tassa sammā vimuttassa, ñāṇaṃ cai hauti tādinau|

‘akuppā mai vimuttī’ti, bhavasaṃyaujanakkhayai||

‘‘aitaṃ khau paramaṃ ñāṇaṃ, aitaṃ sukhamanuttaraṃ|

asaukaṃ virajaṃ khaimaṃ, aitaṃ ānaṇyamuttama’’nti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. mahācundasuttaṃ

46. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā mahācundau caitīsu viharati sayaṃjātiyaṃ sahajātiyaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), sañ‍jātiyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| tatra khau āyasmā mahācundau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahācundassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā mahācundau aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāvusau, dhammayaugā bhikkhū jhāyī bhikkhū apasādainti – ‘imai pana jhāyinaumhā, jhāyinaumhāti jhāyanti pajjhāyanti nijjhāyanti avajjhāyanti apajjhāyanti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.508)| kimimai kiṃ himai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) jhāyanti, kintimai jhāyanti, kathaṃ imai jhāyantī’ti? tattha dhammayaugā ca bhikkhū nappasīdanti, jhāyī ca bhikkhū nappasīdanti, na ca bahujanahitāya paṭipan‍nā haunti bahujanasukhāya bahunau janassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

‘‘idha panāvusau, jhāyī bhikkhū dhammayaugai bhikkhū apasādainti – ‘imai pana dhammayaugamhā, dhammayaugamhāti uddhatā un‍naḷā capalā mukharā vikiṇṇavācā muṭṭhassatī asampajānā asamāhitā vibbhantacittā pākatindriyā| kimimai dhammayaugā, kintimai dhammayaugā, kathaṃ imai dhammayaugā’ti? tattha jhāyī ca bhikkhū nappasīdanti, dhammayaugā ca bhikkhū nappasīdanti, na ca bahujanahitāya paṭipan‍nā haunti bahujanasukhāya bahunau janassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

‘‘idha panāvusau, dhammayaugā bhikkhū dhammayaugānañ‍ñaiva bhikkhūnaṃ vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, nau jhāyīnaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| tattha dhammayaugā ca bhikkhū  nappasīdanti, jhāyī ca bhikkhū nappasīdanti, na ca bahujanahitāya paṭipan‍nā haunti bahujanasukhāya bahunau janassa atthāya hitāya  sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

‘‘idha panāvusau, jhāyī bhikkhū jhāyīnañ‍ñaiva bhikkhūnaṃ vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, nau dhammayaugānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| tattha jhāyī ca bhikkhū nappasīdanti, dhammayaugā ca bhikkhū nappasīdanti, na ca bahujanahitāya paṭipan‍nā haunti bahujanasukhāya  bahunau janassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

‘‘tasmātihāvusau, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘dhammayaugā samānā jhāyīnaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vaṇṇaṃ bhāsissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, āvusau, sikkhitabbaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? acchariyā haitai, āvusau, puggalā dul‍labhā laukasmiṃ, yai amataṃ dhātuṃ kāyaina phusitvā viharanti| tasmātihāvusau, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘jhāyī samānā dhammayaugānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vaṇṇaṃ bhāsissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, āvusau, sikkhitabbaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? acchariyā haitai, āvusau, puggalā dul‍labhā laukasmiṃ yai gambhīraṃ atthapadaṃ pañ‍ñāya ativijjha passantī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamasandiṭṭhikasuttaṃ

47. atha khau mauḷiyasīvakau mauliyasīvakau (sī॰ pī॰), mauḷisīvakau (ka॰) paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mauḷiyasīvakau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘sandiṭṭhikau dhammau, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti?

‘‘taina  hi, sīvaka, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchāmi| yathā tai khamaiyya  tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sīvaka, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ laubhaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ laubhau’ti pajānāsi, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ laubhaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ laubhau’ti pajānāsī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, sīvaka, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ laubhaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ laubhau’ti pajānāsi, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ laubhaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ laubhau’ti pajānāsi – aivampi khau, sīvaka, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sīvaka, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ dausaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ mauhaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ laubhadhammaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ dausadhammaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ mauhadhammaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ mauhadhammau’ti pajānāsi, asantaṃ  vā ajjhattaṃ mauhadhammaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ mauhadhammau’ti pajānāsī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, sīvaka, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ mauhadhammaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ mauhadhammau’ti pajānāsi, asantaṃ  vā ajjhattaṃ mauhadhammaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ mauhadhammau’ti pajānāsi – aivaṃ khau, sīvaka, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti|

‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyasandiṭṭhikasuttaṃ

48. atha khau añ‍ñatarau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘sandiṭṭhikau dhammau, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau’ti, bhau gautama, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhau gautama, sandiṭṭhikau  dhammau hauti akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti?

‘‘taina hi, brāhmaṇa, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi| yathā tai khamaiyya tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ rāgau’ti pajānāsi, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ rāgau’ti pajānāsī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’| ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ rāgau’ti pajānāsi, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ rāgau’ti pajānāsi – aivampi khau, brāhmaṇa, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ dausaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ mauhaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ kāyasandausaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ vacīsandausaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ manausandausaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ manausandausau’ti pajānāsi, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ manausandausaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ manausandausau’ti pajānāsī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’| ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ manausandausaṃ ‘atthi  mai ajjhattaṃ manausandausau’ti pajānāsi, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ manausandausaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ manausandausau’ti  pajānāsi – aivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti|

‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. khaimasuttaṃ

49. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ca khaimau āyasmā ca sumanau sāvatthiyaṃ viharanti  andhavanasmiṃ| atha khau āyasmā ca khaimau āyasmā ca sumanau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā khaimau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘yau sau, bhantai, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau tassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘atthi mai saiyyauti vā atthi mai sadisauti vā atthi mai hīnauti vā’’’ti| idamavaucāyasmā khaimau| samanuñ‍ñau satthā ahausi| atha khau āyasmā khaimau ‘‘samanuñ‍ñau mai satthā’’ti uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau āyasmā sumanau acirapak‍kantai āyasmantai khaimai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yau sau, bhantai, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau tassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘natthi mai saiyyauti vā natthi mai sadisauti vā natthi mai hīnauti vā’’’ti| idamavaucāyasmā sumanau| samanuñ‍ñau satthā  ahausi| atha  khau āyasmā sumanau ‘‘samanuñ‍ñau mai satthā’’ti uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau bhagavā acirapak‍kantaisu āyasmantai ca khaimai āyasmantai ca sumanai bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, kulaputtā añ‍ñaṃ byākaraunti| atthau ca vuttau attā ca anupanītau| atha ca pana idhaikac‍cai maughapurisā hasamānakā hasamānakaṃ (ka॰) mahāva॰ 245 mañ‍ñai añ‍ñaṃ byākaraunti| tai pacchā vighātaṃ āpaj‍jantī’’ti|

‘‘na ussaisu na aumaisu, samattai naupanīyarai naupaniyyarai (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰)|

khīṇā jāti vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, caranti saṃyaujanavippamuttā’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. indriyasaṃvarasuttaṃ

50. a॰ ni॰ 5.24, 168; 2.7.65 ‘‘indriyasaṃvarai, bhikkhavai, asati indriyasaṃvaravipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti sīlaṃ; sīlai asati sīlavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi asati sammāsamādhivipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai asati yathābhūtañāṇadassanavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai asati nibbidāvirāgavipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rukkhau sākhāpalāsavipan‍nau| tassa papaṭikāpi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, tacaupi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, phaiggupi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, sāraupi na pāripūriṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, indriyasaṃvarai asati indriyasaṃvaravipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti sīlaṃ…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ|

‘‘indriyasaṃvarai, bhikkhavai, sati indriyasaṃvarasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti sīlaṃ; sīlai sati sīlasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi sati sammāsamādhisampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai sati yathābhūtañāṇadassanasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai sati nibbidāvirāgasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rukkhau sākhāpalāsasampan‍nau| tassa papaṭikāpi pāripūriṃ gacchati, tacaupi pāripūriṃ gacchati, phaiggupi pāripūriṃ gacchati, sāraupi pāripūriṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, indriyasaṃvarai sati indriyasaṃvarasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti sīlaṃ…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassana’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ānandasuttaṃ

51. atha  khau āyasmā ānandau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhu assutañ‍caiva dhammaṃ suṇāti, sutā cassa dhammā na sammausaṃ gacchanti, yai cassa dhammā pubbai caitasā samphuṭṭhapubbā tai ca samudācaranti, aviñ‍ñātañ‍ca vijānātī’’ti? ‘‘āyasmā khau ānandau bahussutau| paṭibhātu āyasmantaṃyaiva ānanda’’nti| ‘‘tainahāvusau sāriputta, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausi| āyasmā ānandau aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāvusau sāriputta, bhikkhu dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ gāthaṃ udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vaidal‍laṃ| sau yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaiti, yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ vācaiti, yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina sajjhāyaṃ karauti, yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti manasānupaikkhati| yasmiṃ āvāsai thairā bhikkhū viharanti bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā tasmiṃ āvāsai vassaṃ upaiti| tai kālaina kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvā paripucchati paripañhati – ‘idaṃ, bhantai, kathaṃ; imassa k‍vatthau’ti? tai tassa āyasmatau avivaṭañ‍caiva vivaranti, anuttānīkatañ‍ca uttānīkaraunti, anaikavihitaisu ca kaṅkhāṭhāniyaisu dhammaisu kaṅkhaṃ paṭivinaudainti| aittāvatā khau, āvusau  sāriputta, bhikkhu assutañ‍caiva dhammaṃ suṇāti, sutā cassa dhammā na sammausaṃ gacchanti, yai cassa dhammā pubbai caitasā samphuṭṭhapubbā tai ca samudācaranti, aviñ‍ñātañ‍ca vijānātī’’ti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, āvusau, abbhutaṃ, āvusau, yāva subhāsitaṃ cidaṃ āyasmatā ānandaina| imaihi ca mayaṃ chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ dhāraima| āyasmā hi ānandau dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ gāthaṃ udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vaidal‍laṃ| āyasmā ānandau yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ  dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaiti, āyasmā ānandau yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ vācaiti, āyasmā ānandau yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina sajjhāyaṃ karauti, āyasmā ānandau yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti manasānupaikkhati| āyasmā ānandau yasmiṃ āvāsai thairā bhikkhū viharanti bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā tasmiṃ āvāsai vassaṃ upaiti| tai āyasmā ānandau kālaina kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvā paripucchati paripañhati – ‘idaṃ, bhantai, kathaṃ; imassa k‍vatthau’ti? tai āyasmatau ānandassa avivaṭañ‍caiva vivaranti, anuttānīkatañ‍ca uttānīkaraunti, anaikavihitaisu ca kaṅkhāṭhāniyaisu dhammaisu kaṅkhaṃ paṭivinaudaintī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. khattiyasuttaṃ

52. atha khau jāṇussauṇi jāṇusauṇi (ka॰) brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘khattiyā, bhau gautama, kiṃadhippāyā, kiṃupavicārā, kiṃadhiṭṭhānā, kiṃabhinivaisā, kiṃpariyausānā’’ti? ‘‘khattiyā khau, brāhmaṇa, bhaugādhippāyā pañ‍ñūpavicārā balādhiṭṭhānā pathavībhinivaisā issariyapariyausānā’’ti|

‘‘brāhmaṇā pana, bhau gautama, kiṃadhippāyā, kiṃupavicārā, kiṃadhiṭṭhānā, kiṃabhinivaisā, kiṃpariyausānā’’ti? ‘‘brāhmaṇā khau, brāhmaṇa, bhaugādhippāyā  pañ‍ñūpavicārā mantādhiṭṭhānā yañ‍ñābhinivaisā brahmalaukapariyausānā’’ti|

‘‘gahapatikā  pana, bhau gautama, kiṃadhippāyā, kiṃupavicārā, kiṃadhiṭṭhānā, kiṃabhinivaisā, kiṃpariyausānā’’ti? ‘‘gahapatikā khau, brāhmaṇa, bhaugādhippāyā pañ‍ñūpavicārā sippādhiṭṭhānā kammantābhinivaisā niṭṭhitakammantapariyausānā’’ti|

‘‘itthī  pana, bhau gautama, kiṃadhippāyā, kiṃupavicārā, kiṃadhiṭṭhānā, kiṃabhinivaisā, kiṃpariyausānā’’ti? ‘‘itthī khau, brāhmaṇa, purisādhippāyā alaṅkārūpavicārā puttādhiṭṭhānā asapatībhinivaisā issariyapariyausānā’’ti|

‘‘caurā pana, bhau gautama, kiṃadhippāyā, kiṃupavicārā, kiṃadhiṭṭhānā, kiṃabhinivaisā, kiṃpariyausānā’’ti? ‘‘caurā khau, brāhmaṇa, ādānādhippāyā gahanūpavicārā satthādhiṭṭhānā andhakārābhinivaisā adassanapariyausānā’’ti|

‘‘samaṇā pana, bhau gautama, kiṃadhippāyā, kiṃupavicārā, kiṃadhiṭṭhānā, kiṃabhinivaisā, kiṃpariyausānā’’ti? ‘‘samaṇā khau, brāhmaṇa, khantisaurac‍cādhippāyā pañ‍ñūpavicārā sīlādhiṭṭhānā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñābhinivaisā akiñ‍canābhinivaisā (syā॰ ka॰) nibbānapariyausānā’’ti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau gautama, abbhutaṃ, bhau gautama! khattiyānampi bhavaṃ gautamau jānāti adhippāyañ‍ca upavicārañ‍ca adhiṭṭhānañ‍ca abhinivaisañ‍ca pariyausānañ‍ca| brāhmaṇānampi bhavaṃ gautamau jānāti…pai॰…  gahapatīnampi bhavaṃ gautamau jānāti… itthīnampi bhavaṃ gautamau jānāti… caurānampi bhavaṃ gautamau jānāti … samaṇānampi bhavaṃ gautamau jānāti adhippāyañ‍ca upavicārañ‍ca adhiṭṭhānañ‍ca abhinivaisañ‍ca pariyausānañ‍ca| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| dasamaṃ|

11. appamādasuttaṃ

53. atha khau añ‍ñatarau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ  vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘atthi nu khau, bhau gautama, aikau dhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau yau ubhau atthai samadhiggayha tiṭṭhati – diṭṭhadhammikañ‍caiva atthaṃ, yau ca atthau samparāyikau’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, brāhmaṇa, aikau dhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau yau ubhau atthai samadhiggayha tiṭṭhati – diṭṭhadhammikañ‍caiva atthaṃ, yau ca atthau samparāyikau’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana, bhau gautama, aikau dhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau yau ubhau atthai samadhiggayha tiṭṭhati – diṭṭhadhammikañ‍caiva atthaṃ, yau ca atthau samparāyikau’’ti? ‘‘appamādau khau, brāhmaṇa, aikau dhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau ubhau atthai samadhiggayha tiṭṭhati – diṭṭhadhammikañ‍caiva atthaṃ, yau ca atthau samparāyikau’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, yāni kānici jaṅgalānaṃ jaṅgamānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 10.15; ma॰ ni॰ 1.300 pāṇānaṃ padajātāni, sabbāni tāni hatthipadai samaudhānaṃ gacchanti; hatthipadaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ mahantattaina| aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, appamādau aikau dhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau ubhau  atthai samadhiggayha tiṭṭhati – diṭṭhadhammikañ‍caiva atthaṃ, yau ca atthau samparāyikau|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, kūṭāgārassa yā kāci gaupānasiyau sabbā tā kūṭaṅgamā kūṭanin‍nā kūṭasamausaraṇā, kūṭaṃ tāsaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa …pai॰…|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, pabbajalāyakau pabbajaṃ babbajalāyakau babbajaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) lāyitvā aggai gahaitvā audhunāti nidhunāti nicchādaiti; aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa…pai॰…|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, ambapiṇḍiyā vaṇṭacchin‍nāya yāni kānici ambāni vaṇṭūpanibandhanāni sabbāni tāni tadanvayāni bhavanti; aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa…pai॰…|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, yai kaici khuddarājānau kuḍḍarājānau (sī॰ syā॰ aṭṭha॰), kuddarājānau (pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 10.15 sabbaitai rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa anuyantā anuyuttā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhavanti, rājā taisaṃ cak‍kavattī aggamakkhāyati; aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa…pai॰…|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, yā kāci tārakarūpānaṃ pabhā sabbā tā candassa pabhāya kalaṃ nāgghanti sauḷasiṃ, candappabhā tāsaṃ aggamakkhāyati| aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, appamādau aikau  dhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau ubhau atthai samadhiggayha tiṭṭhati – diṭṭhadhammikañ‍caiva atthaṃ yau ca atthau samparāyikau|

‘‘ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, aikau dhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau ubhau atthai samadhiggayha tiṭṭhati – diṭṭhadhammikañ‍caiva atthaṃ, yau ca atthau samparāyikau’’ti|

‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. dhammikasuttaṃ

54. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā dhammikau jātibhūmiyaṃ āvāsikau hauti sabbasau jātibhūmiyaṃ sattasu āvāsaisu| tatra sudaṃ āyasmā dhammikau āgantukai bhikkhū ak‍kausati paribhāsati vihiṃsati vitudati rausaiti vācāya| tai ca āgantukā bhikkhū āyasmatā dhammikaina ak‍kausiyamānā paribhāsiyamānā vihaisiyamānā vitudiyamānā rausiyamānā vācāya pak‍kamanti, na saṇṭhanti na saṇṭhahanti (sī॰), riñ‍canti āvāsaṃ|

atha khau jātibhūmakānaṃ jātibhūmikānaṃ (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) upāsakānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘mayaṃ khau bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitā cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina| atha ca pana āgantukā bhikkhū pak‍kamanti, na saṇṭhanti, riñ‍canti āvāsaṃ| kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina āgantukā bhikkhū pak‍kamanti, na saṇṭhanti, riñ‍canti āvāsa’’nti? atha khau jātibhūmakānaṃ upāsakānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā dhammikau āgantukai bhikkhū ak‍kausati paribhāsati vihiṃsati vitudati rausaiti vācāya| tai ca āgantukā bhikkhū āyasmatā dhammikaina ak‍kausiyamānā paribhāsiyamānā vihaisiyamānā vitudiyamānā rausiyamānā vācāya pak‍kamanti, na saṇṭhanti, riñ‍canti āvāsaṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ dhammikaṃ pabbājaiyyāmā’’ti|

atha  khau jātibhūmakā upāsakā yaina āyasmā dhammikau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ dhammikaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘pak‍kamatu, bhantai, āyasmā dhammikau imamhā āvāsā; alaṃ tai idha vāsainā’’ti| atha  khau āyasmā dhammikau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ agamāsi| tatrapi sudaṃ āyasmā dhammikau āgantukai bhikkhū ak‍kausati paribhāsati vihiṃsati vitudati rausaiti vācāya| tai ca āgantukā bhikkhū āyasmatā dhammikaina ak‍kausiyamānā paribhāsiyamānā vihaisiyamānā vitudiyamānā rausiyamānā vācāya pak‍kamanti, na saṇṭhanti, riñ‍canti  āvāsaṃ|

atha khau jātibhūmakānaṃ upāsakānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘mayaṃ khau bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitā cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina| atha ca pana āgantukā bhikkhū pak‍kamanti, na saṇṭhanti, riñ‍canti āvāsaṃ| kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina āgantukā bhikkhū pak‍kamanti, na saṇṭhanti, riñ‍canti āvāsa’’nti? atha khau jātibhūmakānaṃ upāsakānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā dhammikau āgantukai bhikkhū ak‍kausati paribhāsati vihiṃsati vitudati rausaiti vācāya| tai ca āgantukā bhikkhū āyasmatā dhammikaina ak‍kausiyamānā paribhāsiyamānā vihaisiyamānā vitudiyamānā rausiyamānā vācāya pak‍kamanti, na saṇṭhanti, riñ‍canti āvāsaṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ dhammikaṃ pabbājaiyyāmā’’ti|

atha khau jātibhūmakā upāsakā yaināyasmā dhammikau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ dhammikaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘pak‍kamatu, bhantai, āyasmā dhammikau imamhāpi āvāsā; alaṃ tai idha vāsainā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā dhammikau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ agamāsi| tatrapi sudaṃ āyasmā dhammikau āgantukai bhikkhū ak‍kausati paribhāsati vihiṃsati vitudati rausaiti vācāya| tai ca āgantukā bhikkhū āyasmatā dhammikaina ak‍kausiyamānā paribhāsiyamānā vihaisiyamānā vitudiyamānā rausiyamānā vācāya pak‍kamanti, na saṇṭhanti, riñ‍canti āvāsaṃ|

atha  khau jātibhūmakānaṃ upāsakānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘mayaṃ khau bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitā cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina| atha ca pana āgantukā bhikkhū pak‍kamanti, na saṇṭhanti, riñ‍canti āvāsaṃ| kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina āgantukā bhikkhū pak‍kamanti, na saṇṭhanti, riñ‍canti āvāsa’’nti? atha khau jātibhūmakānaṃ upāsakānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā dhammikau āgantukai bhikkhū ak‍kausati…pai॰…| yaṃnūna mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ dhammikaṃ pabbājaiyyāma sabbasau jātibhūmiyaṃ sattahi āvāsaihī’’ti| atha khau jātibhūmakā upāsakā yaināyasmā dhammikau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ dhammikaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘pak‍kamatu, bhantai, āyasmā dhammikau sabbasau jātibhūmiyaṃ sattahi āvāsaihī’’ti| atha khau āyasmatau dhammikassa aitadahausi – ‘‘pabbājitau khaumhi jātibhūmakaihi upāsakaihi sabbasau jātibhūmiyaṃ sattahi āvāsaihi| kahaṃ nu khau dāni gacchāmī’’ti? atha khau āyasmatau dhammikassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā dhammikau pattacīvaramādāya yaina rājagahaṃ taina pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina yaina rājagahaṃ gijjhakūṭau pabbatau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ dhammikaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘handa kutau nu tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa dhammika, āgacchasī’’ti? ‘‘pabbājitau ahaṃ, bhantai, jātibhūmakaihi upāsakaihi sabbasau jātibhūmiyaṃ sattahi āvāsaihī’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, brāhmaṇa dhammika, kiṃ tai iminā, yaṃ taṃ tatau tatau pabbājainti, sau tvaṃ tatau tatau pabbājitau mamaiva santikai āgacchasi’’|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, brāhmaṇa dhammika, sāmuddikā vāṇijā tīradassiṃ sakuṇaṃ gahaitvā nāvāya samuddaṃ ajjhaugāhanti| tai atīradakkhiṇiyā atīradassaniyā (syā॰), atīradassiyā (ka॰) nāvāya tīradassiṃ sakuṇaṃ muñ‍canti| sau gacchataiva puratthimaṃ disaṃ, gacchati pacchimaṃ disaṃ, gacchati uttaraṃ disaṃ, gacchati dakkhiṇaṃ disaṃ, gacchati uddhaṃ, gacchati anudisaṃ| sacai sau samantā tīraṃ passati, tathāgatakauva tathāgatau (ka॰) dī॰ ni॰ 1.497 passitabbaṃ hauti| sacai pana sau samantā  tīraṃ na passati tamaiva nāvaṃ pac‍cāgacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa dhammika, yaṃ taṃ tatau tatau pabbājainti sau tvaṃ tatau tatau pabbājitau mamaiva santikai āgacchasi|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, brāhmaṇa dhammika, rañ‍ñau kaurabyassa suppatiṭṭhau nāma nigraudharājā ahausi pañ‍casākhau sītacchāyau manauramau| suppatiṭṭhassa khau pana, brāhmaṇa dhammika, nigraudharājassa dvādasayaujanāni  abhinivaisau ahausi, pañ‍ca yaujanāni mūlasantānakānaṃ| suppatiṭṭhassa khau pana, brāhmaṇa dhammika, nigraudharājassa tāva mahantāni phalāni ahaisuṃ; saiyyathāpi nāma āḷhakathālikā| aivamassa sādūni phalāni ahaisuṃ; saiyyathāpi nāma khuddaṃ madhuṃ anailakaṃ| suppatiṭṭhassa khau pana, brāhmaṇa dhammika, nigraudharājassa aikaṃ khandhaṃ rājā paribhuñ‍jati saddhiṃ itthāgāraina, aikaṃ khandhaṃ balakāyau paribhuñ‍jati, aikaṃ khandhaṃ naigamajānapadā paribhuñ‍janti, aikaṃ khandhaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇā paribhuñ‍janti, aikaṃ khandhaṃ migā migapakkhinau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) paribhuñ‍janti| suppatiṭṭhassa khau pana, brāhmaṇa dhammika, nigraudharājassa na kauci phalāni rakkhati, na ca sudaṃ na ca puna (ka॰) añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa phalāni hiṃsanti|

‘‘atha khau, brāhmaṇa dhammika, añ‍ñatarau purisau suppatiṭṭhassa nigraudharājassa yāvadatthaṃ phalāni bhakkhitvā sākhaṃ bhañ‍jitvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau, brāhmaṇa dhammika, suppatiṭṭhai nigraudharājai adhivatthāya daivatāya aitadahausi – ‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! yāva pāpau manussau yāva pāpamanussau (syā॰), yāvatā pāpamanussau (ka॰), yatra hi nāma suppatiṭṭhassa nigraudharājassa yāvadatthaṃ phalāni bhakkhitvā sākhaṃ bhañ‍jitvā pak‍kamissati, yaṃnūna suppatiṭṭhau nigraudharājā āyatiṃ phalaṃ na dadaiyyā’ti| atha khau, brāhmaṇa dhammika, suppatiṭṭhau nigraudharājā āyatiṃ phalaṃ na adāsi|

‘‘atha khau, brāhmaṇa dhammika, rājā  kaurabyau yaina sak‍kau daivānamindau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ aitadavauca – ‘yagghai, mārisa, jānaiyyāsi suppatiṭṭhau nigraudharājā phalaṃ na daitī’ti? atha khau, brāhmaṇa  dhammika, sak‍kau daivānamindau tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkhāsi abhisaṅkhāraisi (syā॰ ka॰), yathā bhusā vātavuṭṭhi āgantvā suppatiṭṭhaṃ nigraudharājaṃ pavattaisi  pātaisi (sī॰ pī॰) ummūlamakāsi| atha khau, brāhmaṇa dhammika, suppatiṭṭhai nigraudharājai adhivatthā daivatā dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi|

‘‘atha khau, brāhmaṇa dhammika, sak‍kau daivānamindau yaina suppatiṭṭhai nigraudharājai adhivatthā daivatā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā suppatiṭṭhai nigraudharājai adhivatthaṃ daivataṃ aitadavauca – ‘kiṃ nu tvaṃ, daivatai, dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā aikamantaṃ ṭhitā’ti? ‘tathā hi pana mai, mārisa, bhusā vātavuṭṭhi āgantvā bhavanaṃ pavattaisi ummūlamakāsī’ti| ‘api nu tvaṃ, daivatai, rukkhadhammai ṭhitāya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi āgantvā bhavanaṃ pavattaisi ummūlamakāsī’ti? ‘kathaṃ pana, mārisa, rukkhau rukkhadhammai ṭhitau hautī’ti? ‘idha, daivatai, rukkhassa mūlaṃ mūlatthikā haranti, tacaṃ tacatthikā haranti, pattaṃ pattatthikā haranti, pupphaṃ pupphatthikā haranti, phalaṃ phalatthikā haranti| na ca taina daivatāya anattamanatā vā anabhinandi anabhiraddhi (sī॰) vā karaṇīyā| aivaṃ khau, daivatai, rukkhau rukkhadhammai ṭhitau hautī’ti| ‘aṭṭhitāyaiva khau mai, mārisa, rukkhadhammai bhusā vātavuṭṭhi āgantvā bhavanaṃ pavattaisi ummūlamakāsī’ti| ‘sacai khau tvaṃ, daivatai, rukkhadhammai tiṭṭhaiyyāsi, siyā siyāpi (sī॰ pī॰) tai bhavanaṃ yathāpurai’ti? ‘ṭhassāmahaṃ, tiṭṭhaiyyāmahaṃ (syā॰) mārisa, rukkhadhammai, hautu mai bhavanaṃ  yathāpurai’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, brāhmaṇa dhammika, sak‍kau daivānamindau tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkhāsi abhisaṅkhāri (syā॰ ka॰), yathā bhusā vātavuṭṭhi āgantvā suppatiṭṭhaṃ nigraudharājaṃ ussāpaisi, sacchavīni mūlāni ahaisuṃ| aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa dhammika, api nu taṃ samaṇadhammai ṭhitaṃ jātibhūmakā upāsakā pabbājaisuṃ sabbasau jātibhūmiyaṃ sattahi āvāsaihī’’ti? ‘‘kathaṃ pana, bhantai, samaṇau samaṇadhammai ṭhitau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, brāhmaṇa dhammika, samaṇau ak‍kausantaṃ na pac‍cak‍kausati, rausantaṃ na paṭirausati, bhaṇḍantaṃ na paṭibhaṇḍati| aivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa dhammika, samaṇau samaṇadhammai ṭhitau hautī’’ti| ‘‘aṭṭhitaṃyaiva maṃ, bhantai, samaṇadhammai jātibhūmakā upāsakā pabbājaisuṃ sabbasau jātibhūmiyaṃ sattahi āvāsaihī’’ti|

a॰ ni॰ 7.66; 7.73 ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, brāhmaṇa dhammika, sunaittau nāma satthā ahausi titthakarau kāmaisu vītarāgau| sunaittassa khau pana, brāhmaṇa dhammika, satthunau anaikāni sāvakasatāni ahaisuṃ| sunaittau satthā sāvakānaṃ brahmalaukasahabyatāya dhammaṃ daisaisi| yai khau pana, brāhmaṇa dhammika, sunaittassa satthunau brahmalaukasahabyatāya dhammaṃ daisaintassa cittāni na pasādaisuṃ tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu| yai khau pana, brāhmaṇa dhammika, sunaittassa satthunau brahmalaukasahabyatāya dhammaṃ daisaintassa cittāni pasādaisuṃ  tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, brāhmaṇa dhammika, mūgapakkhau nāma satthā ahausi…pai॰… aranaimi nāma satthā ahausi… kuddālakau nāma satthā ahausi… hatthipālau nāma satthā ahausi… jautipālau  nāma satthā ahausi titthakarau kāmaisu vītarāgau| jautipālassa khau pana, brāhmaṇa dhammika, satthunau anaikāni sāvakasatāni ahaisuṃ| jautipālau satthā sāvakānaṃ brahmalaukasahabyatāya  dhammaṃ daisaisi| yai khau pana, brāhmaṇa dhammika, jautipālassa satthunau brahmalaukasahabyatāya dhammaṃ daisaintassa cittāni na pasādaisuṃ tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu| yai khau pana, brāhmaṇa dhammika, jautipālassa satthunau brahmalaukasahabyatāya dhammaṃ daisaintassa cittāni pasādaisuṃ tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa dhammika, yau imai cha satthārai titthakarai kāmaisu vītarāgai, anaikasataparivārai sasāvakasaṅghai paduṭṭhacittau ak‍kausaiyya paribhāsaiyya, bahuṃ sau apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yau khau, brāhmaṇa dhammika, imai cha satthārai titthakarai kāmaisu vītarāgai anaikasataparivārai sasāvakasaṅghai paduṭṭhacittau ak‍kausaiyya paribhāsaiyya, bahuṃ sau apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavaiyya| yau aikaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍naṃ puggalaṃ paduṭṭhacittau ak‍kausati paribhāsati, ayaṃ tatau bahutaraṃ  apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| taṃ kissa haitu? nāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa dhammika, itau bahiddhā aivarūpiṃ khantiṃ aivarūpaṃ khantaṃ (syā॰) vadāmi, yathāmaṃ sabrahmacārīsu| tasmātiha, brāhmaṇa dhammika, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na nau samasabrahmacārīsu na nau āmasabrahmacārīsu (syā॰), na nau sabrahmacārīsu (sī॰ pī॰) cittāni paduṭṭhāni bhavissantī’’’ti| aivañhi tai, brāhmaṇa dhammika, sikkhitabbanti|

‘‘sunaittau  mūgapakkhau ca, aranaimi ca brāhmaṇau|

kuddālakau ahu satthā, hatthipālau ca māṇavau||

‘‘jautipālau ca gauvindau, ahu sattapurauhitau|

ahiṃsakā abhisaikā (syā॰) atītaṃsai, cha satthārau yasassinau||

‘‘nirāmagandhā karuṇaidhimuttā vimuttā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), kāmasaṃyaujanātigā kāmasaṃyaujanātitā (syā॰)|

kāmarāgaṃ virājaitvā, brahmalaukūpagā ahuṃ ahu (bahūsu), ahū (ka॰ sī॰)||

‘‘ahaisuṃ sāvakā taisaṃ, anaikāni satānipi|

nirāmagandhā karuṇaidhimuttā, kāmasaṃyaujanātigā|

kāmarāgaṃ virājaitvā, brahmalaukūpagā ahuṃ ahu (bahūsu), ahū (ka॰ sī॰)||

‘‘yaitai  isī bāhirakai, vītarāgai samāhitai|

paduṭṭhamanasaṅkappau, yau narau paribhāsati|

bahuñ‍ca sau pasavati, apuñ‍ñaṃ tādisau narau||

‘‘yau caikaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍naṃ, bhikkhuṃ buddhassa sāvakaṃ|

paduṭṭhamanasaṅkappau, yau narau paribhāsati|

ayaṃ tatau bahutaraṃ, apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavai narau||

‘‘na sādhurūpaṃ āsīdai, diṭṭhiṭṭhānappahāyinaṃ|

sattamau puggalau aisau, ariyasaṅghassa vuc‍cati||

‘‘avītarāgau kāmaisu, yassa pañ‍cindriyā mudū|

saddhā sati ca vīriyaṃ, samathau ca vipassanā||

‘‘tādisaṃ bhikkhumāsaj‍ja, pubbaiva upahañ‍ñati|

attānaṃ upahantvāna, pacchā añ‍ñaṃ vihiṃsati||

‘‘yau  ca rakkhati attānaṃ, rakkhitau tassa bāhirau|

tasmā rakkhaiyya attānaṃ, akkhatau paṇḍitau sadā’’ti|| dvādasamaṃ|

dhammikavaggau  pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

nāgamigasālā iṇaṃ, cundaṃ dvai sandiṭṭhikā duvai|

khaimaindriya ānanda, khattiyā appamādaina dhammikauti||

paṭhamapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



2. dutiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ


6. mahāvaggau

1. sauṇasuttaṃ

55. mahāva॰ 243 āgataṃ aivaṃ,  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā sauṇau rājagahai viharati sītavanasmiṃ| atha khau āyasmatau sauṇassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘yai khau kaici bhagavatau sāvakā āraddhavīriyā viharanti, ahaṃ taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau| atha ca pana mai na anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, saṃvij‍janti khau pana mai kulai bhaugā, sak‍kā bhaugā ca bhuñ‍jituṃ puñ‍ñāni ca kātuṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jaiyyaṃ puñ‍ñāni ca karaiyya’’nti|

atha khau bhagavā āyasmatau sauṇassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau sammiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ sammiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaivaṃ khau – gijjhakūṭai pabbatai antarahitau sītavanai āyasmatau sauṇassa sammukhai pāturahausi| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| āyasmāpi khau sauṇau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ sauṇaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘nanu  tai, sauṇa, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘yai khau kaici bhagavatau sāvakā āraddhavīriyā viharanti, ahaṃ taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau| atha  ca pana mai na anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, saṃvij‍janti khau pana mai kulai bhaugā, sak‍kā bhaugā bhaugai (mahāva॰ 243) ca bhuñ‍jituṃ puñ‍ñāni ca kātuṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jaiyyaṃ puñ‍ñāni ca karaiyya’’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sauṇa, kusalau tvaṃ pubbai agāriyabhūtau āgārikabhūtau (syā॰), agārikabhūtau (mahāva॰ 243) vīṇāya tantissarai’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sauṇa, yadā tai vīṇāya tantiyau ac‍cāyatā haunti, api nu tai vīṇā tasmiṃ samayai saravatī vā hauti kammañ‍ñā vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sauṇa, yadā tai vīṇāya tantiyau atisithilā haunti, api nu tai vīṇā tasmiṃ samayai saravatī vā hauti kammañ‍ñā vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yadā pana tai, sauṇa, vīṇāya tantiyau na ac‍cāyatā haunti nātisithilā samai guṇai patiṭṭhitā, api nu tai vīṇā tasmiṃ samayai saravatī vā hauti kammañ‍ñā vā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, sauṇa, ac‍cāraddhavīriyaṃ uddhac‍cāya saṃvattati, atisithilavīriyaṃ kausaj‍jāya saṃvattati| tasmātiha tvaṃ, sauṇa, vīriyasamathaṃ adhiṭṭhahaṃ, indriyānañ‍ca samataṃ paṭivijjha, tattha ca nimittaṃ gaṇhāhī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā sauṇau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sauṇaṃ iminā auvādaina auvaditvā – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaivaṃ khau – sītavanai antarahitau gijjhakūṭai  pabbatai pāturahausi|

atha  khau āyasmā sauṇau aparaina samayaina vīriyasamathaṃ adhiṭṭhāsi, indriyānañ‍ca samataṃ paṭivijjhi, tattha ca nimittaṃ aggahaisi| atha khau āyasmā sauṇau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anāgāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā sauṇau arahataṃ ahausi|

atha khau āyasmatau sauṇassa arahattappattassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamaiyyaṃ; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau santikai añ‍ñaṃ byākaraiyya’’nti| atha khau āyasmā sauṇau  yaina bhagavā  tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sauṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘yau sau, bhantai, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñāvimuttau, sau cha ṭhānāni adhimuttau hauti – naikkhammādhimuttau hauti, pavivaikādhimuttau hauti, abyāpaj‍jādhimuttau abyāpajjhādhimuttau (ka॰) mahāva॰ 244 passitabbaṃ hauti, taṇhākkhayādhimuttau hauti, upādānakkhayādhimuttau hauti, asammauhādhimuttau hauti|

‘‘siyā khau pana, bhantai, idhaikac‍cassa āyasmatau aivamassa – ‘kaivalaṃsaddhāmattakaṃ nūna ayamāyasmā nissāya naikkhammādhimuttau’ti| na khau panaitaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| khīṇāsavau, bhantai, bhikkhu vusitavā katakaraṇīyau karaṇīyaṃ attanau asamanupassantau katassa vā paṭicayaṃ khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā naikkhammādhimuttau hauti, khayā dausassa vītadausattā naikkhammādhimuttau hauti, khayā mauhassa vītamauhattā naikkhammādhimuttau hauti|

‘‘siyā  khau pana, bhantai, idhaikac‍cassa āyasmatau aivamassa – ‘lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ nūna ayamāyasmā nikāmayamānau pavivaikādhimuttau’ti| na khau panaitaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| khīṇāsavau, bhantai, bhikkhu vusitavā katakaraṇīyau karaṇīyaṃ attanau asamanupassantau katassa vā paṭicayaṃ khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā pavivaikādhimuttau hauti, khayā dausassa vītadausattā pavivaikādhimuttau hauti, khayā mauhassa vītamauhattā pavivaikādhimuttau hauti|

‘‘siyā khau pana, bhantai, idhaikac‍cassa āyasmatau aivamassa – ‘sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ nūna ayamāyasmā sāratau pac‍cāgacchantau abyāpaj‍jādhimuttau’ti| na khau panaitaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| khīṇāsavau, bhantai, bhikkhu vusitavā katakaraṇīyau karaṇīyaṃ attanau asamanupassantau katassa vā paṭicayaṃ khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā abyāpaj‍jādhimuttau hauti, khayā dausassa vītadausattā abyāpaj‍jādhimuttau hauti, khayā mauhassa vītamauhattā abyāpaj‍jādhimuttau hauti|

‘‘khayā  rāgassa vītarāgattā taṇhākkhayādhimuttau hauti, khayā dausassa vītadausattā taṇhākkhayādhimuttau hauti, khayā mauhassa vītamauhattā taṇhākkhayādhimuttau hauti|

‘‘khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā upādānakkhayādhimuttau hauti, khayā dausassa vītadausattā upādānakkhayādhimuttau hauti, khayā mauhassa vītamauhattā upādānakkhayādhimuttau hauti|
