‘‘khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā  asammauhādhimuttau hauti, khayā dausassa vītadausattā asammauhādhimuttau hauti, khayā mauhassa vītamauhattā asammauhādhimuttau hauti|

‘‘aivaṃ sammā vimuttacittassa, bhantai, bhikkhunau bhusā caipi cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā cakkhussa āpāthaṃ āpātaṃ (ka॰) āgacchanti, naivassa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti| amissīkatamaivassa cittaṃ hauti ṭhitaṃ ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ vayañ‍cassānupassati| bhusā caipi sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā… manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā manassa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, naivassa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti| amissīkatamaivassa cittaṃ hauti ṭhitaṃ ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ vayañ‍cassānupassati| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, sailau pabbatau acchiddau asusirau aikagghanau| atha puratthimāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi naiva naṃ saṅkampaiyya na sampakampaiyya na sampavaidhaiyya, atha pacchimāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi…pai॰… atha uttarāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi… atha dakkhiṇāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi naiva naṃ saṅkampaiyya na sampakampaiyya na sampavaidhaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhantai, aivaṃ sammāvimuttacittassa bhikkhunau bhusā caipi cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā cakkhussa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, naivassa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti| amissīkatamaivassa cittaṃ  hauti ṭhitaṃ ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ vayañ‍cassānupassati| bhusā caipi sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā… manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā manassa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, naivassa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti| amissīkatamaivassa cittaṃ hauti ṭhitaṃ ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ vayañ‍cassānupassatī’’ti|

‘‘naikkhammaṃ  adhimuttassa, pavivaikañ‍ca caitasau|

abyāpaj‍jādhimuttassa, upādānakkhayassa ca||

‘‘taṇhākkhayādhimuttassa, asammauhañ‍ca caitasau|

disvā āyatanuppādaṃ, sammā cittaṃ vimuc‍cati||

‘‘tassa sammā vimuttassa, santacittassa bhikkhunau|

katassa paṭicayau natthi, karaṇīyaṃ na vij‍jati||

‘‘sailau  yathā aikagghanau, vātaina na samīrati|

aivaṃ rūpā rasā saddā, gandhā phassā ca kaivalā||

‘‘iṭṭhā dhammā aniṭṭhā ca, nappavaidhainti tādinau|

ṭhitaṃ cittaṃ vippamuttaṃ vimutañ‍ca (ka॰) mahāva॰ 244; kathā॰ 266, vayañ‍cassānupassatī’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. phaggunasuttaṃ

56. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā phaggunau phaiggunau (ka॰), phagguṇau (sī॰ syā॰) ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyasmā, bhantai, phaggunau ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā yaināyasmā phaggunau tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā phaggunau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau āyasmā phaggunau bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna mañ‍cakai samadhausi samañ‍caupi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), saṃ + dhū + ī = samadhausi| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ phaggunaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, phagguna, mā tvaṃ mañ‍cakai samadhausi| santimāni āsanāni paraihi pañ‍ñattāni, tatthāhaṃ nisīdissāmī’’ti| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ phaggunaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kac‍ci tai, phagguna, khamanīyaṃ kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ? kac‍ci tai dukkhā vaidanā paṭik‍kamanti, nau abhik‍kamanti; paṭik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau abhik‍kamau’’ti? ‘‘na mai, bhantai, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, balavā purisau tiṇhaina sikharaina muddhani muddhānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) abhimatthaiyya abhimaṭṭhaiyya (syā॰); aivamaivaṃ khau mai, bhantai, adhimattā vātā muddhani hananti (sī॰ pī॰), auhananti (syā॰)  ūhananti| na mai, bhantai, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ  pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, balavā purisau daḷhaina varattakkhaṇḍaina sīsavaiṭhanaṃ dadaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau mai, bhantai, adhimattā sīsai sīsavaidanā| na mai, bhantai, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, dakkhau gaughātakau vā gaughātakantaivāsī vā tiṇhaina gauvikantanaina kucchiṃ parikantaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau mai, bhantai, adhimattā vātā kucchiṃ parikantanti| na mai, bhantai, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, dvai balavantau purisā dubbalataraṃ purisaṃ nānābāhāsu gahaitvā aṅgārakāsuyā santāpaiyyuṃ samparitāpaiyyuṃ; aivamaivaṃ khau mai, bhantai, adhimattau kāyasmiṃ ḍāhau| na mai, bhantai, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ phaggunaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau āyasmā phaggunau acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau kālamakāsi| tamhi cassa samayai maraṇakālai indriyāni vippasīdiṃsu| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā  tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā  ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyasmā, bhantai, phaggunau acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau  kālamakāsi| tamhi cassa samayai maraṇakālai indriyāni vippasīdiṃsū’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ hānanda, phaggunassa phaiggunassa ānanda (ka॰) bhikkhunau indriyāni na vippasīdissanti! phaggunassa, ānanda, bhikkhunau pañ‍cahi aurambhāgiyaihi  saṃyaujanaihi cittaṃ avimuttaṃ ahausi| tassa taṃ dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā pañ‍cahi aurambhāgiyaihi saṃyaujanaihi cittaṃ vimuttaṃ|

‘‘chayimai, ānanda, ānisaṃsā kālaina dhammassavanai dhammasavaṇai (sī॰) kālaina atthupaparikkhāya| katamai cha? idhānanda, bhikkhunau pañ‍cahi aurambhāgiyaihi saṃyaujanaihi cittaṃ avimuttaṃ hauti| sau tamhi samayai maraṇakālai labhati tathāgataṃ dassanāya| tassa tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| tassa taṃ dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā pañ‍cahi aurambhāgiyaihi saṃyaujanaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| ayaṃ, ānanda, paṭhamau ānisaṃsau kālaina dhammassavanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhunau pañ‍cahi aurambhāgiyaihi saṃyaujanaihi cittaṃ avimuttaṃ hauti| sau tamhi samayai maraṇakālai na haiva khau nau ca khau (ka॰) labhati tathāgataṃ dassanāya, api ca khau tathāgatasāvakaṃ labhati dassanāya| tassa tathāgatasāvakau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| tassa taṃ dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā pañ‍cahi aurambhāgiyaihi saṃyaujanaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| ayaṃ, ānanda, dutiyau ānisaṃsau kālaina dhammassavanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhunau pañ‍cahi aurambhāgiyaihi  saṃyaujanaihi cittaṃ avimuttaṃ hauti| sau tamhi samayai maraṇakālai na haiva khau labhati tathāgataṃ dassanāya, napi tathāgatasāvakaṃ labhati dassanāya; api ca khau yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kaiti  anuvicāraiti manasānupaikkhati| tassa yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ  dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kayatau anuvicārayatau manasānupaikkhatau pañ‍cahi aurambhāgiyaihi saṃyaujanaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| ayaṃ, ānanda, tatiyau ānisaṃsau kālaina atthupaparikkhāya|

‘‘idhānanda, bhikkhunau pañ‍cahi aurambhāgiyaihi saṃyaujanaihi cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hauti, anuttarai ca khau upadhisaṅkhayai cittaṃ avimuttaṃ hauti| sau tamhi samayai maraṇakālai labhati tathāgataṃ dassanāya| tassa tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ…pai॰… brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| tassa taṃ dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā anuttarai upadhisaṅkhayai cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| ayaṃ, ānanda, catutthau ānisaṃsau kālaina dhammassavanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhunau pañ‍cahi aurambhāgiyaihi saṃyaujanaihi cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hauti, anuttarai ca khau upadhisaṅkhayai cittaṃ avimuttaṃ hauti| sau tamhi samayai maraṇakālai na haiva khau labhati tathāgataṃ dassanāya, api ca khau tathāgatasāvakaṃ labhati dassanāya| tassa tathāgatasāvakau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ…pai॰… parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| tassa taṃ dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā anuttarai upadhisaṅkhayai cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| ayaṃ, ānanda, pañ‍camau ānisaṃsau kālaina dhammassavanai|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhunau pañ‍cahi aurambhāgiyaihi saṃyaujanaihi cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hauti, anuttarai  ca khau upadhisaṅkhayai cittaṃ avimuttaṃ hauti| sau tamhi samayai maraṇakālai na haiva khau labhati tathāgataṃ dassanāya, napi tathāgatasāvakaṃ labhati dassanāya; api ca khau  yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti manasānupaikkhati| tassa yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kayatau anuvicārayatau manasānupaikkhatau anuttarai upadhisaṅkhayai cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| ayaṃ, ānanda, chaṭṭhau ānisaṃsau kālaina atthupaparikkhāya| imai khau, ānanda, cha ānisaṃsā kālaina dhammassavanai kālaina atthupaparikkhāyā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. chaḷabhijātisuttaṃ

57. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pūraṇaina, bhantai, kassapaina chaḷabhijātiyau pañ‍ñattā – kaṇhābhijāti pañ‍ñattā, nīlābhijāti pañ‍ñattā, lauhitābhijāti pañ‍ñattā, haliddābhijāti pañ‍ñattā, suk‍kābhijāti pañ‍ñattā, paramasuk‍kābhijāti pañ‍ñattā|

‘‘tatridaṃ, bhantai, pūraṇaina kassapaina kaṇhābhijāti pañ‍ñattā, aurabbhikā sūkarikā sākuṇikā māgavikā luddā macchaghātakā caurā cauraghātakā bandhanāgārikā yai vā panañ‍ñaipi kaici kurūrakammantā|

‘‘tatridaṃ, bhantai, pūraṇaina kassapaina nīlābhijāti pañ‍ñattā, bhikkhū kaṇṭakavuttikā yai vā panañ‍ñaipi kaici kammavādā kriyavādā|

‘‘tatridaṃ, bhantai, pūraṇaina kassapaina lauhitābhijāti pañ‍ñattā, nigaṇṭhā aikasāṭakā|

‘‘tatridaṃ, bhantai, pūraṇaina kassapaina haliddābhijāti pañ‍ñattā, gihī audātavasanā acailakasāvakā|

‘‘tatridaṃ, bhantai, pūraṇaina kassapaina suk‍kābhijāti pañ‍ñattā, ājīvakā ājīvakiniyau|

‘‘tatridaṃ, bhantai, pūraṇaina kassapaina paramasuk‍kābhijāti pañ‍ñattā, nandau vacchau kisau saṃkic‍cau makkhali gausālau| pūraṇaina, bhantai, kassapaina imā chaḷabhijātiyau pañ‍ñattā’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ panānanda, pūraṇassa kassapassa sabbau laukau aitadabbhanujānāti imā chaḷabhijātiyau pañ‍ñāpaitu’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, ānanda, purisau daliddau assakau anāḷhikau, tassa akāmakassa bilaṃ aulaggaiyyuṃ – ‘idaṃ tai, ambhau purisa, maṃsañ‍ca khāditabbaṃ, mūlañ‍ca anuppadātabba’nti| aivamaivaṃ khau, ānanda, pūraṇaina kassapaina appaṭiñ‍ñāya aitaisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ imā chaḷabhijātiyau pañ‍ñattā, yathā taṃ bālaina abyattaina akhaittañ‍ñunā akusalaina|

‘‘ahaṃ khau panānanda, chaḷabhijātiyau pañ‍ñāpaimi| taṃ suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘katamā cānanda, chaḷabhijātiyau? idhānanda, aikac‍cau kaṇhābhijātiyau samānau  kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati| idha panānanda, aikac‍cau kaṇhābhijātiyau samānau suk‍kaṃ  dhammaṃ abhijāyati| idha panānanda, aikac‍cau kaṇhābhijātiyau samānau akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati| idha panānanda, aikac‍cau suk‍kābhijātiyau  samānau kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati| idha panānanda, aikac‍cau suk‍kābhijātiyau samānau suk‍kaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati| idha panānanda, aikac‍cau suk‍kābhijātiyau samānau akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, kaṇhābhijātiyau samānau kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati? idhānanda, aikac‍cau nīcai kulai pac‍cājātau hauti – caṇḍālakulai vā naisādakulai vā vainakulai vaiṇakulai (sabbattha) vā rathakārakulai vā puk‍kusakulai vā, daliddai appan‍napānabhaujanai kasiravuttikai, yattha kasiraina ghāsacchādau labbhati| sau ca hauti dubbaṇṇau duddasikau aukauṭimakau bavhābādhau kāṇau vā kuṇī vā khañ‍jau vā pakkhahatau vā, na lābhī an‍nassa pānassa vatthassa yānassa mālāgandhavilaipanassa saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyassa| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, kaṇhābhijātiyau samānau kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, kaṇhābhijātiyau samānau suk‍kaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati? idhānanda, aikac‍cau nīcai kulai pac‍cājātau hauti – caṇḍālakulai vā…pai॰… saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyassa| sau kāyaina sucaritaṃ carati, vācāya sucaritaṃ carati, manasā sucaritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina sucaritaṃ caritvā, vācāya sucaritaṃ caritvā, manasā sucaritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, kaṇhābhijātiyau samānau suk‍kaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, kaṇhābhijātiyau samānau akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ nibbānaṃ  abhijāyati? idhānanda, aikac‍cau nīcai kulai pac‍cājātau hauti – caṇḍālakulai  vā…pai॰… sau ca hauti dubbaṇṇau duddasikau aukauṭimakau| sau kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai, catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittau, satta baujjhaṅgai  yathābhūtaṃ bhāvaitvā akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, kaṇhābhijātiyau samānau akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, suk‍kābhijātiyau samānau kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati? idhānanda, aikac‍cau uc‍cai kulai pac‍cājātau hauti – khattiyamahāsālakulai vā brāhmaṇamahāsālakulai vā gahapatimahāsālakulai vā, aḍḍhai mahaddhanai mahābhaugai pahūtajātarūparajatai pahūtavittūpakaraṇai pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñai| sau ca hauti abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau, lābhī an‍nassa pānassa vatthassa yānassa mālāgandhavilaipanassa saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyassa| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, suk‍kābhijātiyau samānau kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, suk‍kābhijātiyau samānau suk‍kaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati? idhānanda, aikac‍cau uc‍cai kulai pac‍cājātau hauti – khattiyamahāsālakulai  vā…pai॰… saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyassa| sau kāyaina sucaritaṃ carati, vācāya sucaritaṃ carati, manasā sucaritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina sucaritaṃ caritvā, vācāya sucaritaṃ caritvā, manasā sucaritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, suk‍kābhijātiyau samānau suk‍kaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, suk‍kābhijātiyau samānau akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati? idhānanda, aikac‍cau uc‍cai kulai pac‍cājātau hauti – khattiyamahāsālakulai  vā brāhmaṇamahāsālakulai vā gahapatimahāsālakulai vā, aḍḍhai mahaddhanai mahābhaugai pahūtajātarūparajatai pahūtavittūpakaraṇai pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñai| sau ca hauti abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau, lābhī an‍nassa pānassa vatthassa yānassa mālāgandhavilaipanassa saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyassa| sau kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai, catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittau, satta baujjhaṅgai yathābhūtaṃ bhāvaitvā akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, suk‍kābhijātiyau samānau akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati| imā khau, ānanda, chaḷabhijātiyau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. āsavasuttaṃ

58. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti  pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa|

katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau yai āsavā saṃvarā pahātabbā tai saṃvaraina pahīnā haunti, yai āsavā paṭisaivanā pahātabbā tai paṭisaivanāya pahīnā haunti, yai āsavā adhivāsanā pahātabbā tai adhivāsanāya pahīnā haunti, yai āsavā parivaj‍janā pahātabbā tai parivaj‍janāya pahīnā haunti, yai āsavā vinaudanā pahātabbā tai vinaudanāya pahīnā haunti, yai āsavā bhāvanā pahātabbā tai bhāvanāya pahīnā haunti|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, āsavā saṃvarā pahātabbā yai saṃvaraina pahīnā haunti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau cakkhundriyasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati| yaṃ hissa, bhikkhavai, cakkhundriyasaṃvaraṃ asaṃvutassa viharatau uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā  vighātapariḷāhā, cakkhundriyasaṃvaraṃ saṃvutassa viharatau aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau sautindriya…pai॰… ghānindriya… jivhindriya… kāyindriya… manindriyasaṃvarasaṃvutau  viharati| yaṃ hissa, bhikkhavai, manindriyasaṃvaraṃ asaṃvutassa viharatau uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, manindriyasaṃvaraṃ saṃvutassa viharatau aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, āsavā saṃvarā pahātabbā yai saṃvaraina pahīnā haunti|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, āsavā paṭisaivanā pahātabbā yai paṭisaivanāya  pahīnā haunti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau cīvaraṃ paṭisaivati – ‘yāvadaiva sītassa paṭighātāya, uṇhassa paṭighātāya, ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ ḍaṃsa… siriṃsapasamphassānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī) ma॰ ni॰ 1.23 paṭighātāya, yāvadaiva hirikaupīnapaṭicchādanatthaṃ’| paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau piṇḍapātaṃ paṭisaivati – ‘naiva davāya na madāya na maṇḍanāya na vibhūsanāya, yāvadaiva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya, vihiṃsūparatiyā brahmacariyānuggahāya, iti purāṇañ‍ca vaidanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi, navañ‍ca vaidanaṃ na uppādaissāmi, yātrā ca mai bhavissati anavaj‍jatā ca phāsuvihārau ca’ phāsuvihārau cāti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau saināsanaṃ paṭisaivati – ‘yāvadaiva sītassa paṭighātāya, uṇhassa paṭighātāya, ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ paṭighātāya, yāvadaiva utuparissayavinaudanapaṭisal‍lānārāmatthaṃ’| paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ paṭisaivati – ‘yāvadaiva uppan‍nānaṃ vaiyyābādhikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ paṭighātāya, abyābajjhaparamatāyā’ti| yaṃ  hissa, bhikkhavai, appaṭisaivatau uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, paṭisaivatau aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, āsavā paṭisaivanā pahātabbā yai paṭisaivanāya pahīnā haunti|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, āsavā adhivāsanā pahātabbā yai adhivāsanāya pahīnā haunti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau khamau hauti sītassa uṇhassa, jighacchāya, pipāsāya, ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ, duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ, uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsakajātikau  hauti| yaṃ hissa, bhikkhavai, anadhivāsatau uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, adhivāsatau aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, āsavā adhivāsanā pahātabbā yai adhivāsanāya pahīnā haunti|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, āsavā parivaj‍janā pahātabbā yai parivaj‍janāya pahīnā haunti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau caṇḍaṃ hatthiṃ parivaj‍jaiti, caṇḍaṃ assaṃ parivaj‍jaiti, caṇḍaṃ gauṇaṃ parivaj‍jaiti, caṇḍaṃ kuk‍kuraṃ parivaj‍jaiti, ahiṃ khāṇuṃ kaṇṭakaṭṭhānaṃ saubbhaṃ papātaṃ candanikaṃ auḷigal‍laṃ, yathārūpai anāsanai nisin‍naṃ, yathārūpai agaucarai carantaṃ, yathārūpai pāpakai mittai bhajantaṃ viñ‍ñū sabrahmacārī pāpakaisu ṭhānaisu aukappaiyyuṃ, sau tañ‍ca anāsanaṃ tañ‍ca agaucaraṃ tai ca pāpakai mittai paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau parivaj‍jaiti| yaṃ hissa, bhikkhavai, aparivaj‍jayatau uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, parivaj‍jayatau aivaṃsa tai āsavā  vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| imai  vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, āsavā parivaj‍janā pahātabbā yai parivaj‍janāya pahīnā haunti|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, āsavā vinaudanā pahātabbā yai vinaudanāya pahīnā haunti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau uppan‍naṃ kāmavitak‍kaṃ nādhivāsaiti pajahati vinaudaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti, paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau uppan‍naṃ byāpādavitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ… uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai nādhivāsaiti pajahati vinaudaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| yaṃ hissa, bhikkhavai, avinaudayatau uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, vinaudayatau aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, āsavā vinaudanā pahātabbā yai vinaudanāya pahīnā haunti|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, āsavā bhāvanā pahātabbā yai bhāvanāya pahīnā haunti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ, paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| yaṃ hissa, bhikkhavai, abhāvayatau  uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, bhāvayatau aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, āsavā bhāvanā pahātabbā yai bhāvanāya pahīnā haunti|

‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dārukammikasuttaṃ

59. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā nātikai viharati giñ‍jakāvasathai| atha khau dārukammikau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau dārukammikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘api nu  tai, gahapati, kulai dānaṃ dīyatī’’ti? ‘‘dīyati mai, bhantai, kulai dānaṃ| tañ‍ca khau yai tai bhikkhū ārañ‍ñikā piṇḍapātikā paṃsukūlikā arahantau vā arahattamaggaṃ vā samāpan‍nā, tathārūpaisu  mai, bhantai, bhikkhūsu dānaṃ dīyatī’’ti|

‘‘duj‍jānaṃ khau aitaṃ, gahapati, tayā gihinā kāmabhauginā puttasambādhasayanaṃ ajjhāvasantaina, kāsikacandanaṃ pac‍canubhauntaina, mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ dhārayantaina, jātarūparajataṃ sādiyantaina imai vā arahantau imai vā arahattamaggaṃ samāpan‍nāti|

‘‘ārañ‍ñikau caipi, gahapati, bhikkhu hauti uddhatau un‍naḷau capalau mukharau vikiṇṇavācau muṭṭhassati asampajānau asamāhitau vibbhantacittau pākatindriyau| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina gārayhau| ārañ‍ñikau caipi, gahapati, bhikkhu hauti anuddhatau anun‍naḷau acapalau amukharau avikiṇṇavācau upaṭṭhitassati sampajānau samāhitau aikaggacittau saṃvutindriyau| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina pāsaṃsau|

‘‘gāmantavihārī caipi, gahapati, bhikkhu hauti uddhatau…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina gārayhau| gāmantavihārī caipi, gahapati, bhikkhu hauti anuddhatau…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina pāsaṃsau|

‘‘piṇḍapātikau  caipi, gahapati, bhikkhu hauti uddhatau…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina gārayhau| piṇḍapātikau caipi, gahapati, bhikkhu hauti anuddhatau…pai॰…  aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina pāsaṃsau|

‘‘naimantanikau caipi, gahapati, bhikkhu hauti uddhatau…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina gārayhau| naimantanikau caipi, gahapati, bhikkhu hauti anuddhatau…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina pāsaṃsau|

‘‘paṃsukūlikau caipi, gahapati, bhikkhu hauti uddhatau…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina gārayhau| paṃsukūlikau caipi, gahapati, bhikkhu hauti anuddhatau…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina pāsaṃsau|

‘‘gahapaticīvaradharau caipi, gahapati, bhikkhu  hauti uddhatau un‍naḷau capalau mukharau vikiṇṇavācau muṭṭhassati asampajānau asamāhitau vibbhantacittau pākatindriyau| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina gārayhau| gahapaticīvaradharau caipi, gahapati, bhikkhu hauti anuddhatau anun‍naḷau acapalau amukharau avikiṇṇavācau upaṭṭhitassati sampajānau samāhitau aikaggacittau saṃvutindriyau| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina pāsaṃsau|

‘‘iṅgha tvaṃ, gahapati, saṅghai dānaṃ dānāni (ka॰) daihi| saṅghai tai dānaṃ dadatau cittaṃ pasīdissati| sau tvaṃ pasan‍nacittau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissasī’’ti| ‘‘aisāhaṃ, bhantai, aj‍jataggai saṅghai dānaṃ dassāmī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. hatthisāriputtasuttaṃ

60. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ viharati isipatanai migadāyai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā thairā bhikkhū pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantā maṇḍalamāḷai san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā abhidhammakathaṃ kathainti| tatra sudaṃ āyasmā cittau hatthisāriputtau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhidhammakathaṃ kathaintānaṃ antarantarā kathaṃ aupātaiti| atha khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmantaṃ cittaṃ hatthisāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘māyasmā cittau hatthisāriputtau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhidhammakathaṃ  kathaintānaṃ antarantarā kathaṃ  aupātaisi, yāva kathāpariyausānaṃ āyasmā cittau āgamaitū’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai āyasmatau cittassa hatthisāriputtassa sahāyakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ mahākauṭṭhikaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘māyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmantaṃ  cittaṃ hatthisāriputtaṃ apasādaisi, paṇḍitau āyasmā cittau hatthisāriputtau| pahauti cāyasmā cittau hatthisāriputtau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhidhammakathaṃ kathaitu’’nti|

‘‘duj‍jānaṃ khau aitaṃ, āvusau, parassa caitaupariyāyaṃ ajānantaihi| idhāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau tāvadaiva sauratasauratau hauti, nivātanivātau hauti, upasantupasantau hauti, yāva satthāraṃ  upanissāya viharati añ‍ñataraṃ vā garuṭṭhāniyaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ| yatau ca khau sau vapakassataiva satthārā, vapakassati garuṭṭhāniyaihi sabrahmacārīhi, sau saṃsaṭṭhau viharati bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi upāsakaihi upāsikāhi rañ‍ñā rājamahāmattaihi titthiyaihi titthiyasāvakaihi| tassa saṃsaṭṭhassa vissatthassa pākatassa bhassamanuyuttassa viharatau rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti| sau rāgānuddhaṃsitaina cittaina sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, gauṇau kiṭṭhādau dāmaina vā baddhau ārāmai vā bandhau (ka॰) vajai vā auruddhau| yau nu khau, āvusau, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na dānāyaṃ gauṇau kiṭṭhādau punadaiva kiṭṭhaṃ autarissatī’ti, sammā nu khau sau, āvusau, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘ṭhānañhaitaṃ, āvusau, vij‍jati, yaṃ sau gauṇau kiṭṭhādau dāmaṃ vā chaitvā vajaṃ vā bhinditvā, atha punadaiva kiṭṭhaṃ autaraiyyāti| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, idhaikac‍cau puggalau  tāvadaiva sauratasauratau hauti, nivātanivātau hauti, upasantupasantau hauti yāva satthāraṃ upanissāya viharati añ‍ñataraṃ vā garuṭṭhāniyaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ| yatau ca khau sau vapakassataiva satthārā, vapakassati  garuṭṭhāniyaihi sabrahmacārīhi, sau saṃsaṭṭhau viharati bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi upāsakaihi upāsikāhi rañ‍ñā rājamahāmattaihi titthiyaihi titthiyasāvakaihi| tassa saṃsaṭṭhassa vissatthassa pākatassa bhassamanuyuttassa viharatau rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti| sau rāgānuddhaṃsitaina cittaina sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati|

‘‘idha panāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau ‘lābhimhi paṭhamassa jhānassā’ti saṃsaṭṭhau viharati bhikkhūhi…pai॰… sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, cātumahāpathai thul‍laphusitakau daivau vassantau thul‍laphusitakai daivai vassantai (ka॰) rajaṃ antaradhāpaiyya, cikkhal‍laṃ pātukaraiyya| yau nu khau, āvusau, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na dāni amusmiṃ amukasmiṃ (ka॰) cātumahāpathai punadaiva rajau pātubhavissatī’ti, sammā nu khau sau, āvusau, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘ṭhānañhaitaṃ, āvusau, vij‍jati, yaṃ amusmiṃ cātumahāpathai manussā vā atik‍kamaiyyuṃ, gaupasū vā atik‍kamaiyyuṃ, vātātapau vā snaihagataṃ pariyādiyaiyya, atha punadaiva rajau pātubhavaiyyāti| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, idhaikac‍cau puggalau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati| sau ‘lābhimhi paṭhamassa jhānassā’ti saṃsaṭṭhau viharati bhikkhūhi…pai॰… sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati|

‘‘idha  panāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau ‘lābhimhi dutiyassa jhānassā’ti saṃsaṭṭhau viharati bhikkhūhi…pai॰… sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, gāmassa vā nigamassa vā avidūrai mahantaṃ taḷākaṃ| tattha thul‍laphusitakau daivau vuṭṭhau sippisambukampi sakkharakaṭhalampi antaradhāpaiyya| yau nu khau, āvusau, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na dāni amusmiṃ taḷākai punadaiva sippisambukā vā sakkharakaṭhalā vā pātubhavissantī’ti, sammā nu khau sau, āvusau, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘ṭhānañhaitaṃ, āvusau, vij‍jati, yaṃ amusmiṃ taḷākai manussā vā pivaiyyuṃ, gaupasū vā pivaiyyuṃ, vātātapau vā snaihagataṃ pariyādiyaiyya, atha punadaiva sippisambukāpi sakkharakaṭhalāpi pātubhavaiyyunti| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, idhaikac‍cau puggalau vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau ‘lābhimhi dutiyassa jhānassā’ti saṃsaṭṭhau viharati bhikkhūhi…pai॰… sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati|

‘‘idha  panāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau ‘lābhimhi tatiyassa jhānassā’ti saṃsaṭṭhau viharati bhikkhūhi…pai॰… sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, purisaṃ paṇītabhaujanaṃ bhuttāviṃ ābhidausikaṃ  bhaujanaṃ nacchādaiyya| yau nu khau, āvusau, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na dāni amuṃ purisaṃ punadaiva bhaujanaṃ chādaissatī’ti, sammā nu khau sau, āvusau, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘ṭhānañhaitaṃ, āvusau, vij‍jati, amuṃ purisaṃ paṇītabhaujanaṃ bhuttāviṃ yāvassa sā aujā kāyai ṭhassati tāva na añ‍ñaṃ bhaujanaṃ chādaissati| yatau ca khvassa  sā aujā antaradhāyissati, atha punadaiva taṃ bhaujanaṃ chādaiyyāti| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, idhaikac‍cau puggalau pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau ‘lābhimhi tatiyassa jhānassā’ti saṃsaṭṭhau viharati bhikkhūhi…pai॰… sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati|

‘‘idha, panāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau ‘lābhimhi catutthassa jhānassā’ti saṃsaṭṭhau viharati bhikkhūhi…pai॰… sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, pabbatasaṅkhaipai udakarahadau nivātau vigataūmikau| yau nu khau, āvusau, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na dāni amusmiṃ udakarahadai punadaiva ūmi pātubhavissatī’ti, sammā nu khau sau, āvusau, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘ṭhānañhaitaṃ, āvusau, vij‍jati, yā puratthimāya disāya āgacchaiyya bhusā  vātavuṭṭhi| sā tasmiṃ udakarahadai ūmiṃ janaiyya| yā pacchimāya disāya āgacchaiyya…pai॰… yā uttarāya disāya āgacchaiyya… yā dakkhiṇāya disāya āgacchaiyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi| sā tasmiṃ udakarahadai ūmiṃ janaiyyāti| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, idhaikac‍cau puggalau sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau ‘lābhimhi catutthassa jhānassā’ti saṃsaṭṭhau viharati bhikkhūhi…pai॰… sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati|

‘‘idha, panāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau sabbanimittānaṃ amanasikārā animittaṃ caitausamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau ‘lābhimhi animittassa caitausamādhissā’ti saṃsaṭṭhau viharati bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi upāsakaihi  upāsikāhi rañ‍ñā rājamahāmattaihi titthiyaihi titthiyasāvakaihi| tassa saṃsaṭṭhassa vissatthassa pākatassa bhassamanuyuttassa viharatau rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti| sau rāgānuddhaṃsitaina cittaina sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, rājā vā rājamahāmattau vā caturaṅginiyā saināya addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai aikarattiṃ vāsaṃ upagacchaiyya| tatra tattha (sī॰ pī॰) hatthisaddaina assasaddaina rathasaddaina pattisaddaina bhairipaṇavasaṅkhatiṇavanin‍nādasaddaina cīrikasaddau ciriḷikāsaddau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) antaradhāyaiyya antaradhāpaiyya (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰)| yau nu khau, āvusau, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na dāni amusmiṃ vanasaṇḍai punadaiva cīrikasaddau pātubhavissatī’ti, sammā nu khau sau, āvusau, vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘ṭhānañhaitaṃ, āvusau, vij‍jati, yaṃ sau rājā vā rājamahāmattau vā tamhā vanasaṇḍā pak‍kamaiyya, atha punadaiva cīrikasaddau pātubhavaiyyāti| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, idhaikac‍cau puggalau sabbanimittānaṃ amanasikārā animittaṃ caitausamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau ‘lābhimhi animittassa caitausamādhissā’ti saṃsaṭṭhau viharati bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi upāsakaihi upāsikāhi  rañ‍ñā rājamahāmattaihi titthiyaihi titthiyasāvakaihi| tassa saṃsaṭṭhassa vissatthassa pākatassa bhassamanuyuttassa viharatau rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti| sau rāgānuddhaṃsitaina cittaina sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattatī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā cittau hatthisāriputtau aparaina samayaina sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| atha khau cittassa hatthisāriputtassa sahāyakā  bhikkhū yaināyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākauṭṭhikaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau āyasmatā mahākauṭṭhikaina cittau hatthisāriputtau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau – ‘imāsañ‍ca imāsañ‍ca vihārasamāpattīnaṃ cittau hatthisāriputtau lābhī, atha ca pana sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissatī’ti; udāhu daivatā aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ – ‘cittau, bhantai, hatthisāriputtau imāsañ‍ca imāsañ‍ca vihārasamāpattīnaṃ lābhī, atha ca pana sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissatī’’’ti? ‘‘caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau mai, āvusau – ‘cittau hatthisāriputtau imāsañ‍ca imāsañ‍ca vihārasamāpattīnaṃ lābhī, atha ca pana sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissatī’ti| daivatāpi mai aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisuṃ – ‘cittau, bhantai, hatthisāriputtau imāsañ‍ca imāsañ‍ca vihārasamāpattīnaṃ lābhī, atha ca pana sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissatī’’ti|

atha khau cittassa hatthisāriputtassa sahāyakā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ  nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘cittau, bhantai, hatthisāriputtau imāsañ‍ca imāsañ‍ca vihārasamāpattīnaṃ lābhī, atha ca pana sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattatī’’ti| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, cittau ciraṃ sarissati padissati (ka॰) naikkhammassā’’ti|

atha khau cittau hatthisāriputtau nacirassaiva kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaji| atha khau  āyasmā cittau hatthisāriputtau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti  abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā cittau hatthisāriputtau arahataṃ ahausīti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. majjhaisuttaṃ

61. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ viharati isipatanai migadāyai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulānaṃ thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ maṇḍalamāḷai san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘‘vuttamidaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā pārāyanai maittaiyyapañhai –

cūḷani॰ tissamittaiyyamāṇavapucchā 67 ‘‘yau ubhauntai viditvāna, majjhai mantā na lippati na limpati (ka॰)|

taṃ brūmi mahāpurisauti, saudha sibbini sibbani (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) mac‍cagā’’ti||

‘‘katamau nu khau, āvusau, aikau antau, katamau dutiyau antau, kiṃ majjhai, kā sibbinī’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu thairai bhikkhū  aitadavauca – ‘‘phassau khau, āvusau, aikau antau, phassasamudayau dutiyau antau, phassaniraudhau majjhai, taṇhā sibbinī; taṇhā hi naṃ sibbati tassa tassaiva bhavassa abhinibbattiyā| aittāvatā khau, āvusau, bhikkhu abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānāti, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānāti, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānantau abhijānitvā (ka॰) pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānantau parijānitvā (ka॰) diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hautī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘atītaṃ khau, āvusau, aikau antau, anāgataṃ dutiyau antau, pac‍cuppan‍naṃ majjhai, taṇhā sibbinī; taṇhā hi naṃ sibbati tassa tassaiva bhavassa  abhinibbattiyā| aittāvatā khau, āvusau, bhikkhu abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānāti, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānāti, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānantau, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānantau diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hautī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘sukhā, āvusau, vaidanā aikau antau, dukkhā vaidanā dutiyau antau, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā majjhai, taṇhā sibbinī; taṇhā hi naṃ  sibbati tassa tassaiva bhavassa abhinibbattiyā| aittāvatā khau, āvusau, bhikkhu abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānāti, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānāti, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānantau, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānantau diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hautī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘nāmaṃ khau, āvusau, aikau antau, rūpaṃ dutiyau antau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ majjhai, taṇhā sibbinī; taṇhā hi naṃ sibbati tassa tassaiva bhavassa abhinibbattiyā| aittāvatā khau, āvusau, bhikkhu abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānāti, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānāti, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānantau pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānantau diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hautī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘cha khau, āvusau, ajjhattikāni āyatanāni aikau antau, cha bāhirāni āyatanāni dutiyau antau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ majjhai, taṇhā sibbinī; taṇhā hi naṃ sibbati tassa tassaiva bhavassa abhinibbattiyā| aittāvatā khau āvusau, bhikkhu abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ  abhijānāti, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ  parijānāti, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānantau pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānantau diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hautī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘sak‍kāyau khau, āvusau, aikau antau, sak‍kāyasamudayau dutiyau antau, sak‍kāyaniraudhau majjhai, taṇhā sibbinī; taṇhā hi naṃ sibbati tassa tassaiva bhavassa abhinibbattiyā| aittāvatā khau, āvusau, bhikkhu abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānāti, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānāti, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānantau pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānantau sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hautī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘byākataṃ khau, āvusau, amhaihi sabbaihaiva yathāsakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ| āyāmāvusau, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamissāma; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaissāma| yathā nau bhagavā byākarissati tathā naṃ dhāraissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti  khau thairā bhikkhū tassa bhikkhunau pac‍cassausuṃ| atha khau thairā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau thairā bhikkhū yāvatakau ahausi sabbaihaiva saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau, taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘kassa nu khau, bhantai, subhāsita’’nti? ‘‘sabbaisaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, subhāsitaṃ pariyāyaina, api ca yaṃ mayā sandhāya bhāsitaṃ pārāyanai maittaiyyapañhai –

‘‘yau ubhauntai viditvāna, majjhai mantā na lippati|

taṃ brūmi mahāpurisauti, saudha sibbinimac‍cagā’’ti||

‘‘taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau thairā bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘phassau khau, bhikkhavai, aikau antau, phassasamudayau dutiyau antau, phassaniraudhau majjhai, taṇhā sibbinī; taṇhā hi naṃ sibbati tassa tassaiva bhavassa abhinibbattiyā| aittāvatā khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānāti, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānāti, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhijānantau  pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ parijānantau diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. purisindriyañāṇasuttaṃ

62. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina daṇḍakappakaṃ nāma kausalānaṃ nigamau tadavasari| atha khau bhagavā maggā auk‍kamma añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| tai ca bhikkhū daṇḍakappakaṃ pavisiṃsu āvasathaṃ pariyaisituṃ|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ yaina aciravatī nadī tainupasaṅkami gattāni parisiñ‍cituṃ| aciravatiyā nadiyā gattāni parisiñ‍citvā pac‍cuttaritvā aikacīvarau aṭṭhāsi gattāni pubbāpayamānau| atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau ānanda, sabbaṃ caitasau saman‍nāharitvā nu khau daivadattau bhagavatā byākatau – ‘āpāyikau daivadattau nairayikau  kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau’ti cūḷava॰ 348; a॰ ni॰ 8.7 passitabbaṃ, udāhu kainacidaiva pariyāyainā’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā byākata’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ yaina aciravatī nadī tainupasaṅkamiṃ gattāni parisiñ‍cituṃ| aciravatiyā nadiyā gattāni parisiñ‍citvā pac‍cuttaritvā aikacīvarau aṭṭhāsiṃ gattāni pubbāpayamānau| atha khau, bhantai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, ānanda sabbaṃ caitasau saman‍nāharitvā nu  khau daivadattau bhagavatā byākatau – āpāyikau daivadattau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchauti, udāhu kainacidaiva pariyāyainā’ti? aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ, bhantai, taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘aivaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā byākata’’’nti|

‘‘sau  vā sau ca (syā॰) khau, ānanda, bhikkhu navau bhavissati acirapabbajitau, thairau vā pana bālau abyattau| kathañhi nāma yaṃ mayā aikaṃsaina byākataṃ tattha dvaijjhaṃ āpaj‍jissati! nāhaṃ, ānanda, añ‍ñaṃ aikapuggalampi samanupassāmi, yau aivaṃ mayā sabbaṃ caitasau saman‍nāharitvā byākatau, yathayidaṃ daivadattau| yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, ānanda, daivadattassa vālaggakauṭinittudanamattampi suk‍kadhammaṃ addasaṃ; naiva tāvāhaṃ daivadattaṃ byākāsiṃ – ‘āpāyikau daivadattau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau’ti| yatau ca khau ahaṃ, ānanda, daivadattassa vālaggakauṭinittudanamattampi suk‍kadhammaṃ na addasaṃ; athāhaṃ daivadattaṃ byākāsiṃ – ‘āpāyikau daivadattau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, ānanda, gūthakūpau sādhikapaurisau pūrau gūthassa samatittikau| tatra purisau sasīsakau nimuggau assa| tassa kaucidaiva purisau uppaj‍jaiyya atthakāmau hitakāmau yaugakkhaimakāmau tamhā gūthakūpā uddharitukāmau| sau taṃ gūthakūpaṃ samantānuparigacchantau naiva passaiyya tassa purisassa vālaggakauṭinittudanamattampi gūthaina amakkhitaṃ, yattha taṃ  gahaitvā uddharaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, ānanda, yatau daivadattassa vālaggakauṭinittudanamattampi suk‍kadhammaṃ na addasaṃ; athāhaṃ daivadattaṃ  byākāsiṃ – ‘āpāyikau daivadattau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau’ti| sacai tumhai, ānanda, suṇaiyyātha tathāgatassa purisindriyañāṇāni vibhajissāmī’’ti vibhajantassāti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)?

‘‘aitassa, bhagavā, kālau; aitassa, sugata, kālau yaṃ bhagavā purisindriyañāṇāni vibhajaiyya| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘tainahānanda, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāhaṃ, ānanda, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa vij‍jamānā kusalāpi dhammā akusalāpi dhammā’ti| tamainaṃ aparaina samayaina aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa kusalā dhammā antarahitā, akusalā dhammā sammukhībhūtā| atthi ca khvassa kusalamūlaṃ asamucchin‍naṃ, tamhā tassa kusalā kusalaṃ pātubhavissati| aivamayaṃ puggalau āyatiṃ aparihānadhammau bhavissatī’ti| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, bījāni akhaṇḍāni apūtīni avātātapahatāni sāradāni sukhasayitāni sukhaittai suparikammakatāya bhūmiyā nikkhittāni| jānaiyyāsi tvaṃ, ānanda, imāni bījāni vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissantī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, ānanda, idhaikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa vij‍jamānā kusalāpi dhammā  akusalāpi dhammā’ti| tamainaṃ aparaina samayaina aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa kusalā dhammā antarahitā, akusalā dhammā  sammukhībhūtā| atthi ca khvassa kusalamūlaṃ asamucchin‍naṃ, tamhā tassa kusalā kusalaṃ pātubhavissati| aivamayaṃ puggalau āyatiṃ aparihānadhammau bhavissatī’ti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa purisapuggalau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau hauti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa purisindriyañāṇaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditaṃ hauti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa āyatiṃ dhammasamuppādau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau hauti|

‘‘idha panāhaṃ, ānanda, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa vij‍jamānā kusalāpi dhammā akusalāpi dhammā’ti| tamainaṃ aparaina samayaina  aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa akusalā dhammā antarahitā, kusalā dhammā sammukhībhūtā| atthi ca khvassa akusalamūlaṃ asamucchin‍naṃ, tamhā tassa akusalā akusalaṃ pātubhavissati| aivamayaṃ puggalau āyatiṃ parihānadhammau bhavissatī’ti| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, bījāni akhaṇḍāni apūtīni avātātapahatāni sāradāni sukhasayitāni puthusilāya nikkhittāni| jānaiyyāsi tvaṃ, ānanda, nayimāni bījāni vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissantī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, ānanda, idhaikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa vij‍jamānā kusalāpi dhammā akusalāpi dhammā’ti| tamainaṃ aparaina samayaina aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa akusalā dhammā antarahitā, kusalā dhammā sammukhībhūtā| atthi  ca khvassa akusalamūlaṃ asamucchin‍naṃ, tamhā tassa akusalā akusalaṃ pātubhavissati| aivamayaṃ puggalau āyatiṃ  parihānadhammau bhavissatī’ti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa purisapuggalau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau hauti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa purisindriyañāṇaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditaṃ hauti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa āyatiṃ dhammasamuppādau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau hauti|

‘‘idha panāhaṃ, ānanda, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa vij‍jamānā kusalāpi dhammā akusalāpi dhammā’ti| tamainaṃ aparaina samayaina aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘natthi imassa puggalassa vālaggakauṭinittudanamattaupi suk‍kau dhammau, saman‍nāgatauyaṃ puggalau aikantakāḷakaihi akusalaihi dhammaihi, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jissatī’ti| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, bījāni khaṇḍāni pūtīni vātātapahatāni sukhaittai suparikammakatāya bhūmiyā nikkhittāni| jānaiyyāsi tvaṃ, ānanda, nayimāni bījāni vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissantī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, ānanda, idhaikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa vij‍jamānā kusalāpi dhammā akusalāpi dhammā’ti| tamainaṃ aparaina samayaina aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘natthi imassa puggalassa vālaggakauṭinittudanamattaupi suk‍kau dhammau, saman‍nāgatauyaṃ puggalau aikantakāḷakaihi akusalaihi dhammaihi, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ  maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ  upapaj‍jissatī’ti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa purisapuggalau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau hauti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa purisindriyañāṇaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditaṃ hauti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa āyatiṃ dhammasamuppādau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau hautī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sak‍kā  nu khau, bhantai, imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ aparaipi tayau puggalā sappaṭibhāgā pañ‍ñāpaitu’’nti? ‘‘sak‍kā, ānandā’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, ānanda, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa vij‍jamānā kusalāpi dhammā akusalāpi dhammā’ti| tamainaṃ aparaina samayaina aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa kusalā dhammā antarahitā, akusalā dhammā sammukhībhūtā| atthi ca khvassa kusalamūlaṃ  asamucchin‍naṃ, tampi sabbaina sabbaṃ samugghātaṃ gacchati| aivamayaṃ puggalau āyatiṃ parihānadhammau bhavissatī’ti| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, aṅgārāni ādittāni sampaj‍jalitāni sajautibhūtāni puthusilāya nikkhittāni| jānaiyyāsi tvaṃ, ānanda, nayimāni aṅgārāni vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissantī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘saiyyathāpi vā pana, ānanda, sāyanhasamayaṃ sāyanhasamayai (syā॰ ka॰) sūriyai augacchantai, jānaiyyāsi tvaṃ, ānanda, ālaukau antaradhāyissati andhakārau pātubhavissatī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘saiyyathāpi  vā, panānanda, abhidau addharattaṃ bhattakālasamayai, jānaiyyāsi tvaṃ, ānanda, ālaukau antarahitau andhakārau pātubhūtau’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, ānanda, idhaikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa vij‍jamānā kusalāpi dhammā akusalāpi dhammā’ti| tamainaṃ aparaina samayaina aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa kusalā dhammā antarahitā, akusalā dhammā sammukhībhūtā| atthi ca khvassa kusalamūlaṃ asamucchin‍naṃ, tampi sabbaina sabbaṃ samugghātaṃ gacchati| aivamayaṃ puggalau āyatiṃ parihānadhammau bhavissatī’ti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa purisapuggalau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau hauti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa  purisindriyañāṇaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditaṃ hauti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa āyatiṃ dhammasamuppādau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau hauti|

‘‘idha  panāhaṃ, ānanda, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa vij‍jamānā kusalāpi dhammā akusalāpi dhammā’ti| tamainaṃ aparaina samayaina aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa akusalā dhammā antarahitā, kusalā dhammā sammukhībhūtā| atthi ca khvassa akusalamūlaṃ asamucchin‍naṃ, tampi sabbaina sabbaṃ samugghātaṃ gacchati| aivamayaṃ puggalau āyatiṃ aparihānadhammau bhavissatī’ti| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, aṅgārāni ādittāni sampaj‍jalitāni sajautibhūtāni sukkhai tiṇapuñ‍jai vā  kaṭṭhapuñ‍jai vā nikkhittāni| jānaiyyāsi tvaṃ, ānanda, imāni aṅgārāni vuḍḍhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissantī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘saiyyathāpi vā  panānanda, rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ rattipac‍cūsasamayai (ka॰) sūriyai uggacchantai, jānaiyyāsi tvaṃ, ānanda, andhakārau antaradhāyissati, ālaukau pātubhavissatī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘saiyyathāpi vā panānanda, abhidau majjhanhikai bhattakālasamayai, jānaiyyāsi tvaṃ, ānanda, andhakārau antarahitau ālaukau pātubhūtau’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, ānanda, idhaikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa vij‍jamānā kusalāpi dhammā akusalāpi dhammā’ti| tamainaṃ aparaina samayaina aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa akusalā dhammā antarahitā, kusalā dhammā sammukhībhūtā| atthi ca khvassa akusalamūlaṃ asamucchin‍naṃ, tampi sabbaina sabbaṃ samugghātaṃ gacchati| aivamayaṃ puggalau āyatiṃ aparihānadhammau bhavissatī’ti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa purisapuggalau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau hauti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa purisindriyañāṇaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca  viditaṃ hauti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa āyatiṃ dhammasamuppādau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau hauti|

‘‘idha panāhaṃ, ānanda, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa vij‍jamānā kusalāpi dhammā akusalāpi dhammā’ti| tamainaṃ aparaina samayaina aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘natthi imassa  puggalassa vālaggakauṭinittudanamattaupi akusalau dhammau, saman‍nāgatauyaṃ puggalau aikantasuk‍kaihi anavaj‍jaihi dhammaihi, diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyissatī’ti| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, aṅgārāni sītāni nibbutāni sukkhai tiṇapuñ‍jai vā kaṭṭhapuñ‍jai vā nikkhittāni| jānaiyyāsi tvaṃ, ānanda, nayimāni aṅgārāni vuḍḍhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissantī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, ānanda, idhaikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ  caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘imassa khau puggalassa vij‍jamānā kusalāpi dhammā akusalāpi dhammā’ti| tamainaṃ aparaina samayaina aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘natthi imassa puggalassa vālaggakauṭinittudanamattaupi akusalau dhammau, saman‍nāgatauyaṃ puggalau aikantasuk‍kaihi anavaj‍jaihi dhammaihi, diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyissatī’ti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa purisapuggalau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau hauti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa purisindriyañāṇaṃ  caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditaṃ hauti| aivampi khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa āyatiṃ dhammasamuppādau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau hauti|

‘‘tatrānanda, yai tai purimā tayau puggalā taisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ aikau aparihānadhammau, aikau parihānadhammau, aikau āpāyikau nairayikau| tatrānanda, yaimai pacchimā tayau puggalā imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ aikau parihānadhammau, aikau aparihānadhammau, aikau parinibbānadhammau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. nibbaidhikasuttaṃ

63. ‘‘nibbaidhikapariyāyaṃ  vau, bhikkhavai, dhammapariyāyaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ  suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamau ca sau, bhikkhavai, nibbaidhikapariyāyau dhammapariyāyau? kāmā, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbā, kāmānaṃ nidānasambhavau vaiditabbau, kāmānaṃ vaimattatā vaiditabbā, kāmānaṃ vipākau vaiditabbau, kāmaniraudhau kāmānaṃ niraudhau (ka॰) aivaṃ vaidanāniraudhau-ic‍cādīsupi vaiditabbau, kāmaniraudhagāminī kāmānaṃ niraudhagāminī (ka॰) aivaṃ vaidanāniraudhagāminī-ic‍cādīsupi paṭipadā vaiditabbā|

‘‘vaidanā, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbā, vaidanānaṃ nidānasambhavau vaiditabbau, vaidanānaṃ vaimattatā vaiditabbā, vaidanānaṃ vipākau vaiditabbau, vaidanāniraudhau vaiditabbau, vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā vaiditabbā|

‘‘sañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbā, sañ‍ñānaṃ nidānasambhavau vaiditabbau, sañ‍ñānaṃ vaimattatā vaiditabbā, sañ‍ñānaṃ vipākau vaiditabbau, sañ‍ñāniraudhau vaiditabbau, sañ‍ñāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā vaiditabbā|

‘‘āsavā, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbā, āsavānaṃ nidānasambhavau vaiditabbau, āsavānaṃ vaimattatā vaiditabbā, āsavānaṃ vipākau vaiditabbau, āsavaniraudhau vaiditabbau, āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā vaiditabbā|

‘‘kammaṃ, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbaṃ, kammānaṃ nidānasambhavau vaiditabbau, kammānaṃ vaimattatā vaiditabbā, kammānaṃ vipākau vaiditabbau, kammaniraudhau vaiditabbau, kammaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā vaiditabbā|

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbaṃ, dukkhassa nidānasambhavau vaiditabbau, dukkhassa vaimattatā vaiditabbā, dukkhassa vipākau vaiditabbau, dukkhaniraudhau vaiditabbau, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā vaiditabbā|

‘‘‘kāmā, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbā, kāmānaṃ nidānasambhavau vaiditabbau, kāmānaṃ  vaimattatā vaiditabbā, kāmānaṃ vipākau  vaiditabbau, kāmaniraudhau vaiditabbau, kāmaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā vaiditabbā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ ma॰ ni॰ 1.166; saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.268? pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, kāmaguṇā – cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| api ca khau, bhikkhavai, naitai kāmā kāmaguṇā nāmaitai tai kāmaguṇā nāma naitai kāmā (ka॰) ariyassa vinayai vuc‍canti –

kathā॰ 514 ‘‘saṅkapparāgau purisassa kāmau,

naitai na tai (syā॰) kāmā yāni citrāni laukai|

saṅkapparāgau purisassa kāmau,

tiṭṭhanti citrāni tathaiva laukai|

athaittha dhīrā vinayanti chanda’’nti||

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, kāmānaṃ nidānasambhavau? phassau, bhikkhavai, kāmānaṃ nidānasambhavau|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, kāmānaṃ vaimattatā? añ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, kāmau rūpaisu, añ‍ñau kāmau saddaisu, añ‍ñau kāmau gandhaisu, añ‍ñau kāmau rasaisu, añ‍ñau kāmau phauṭṭhabbaisu| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kāmānaṃ vaimattatā|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, kāmānaṃ vipākau? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, kāmayamānau taj‍jaṃ taj‍jaṃ attabhāvaṃ abhinibbattaiti  puñ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ vā apuñ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ vā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kāmānaṃ vipākau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, kāmaniraudhau? phassaniraudhau phassaniraudhā (syā॰), bhikkhavai, kāmaniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau kāmaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau yatau ca khau (bahūsu), bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ kāmai pajānāti, aivaṃ kāmānaṃ nidānasambhavaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ kāmānaṃ vaimattataṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ kāmānaṃ vipākaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ kāmaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ kāmaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau imaṃ nibbaidhikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pajānāti kāmaniraudhaṃ| kāmā, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbā…pai॰… kāmaniraudhagāminī sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati paṭipadā vaiditabbāti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘vaidanā, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbā…pai॰… vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā vaiditabbāti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tissau imā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, vaidanānaṃ nidānasambhavau? phassau, bhikkhavai, vaidanānaṃ nidānasambhavau|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, vaidanānaṃ vaimattatā? atthi, bhikkhavai, sāmisā sukhā vaidanā, atthi nirāmisā sukhā vaidanā, atthi sāmisā dukkhā vaidanā, atthi nirāmisā dukkhā vaidanā, atthi sāmisā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā, atthi nirāmisā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vaidanānaṃ vaimattatā|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, vaidanānaṃ vipākau? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, vaidiyamānau vaidayamānau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) a॰ ni॰ 4.233 taj‍jaṃ taj‍jaṃ attabhāvaṃ abhinibbattaiti puñ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ vā apuñ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ vā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vaidanānaṃ vipākau|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, vaidanāniraudhau? phassaniraudhau phassaniraudhā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), bhikkhavai, vaidanāniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ vaidanaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ vaidanānaṃ nidānasambhavaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ vaidanānaṃ  vaimattataṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ vaidanānaṃ vipākaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ vaidanāniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ vaidanāniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti| sau imaṃ nibbaidhikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pajānāti vaidanāniraudhaṃ| vaidanā, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbā…pai॰… vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā vaiditabbāti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘sañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbā…pai॰… sañ‍ñāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā vaiditabbāti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? chayimā, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñā – rūpasañ‍ñā, saddasañ‍ñā, gandhasañ‍ñā, rasasañ‍ñā, phauṭṭhabbasañ‍ñā, dhammasañ‍ñā|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñānaṃ nidānasambhavau? phassau, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñānaṃ nidānasambhavau|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñānaṃ vaimattatā? añ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñā rūpaisu, añ‍ñā  sañ‍ñā saddaisu añ‍ñā bhikkhavai rūpaisu sañ‍ñā añ‍ñā saddaisu sañ‍ñā (ka॰) aivaṃ saisaisupi, añ‍ñā sañ‍ñā gandhaisu, añ‍ñā sañ‍ñā rasaisu, añ‍ñā sañ‍ñā phauṭṭhabbaisu, añ‍ñā sañ‍ñā dhammaisu| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñānaṃ vaimattatā|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñānaṃ vipākau? vauhāravaipak‍kaṃ vauhāravaipak‍kāhaṃ (syā॰ pī॰), vauhārapak‍kāhaṃ (sī॰), bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍ñā (syā॰ pī॰) vadāmi| yathā yathā naṃ sañ‍jānāti tathā tathā vauharati, aivaṃ sañ‍ñī ahausinti ahausīti (ka॰)| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñānaṃ vipākau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñāniraudhau? phassaniraudhau, phassaniraudhā (syā॰ ka॰) bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñāniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau sañ‍ñāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau  khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ sañ‍ñānaṃ nidānasambhavaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ sañ‍ñānaṃ vaimattataṃ  pajānāti, aivaṃ sañ‍ñānaṃ vipākaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ sañ‍ñāniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ sañ‍ñāniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau imaṃ nibbaidhikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pajānāti sañ‍ñāniraudhaṃ| sañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbā…pai॰… sañ‍ñāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā vaiditabbāti| iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘āsavā, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbā…pai॰… āsavaniraudhagāminī sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati paṭipadā vaiditabbāti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tayaumai, bhikkhavai, āsavā – kāmāsavau, bhavāsavau, avij‍jāsavau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, āsavānaṃ nidānasambhavau? avij‍jā, bhikkhavai, āsavānaṃ nidānasambhavau|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, āsavānaṃ vaimattatā? atthi, bhikkhavai, āsavā nirayagamanīyā nirayagāminiyā (sī॰ ka॰), atthi āsavā tiracchānayaunigamanīyā, atthi āsavā paittivisayagamanīyā, atthi āsavā manussalaukagamanīyā, atthi āsavā  daivalaukagamanīyā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, āsavānaṃ vaimattatā|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, āsavānaṃ vipākau? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, avij‍jāgatau taj‍jaṃ taj‍jaṃ attabhāvaṃ abhinibbattaiti puñ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ vā apuñ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ vā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, āsavānaṃ vipākau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, āsavaniraudhau? avij‍jāniraudhau, bhikkhavai, āsavaniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau āsavaniraudhagāminī sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ āsavai pajānāti, aivaṃ āsavānaṃ nidānasambhavaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ āsavānaṃ vaimattataṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ āsavānaṃ vipākaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ āsavānaṃ niraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ āsavānaṃ niraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau imaṃ nibbaidhikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pajānāti āsavaniraudhaṃ| āsavā, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbā…pai॰… āsavaniraudhagāminī  sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati paṭipadā vaiditabbāti| iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘kammaṃ, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbaṃ…pai॰… kammaniraudhagāminī sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati paṭipadā vaiditabbāti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? kathā॰ 539 caitanāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ vadāmi| caitayitvā kammaṃ karauti – kāyaina vācāya manasā|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, kammānaṃ nidānasambhavau? phassau, bhikkhavai, kammānaṃ nidānasambhavau|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, kammānaṃ vaimattatā? atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ nirayavaidanīyaṃ, atthi kammaṃ tiracchānayaunivaidanīyaṃ, atthi kammaṃ paittivisayavaidanīyaṃ, atthi kammaṃ manussalaukavaidanīyaṃ, atthi kammaṃ daivalaukavaidanīyaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammānaṃ vaimattatā|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, kammānaṃ vipākau? tividhāhaṃ imāhaṃ (ka॰), bhikkhavai, kammānaṃ vipākaṃ vadāmi  – diṭṭhaiva diṭṭhai vā (sī॰) dhammai, upapaj‍jai vā upapaj‍jaṃ vā (ka॰ sī॰, a॰ ni॰ 10.217), upapaj‍ja vā (?), ma॰ ni॰ 3.303 pāḷiyā tadatthavaṇṇanāya ca saṃsaddaitabbaṃ, aparai vā pariyāyai| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammānaṃ vipākau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, kammaniraudhau? phassaniraudhau, phassaniraudhā (ka॰ sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) bhikkhavai, kammaniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau kammaniraudhagāminī sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ kammaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ kammānaṃ nidānasambhavaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ kammānaṃ vaimattataṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ kammānaṃ vipākaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ kammaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ kammaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau imaṃ nibbaidhikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pajānāti kammaniraudhaṃ| kammaṃ, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbaṃ…pai॰… kammaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā  vaiditabbāti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbaṃ, dukkhassa nidānasambhavau vaiditabbau, dukkhassa vaimattatā vaiditabbā, dukkhassa vipākau vaiditabbau, dukkhaniraudhau vaiditabbau, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā vaiditabbāti| iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? jātipi dukkhā, jarāpi dukkhā, byādhipi dukkhau byādhipi dukkhā (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰), maraṇampi dukkhaṃ, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsāpi dukkhā, yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ, saṃkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā pañ‍cupādānakkhandhāpi (ka॰) dukkhā|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa nidānasambhavau? taṇhā, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa nidānasambhavau|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa vaimattatā? atthi, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ adhimattaṃ, atthi parittaṃ, atthi dandhavirāgi, atthi khippavirāgi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa vaimattatā|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa vipākau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau yaina dukkhaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau pariyādiṇṇacittau (ka॰) saucati kilamati paridaivati, urattāḷiṃ kandati, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati, yaina vā pana dukkhaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau bahiddhā pariyaiṭṭhiṃ āpaj‍jati – ‘kau sau na (ka॰) aikapadaṃ dvipadaṃ jānāti pajānāti (ka॰) imassa dukkhassa niraudhāyā’ti? sammauhavaipak‍kaṃ vāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ vadāmi pariyaiṭṭhivaipak‍kaṃ vā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa vipākau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhau? taṇhāniraudhau, taṇhāniraudhā (ka॰ sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau dukkhassa niraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ dukkhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ  dukkhassa nidānasambhavaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ dukkhassa vaimattataṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ dukkhassa vipākaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau imaṃ nibbaidhikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pajānāti dukkhaniraudhaṃ| dukkhaṃ, bhikkhavai, vaiditabbaṃ, dukkhassa nidānasambhavau vaiditabbau, dukkhassa vaimattatā vaiditabbā, dukkhassa vipākau vaiditabbau, dukkhaniraudhau vaiditabbau, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā vaiditabbāti| iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘ayaṃ khau sau, bhikkhavai, nibbaidhikapariyāyau dhammapariyāyau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. sīhanādasuttaṃ

64. ma॰ ni॰ 1.148; vibha॰ 760; a॰ ni॰ 10.21; paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.44 ‘‘chayimāni, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalāni, yaihi balaihi saman‍nāgatau tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti| katamāni cha? idha, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau ṭhānañ‍ca ṭhānatau aṭṭhānañ‍ca aṭṭhānatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau ṭhānañ‍ca ṭhānatau aṭṭhānañ‍ca aṭṭhānatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānasau haitusau vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānasau haitusau vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau jhānavimaukkhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ  saṃkilaisaṃ vaudānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau…pai॰… idampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ  – aikampi jātiṃ, dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ, dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| idampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau  dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti, idampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati, idampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa tathāgatabalaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ  paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti| imāni  khau, bhikkhavai, cha tathāgatassa tathāgatabalāni, yaihi balaihi saman‍nāgatau tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti|

‘‘tatra cai, bhikkhavai, parai tathāgataṃ ṭhānañ‍ca ṭhānatau aṭṭhānañ‍ca aṭṭhānatau yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaina upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchanti| yathā yathā, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa ṭhānañ‍ca ṭhānatau aṭṭhānañ‍ca aṭṭhānatau yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ viditaṃ tathā tathā taisaṃ tathāgatau ṭhānañ‍ca ṭhānatau aṭṭhānañ‍ca aṭṭhānatau yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaina pañhaṃ puṭṭhau byākarauti|

‘‘tatra cai, bhikkhavai, parai tathāgataṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānasau haitusau vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaina upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchanti| yathā yathā, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānasau haitusau vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ viditaṃ tathā tathā taisaṃ tathāgatau atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānasau haitusau vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaina pañhaṃ puṭṭhau byākarauti|

‘‘tatra cai, bhikkhavai, parai tathāgataṃ jhānavimaukkhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ saṃkilaisaṃ vaudānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaina upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchanti| yathā yathā, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa jhānavimaukkhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ saṃkilaisaṃ vaudānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ viditaṃ, tathā tathā taisaṃ tathāgatau jhānavimaukkhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ saṃkilaisaṃ vaudānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaina pañhaṃ puṭṭhau byākarauti|

‘‘tatra  cai, bhikkhavai, parai tathāgataṃ pubbainivāsānussatiṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaina upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchanti| yathā yathā, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa pubbainivāsānussatiṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ viditaṃ, tathā  tathā taisaṃ  tathāgatau pubbainivāsānussatiṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaina pañhaṃ puṭṭhau byākarauti|

‘‘tatra cai, bhikkhavai, parai tathāgataṃ sattānaṃ cutūpapātaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaina upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchanti| yathā yathā, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa sattānaṃ cutūpapātaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ viditaṃ, tathā tathā taisaṃ tathāgatau sattānaṃ cutūpapātaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaina pañhaṃ puṭṭhau byākarauti|

‘‘tatra  cai, bhikkhavai, parai tathāgataṃ āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaina upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchanti| yathā yathā, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ viditaṃ, tathā tathā taisaṃ tathāgatau āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaina pañhaṃ puṭṭhau byākarauti|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yampidaṃ yamidaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), yadidaṃ (ka॰) ṭhānañ‍ca ṭhānatau aṭṭhānañ‍ca aṭṭhānatau yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ tampi samāhitassa vadāmi nau asamāhitassa| yampidaṃ yadidaṃ (ka॰) atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānasau haitusau vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ tampi samāhitassa vadāmi nau asamāhitassa| yampidaṃ yadidaṃ (ka॰) jhānavimaukkhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ saṃkilaisaṃ vaudānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ tampi samāhitassa vadāmi nau asamāhitassa| yampidaṃ yadidaṃ (ka॰) pubbainivāsānussatiṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ tampi samāhitassa vadāmi nau asamāhitassa| yampidaṃ yadidaṃ (ka॰) sattānaṃ cutūpapātaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ tampi samāhitassa vadāmi nau asamāhitassa| yampidaṃ yadidaṃ (ka॰) āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ tampi samāhitassa vadāmi nau asamāhitassa| iti khau, bhikkhavai, samādhi maggau, asamādhi kummaggau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

mahāvaggau chaṭṭhau| paṭhamau (syā॰ ka॰)


tassuddānaṃ  –

sauṇau  phaggunau bhijāti, āsavā dāruhatthi ca|

majjhai ñāṇaṃ nibbaidhikaṃ, sīhanādauti tai dasāti||


7. daivatāvaggau

1. anāgāmiphalasuttaṃ

65. ‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau anāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamai cha? assaddhiyaṃ, ahirikaṃ, anauttappaṃ, kausaj‍jaṃ, muṭṭhassac‍caṃ, duppañ‍ñataṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai appahāya abhabbau anāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikātuṃ|

‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau anāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamai cha? assaddhiyaṃ, ahirikaṃ, anauttappaṃ, kausaj‍jaṃ, muṭṭhassac‍caṃ, duppañ‍ñataṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai pahāya bhabbau anāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikātu’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. arahattasuttaṃ

66. ‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamai cha? thinaṃ thīnaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), middhaṃ, uddhac‍caṃ, kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, assaddhiyaṃ, pamādaṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai appahāya abhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ|

‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamai cha? thinaṃ, middhaṃ, uddhac‍caṃ, kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, assaddhiyaṃ, pamādaṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai pahāya bhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātu’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. mittasuttaṃ

67. ‘‘‘sau  vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pāpamittau pāpasahāyau pāpasampavaṅkau, pāpamittai pāpakai mittai (ka॰) saivamānau bhajamānau payirupāsamānau, taisañ‍ca diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jamānau ābhisamācārikaṃ dhammaṃ  paripūraissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘ābhisamācārikaṃ dhammaṃ  aparipūraitvā saikhaṃ dhammaṃ paripūraissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘saikhaṃ dhammaṃ aparipūraitvā sīlāni paripūraissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sīlāni aparipūraitvā kāmarāgaṃ vā rūparāgaṃ vā arūparāgaṃ vā pajahissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau, kalyāṇamittai saivamānau bhajamānau payirupāsamānau, taisañ‍ca diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jamānau ābhisamācārikaṃ dhammaṃ paripūraissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘ābhisamācārikaṃ dhammaṃ paripūraitvā saikhaṃ dhammaṃ paripūraissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘saikhaṃ dhammaṃ paripūraitvā sīlāni paripūraissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sīlāni paripūraitvā kāmarāgaṃ vā rūparāgaṃ vā arūparāgaṃ vā pajahissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. saṅgaṇikārāmasuttaṃ

68. ‘‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅgaṇikārāmau saṅgaṇikaratau saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ anuyuttau, gaṇārāmau gaṇaratau gaṇārāmataṃ anuyuttau, aikau pavivaikai abhiramissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘aikau pavivaikai anabhiramantau cittassa  nimittaṃ gahaissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘cittassa nimittaṃ agaṇhantau sammādiṭṭhiṃ paripūraissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sammādiṭṭhiṃ aparipūraitvā sammāsamādhiṃ paripūraissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sammāsamādhiṃ aparipūraitvā saṃyaujanāni  pajahissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘saṃyaujanāni appahāya nibbānaṃ sacchikarissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na saṅgaṇikārāmau na saṅgaṇikaratau na saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ anuyuttau, na gaṇārāmau na gaṇaratau na gaṇārāmataṃ anuyuttau, aikau pavivaikai abhiramissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘aikau pavivaikai abhiramantau cittassa nimittaṃ gahaissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘cittassa nimittaṃ gaṇhantau sammādiṭṭhiṃ paripūraissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sammādiṭṭhiṃ paripūraitvā sammāsamādhiṃ paripūraissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sammāsamādhiṃ  paripūraitvā saṃyaujanāni pajahissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘saṃyaujanāni pahāya nibbānaṃ sacchikarissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. daivatāsuttaṃ

69. atha  khau añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘chayimai, bhantai, dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai cha? satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā, saṅghagāravatā, sikkhāgāravatā, sauvacassatā, kalyāṇamittatā – imai khau, bhantai, cha dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| idamavauca sā daivatā| samanuñ‍ñau satthā ahausi| atha khau sā daivatā ‘‘samanuñ‍ñau mai satthā’’ti bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyi|

atha  khau bhagavā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau, bhikkhavai, sā daivatā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘chayimai, bhantai, dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai cha? satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā, saṅghagāravatā, sikkhāgāravatā, sauvacassatā, kalyāṇamittatā – imai khau, bhantai, cha dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’ti| idamavauca, bhikkhavai, sā daivatā| idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘imassa khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| idha, bhantai, bhikkhu attanā ca satthugāravau hauti satthugāravatāya ca vaṇṇavādī| yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū na satthugāravā tai ca satthugāravatāya samādapaiti| yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū satthugāravā taisañ‍ca vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina| attanā ca dhammagāravau hauti…pai॰… saṅghagāravau hauti… sikkhāgāravau hauti  … suvacau hauti… kalyāṇamittau hauti kalyāṇamittatāya ca vaṇṇavādī| yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū na kalyāṇamittā tai ca kalyāṇamittatāya samādapaiti| yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū kalyāṇamittā taisañ‍ca vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina| imassa khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā  saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! sādhu khau tvaṃ, sāriputta, imassa mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi| idha, sāriputta, bhikkhu attanā ca satthugāravau hauti  satthugāravatāya ca vaṇṇavādī| yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū na satthugāravā tai ca satthugāravatāya samādapaiti| yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū satthugāravā taisañ‍ca vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina| attanā ca dhammagāravau hauti…pai॰… saṅghagāravau hauti… sikkhāgāravau hauti… suvacau hauti… kalyāṇamittau hauti kalyāṇamittatāya ca vaṇṇavādī| yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū na kalyāṇamittā tai ca kalyāṇamittatāya samādapaiti| yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū kalyāṇamittā taisañ‍ca vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina| imassa khau, sāriputta, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. samādhisuttaṃ

70. ‘‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na santaina samādhinā na paṇītaina na paṭippassaddhiladdhaina na paṭippassaddhaladdhaina (sī॰) na aikaudibhāvādhigataina anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhavissati – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā bhavissati, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau bhavissati…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇissati – dibbai ca mānusai ca yai dūrai santikai cā’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānissati – sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittanti pajānissati  …pai॰… vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ cittanti pajānissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarissati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ, dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ  pubbainivāsaṃ anussarissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passissati…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘‘sau  vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu santaina samādhinā paṇītaina paṭippassaddhiladdhaina aikaudibhāvādhigataina anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhavissati…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇissati – dibbai ca mānusai ca yai dūrai santikai cā’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānissati – sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittanti pajānissati…pai॰… vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ cittanti pajānissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarissati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ, dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passissati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sakkhibhabbasuttaṃ

71. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇituṃ sati sati āyatanai| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘imai hānabhāgiyā dhammā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, ‘imai ṭhitibhāgiyā dhammā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, ‘imai visaisabhāgiyā dhammā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, ‘imai nibbaidhabhāgiyā dhammā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, asak‍kac‍cakārī ca hauti, asappāyakārī ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇituṃ sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇituṃ sati sati āyatanai| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘imai hānabhāgiyā dhammā’ti  yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘imai ṭhitibhāgiyā dhammā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘imai visaisabhāgiyā dhammā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘imai nibbaidhabhāgiyā dhammā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, sak‍kac‍cakārī ca hauti, sappāyakārī ca| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇituṃ sati sati āyatanai’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. balasuttaṃ
