72. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau samādhismiṃ samādhimhi (ka॰) balataṃ pāpuṇituṃ| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na  samādhissa samāpattikusalau hauti, na samādhissa ṭhitikusalau hauti, na samādhissa na samādhimhā (ka॰) uparisattakanipātai daivatāvaggai pana sabbatthapi ‘‘samādhissa’’itvaiva dissati vuṭṭhānakusalau hauti, asak‍kac‍cakārī ca hauti, asātac‍cakārī ca, asappāyakārī ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau samādhismiṃ balataṃ pāpuṇituṃ|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau samādhismiṃ balataṃ pāpuṇituṃ| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu samādhissa samāpattikusalau hauti, samādhissa ṭhitikusalau hauti, samādhissa vuṭṭhānakusalau hauti, sak‍kac‍cakārī ca hauti, sātac‍cakārī ca, sappāyakārī ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau samādhismiṃ balataṃ pāpuṇitu’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamatajjhānasuttaṃ

73. ‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ| katamai cha? kāmacchandaṃ, byāpādaṃ, thinamiddhaṃ, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ, vicikicchaṃ| kāmaisu khau panassa ādīnavau na yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai appahāya abhabbau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ|

‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ| katamai cha? kāmacchandaṃ, byāpādaṃ, thinamiddhaṃ, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ, vicikicchaṃ, kāmaisu khau panassa ādīnavau  na yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai pahāya bhabbau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharitu’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyatajjhānasuttaṃ

74. ‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ| katamai cha? kāmavitak‍kaṃ, byāpādavitak‍kaṃ, vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ, kāmasañ‍ñaṃ, byāpādasañ‍ñaṃ, vihiṃsāsañ‍ñaṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai appahāya abhabbau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ|

‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ| katamai cha? kāmavitak‍kaṃ, byāpādavitak‍kaṃ, vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ, kāmasañ‍ñaṃ, byāpādasañ‍ñaṃ, vihiṃsāsañ‍ñaṃ – imai  khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai pahāya bhabbau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharitu’’nti| dasamaṃ|

daivatāvaggau sattamau| dutiyau (syā॰ ka॰)


tassuddānaṃ –

anāgāmi arahaṃ mittā, saṅgaṇikārāmadaivatā|

samādhi sakkhibhabbaṃ balaṃ, tajjhānā aparai duvaiti||


8. arahattavaggau

1. dukkhasuttaṃ

75. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhaṃ viharati savighātaṃ saupāyāsaṃ sapariḷāhaṃ, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā duggati pāṭikaṅkhā| katamaihi chahi? kāmavitak‍kaina, byāpādavitak‍kaina, vihiṃsāvitak‍kaina, kāmasañ‍ñāya, byāpādasañ‍ñāya, vihiṃsāsañ‍ñāya – imaihi, khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhaṃ viharati savighātaṃ saupāyāsaṃ sapariḷāhaṃ, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā duggati pāṭikaṅkhā|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu diṭṭhaiva dhammai sukhaṃ viharati avighātaṃ anupāyāsaṃ apariḷāhaṃ, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ  maraṇā sugati pāṭikaṅkhā| katamaihi chahi? naikkhammavitak‍kaina, abyāpādavitak‍kaina, avihiṃsāvitak‍kaina, naikkhammasañ‍ñāya, abyāpādasañ‍ñāya, avihiṃsāsañ‍ñāya – imaihi, khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu diṭṭhaiva dhammai sukhaṃ viharati avighātaṃ anupāyāsaṃ apariḷāhaṃ, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugati pāṭikaṅkhā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. arahattasuttaṃ

76. ‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamai cha? mānaṃ, aumānaṃ, atimānaṃ, adhimānaṃ, thambhaṃ, atinipātaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai appahāya abhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ|

‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamai cha? mānaṃ, aumānaṃ, atimānaṃ, adhimānaṃ, thambhaṃ, atinipātaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai pahāya bhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātu’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. uttarimanussadhammasuttaṃ

77. ‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau uttarimanussadhammaṃ alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamai cha? muṭṭhassac‍caṃ, asampajañ‍ñaṃ, indriyaisu aguttadvārataṃ, bhaujanai amattañ‍ñutaṃ, kuhanaṃ, lapanaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai appahāya abhabbau uttarimanussadhammaṃ alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ sacchikātuṃ|

‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau uttarimanussadhammaṃ alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ  sacchikātuṃ| katamai cha? muṭṭhassac‍caṃ, asampajañ‍ñaṃ, indriyaisu aguttadvārataṃ, bhaujanai amattañ‍ñutaṃ, kuhanaṃ, lapanaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai pahāya bhabbau uttarimanussadhammaṃ alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ sacchikātu’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. sukhasaumanassasuttaṃ

78. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu diṭṭhaiva dhammai sukhasaumanassabahulau viharati, yauni cassa āraddhā hauti āsavānaṃ khayāya| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammārāmau hauti, bhāvanārāmau hauti, pahānārāmau hauti, pavivaikārāmau hauti, abyāpajjhārāmau hauti, nippapañ‍cārāmau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu diṭṭhaiva dhammai sukhasaumanassabahulau viharati, yauni cassa āraddhā hauti āsavānaṃ khayāyā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. adhigamasuttaṃ

79. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau anadhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigantuṃ, adhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ phātiṃ kātuṃ phātikattuṃ (sī॰), phātikātuṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  na āyakusalau ca hauti, na apāyakusalau ca hauti, na upāyakusalau ca hauti, anadhigatānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ adhigamāya na chandaṃ janaiti, adhigatai kusalai dhammai na ārakkhati sārakkhati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī), sātac‍cakiriyāya na sampādaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau anadhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigantuṃ, adhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ phātiṃ kātuṃ|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau anadhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigantuṃ, adhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ phātiṃ kātuṃ| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āyakusalau ca hauti, apāyakusalau ca hauti, upāyakusalau ca hauti, anadhigatānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ  adhigamāya chandaṃ janaiti, adhigatai kusalai dhammai ārakkhati, sātac‍cakiriyāya sampādaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau anadhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigantuṃ, adhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ phātiṃ kātu’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. mahantattasuttaṃ

80. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nacirassaiva mahantattaṃ mahattaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) vaipul‍lattaṃ pāpuṇāti dhammaisu| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ālaukabahulau ca hauti yaugabahulau ca vaidabahulau ca asantuṭṭhibahulau ca anikkhittadhurau ca kusalaisu dhammaisu uttari ca patāraiti pakarauti (ka॰)| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nacirassaiva mahantattaṃ vaipul‍lattaṃ pāpuṇāti dhammaisū’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamanirayasuttaṃ

81. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi chahi? pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, musāvādī hauti, pāpicchau ca, micchādiṭṭhi ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi chahi? pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, appicchau ca, sammādiṭṭhi ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyanirayasuttaṃ

82. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi chahi musāvādī hauti, pisuṇavācā hauti, pharusavācau hauti, samphappalāpī hauti, (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) aivaṃ suk‍kapakkhaipi? pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, musāvādī hauti musāvādī hauti, pisuṇavācā hauti, pharusavācau hauti, samphappalāpī hauti, (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) aivaṃ suk‍kapakkhaipi, luddhau ca, pagabbhau ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi chahi? pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, aluddhau ca, appagabbhau ca| imaihi khau bhikkhavai chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. aggadhammasuttaṃ

83. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau aggaṃ dhammaṃ arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu assaddhau hauti, ahirikau hauti, anauttappī hauti, kusītau hauti, duppañ‍ñau  hauti, kāyai ca jīvitai ca sāpaikkhau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau aggaṃ dhammaṃ arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau aggaṃ dhammaṃ arahattaṃ  sacchikātuṃ| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau hauti, hirimā hauti, auttappī hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti, kāyai ca jīvitai ca anapaikkhau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau aggaṃ dhammaṃ arahattaṃ sacchikātu’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. rattidivasasuttaṃ

84. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati hāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau vuddhi| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mahicchau hauti, vighātavā, asantuṭṭhau, itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina, assaddhau hauti, dussīlau hauti, kusītau hauti, muṭṭhassati hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati hāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau vuddhi|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihāni| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na mahicchau hauti, avighātavā, santuṭṭhau, itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina, saddhau hauti, sīlavā hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, satimā hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā, kusalaisu dhammaisu nau parihānī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

arahattavaggau aṭṭhamau| tatiyau (syā॰ ka॰)


tassuddānaṃ –

dukkhaṃ  arahattaṃ uttari ca, sukhaṃ adhigamaina ca|

mahantattaṃ dvayaṃ nirayai mahattadvayanirayai (syā॰), aggadhammañ‍ca rattiyauti||


9. sītivaggau

1. sītibhāvasuttaṃ

85. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamaihi chahi visuddhi॰ 1.64 ādayau vitthārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ niggahaitabbaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ na niggaṇhāti, yasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ paggahaitabbaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ na paggaṇhāti, yasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ sampahaṃsitabbaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ na sampahaṃsaiti, yasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ ajjhupaikkhitabbaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ na ajjhupaikkhati, hīnādhimuttikau ca hauti, sak‍kāyābhiratau ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ sacchikātuṃ|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ niggahaitabbaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ niggaṇhāti, yasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ paggahaitabbaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ paggaṇhāti, yasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ sampahaṃsitabbaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ sampahaṃsaiti, yasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ ajjhupaikkhitabbaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ ajjhupaikkhati, paṇītādhimuttikau ca hauti, nibbānābhiratau ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ sacchikātu’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. āvaraṇasuttaṃ

86. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantaupi saddhammaṃ abhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ| katamaihi chahi? kammāvaraṇatāya  saman‍nāgatau hauti, kilaisāvaraṇatāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, vipākāvaraṇatāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, assaddhau ca hauti, acchandikau ca, duppañ‍ñau ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantaupi saddhammaṃ abhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantau saddhammaṃ bhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ| katamaihi chahi? na kammāvaraṇatāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, na kilaisāvaraṇatāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, na vipākāvaraṇatāya saman‍nāgatau hauti, saddhau ca hauti, chandikau ca, pañ‍ñavā ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantau saddhammaṃ bhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammatta’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. vauraupitasuttaṃ

87. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantaupi saddhammaṃ abhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ| katamaihi chahi? mātā  jīvitā vauraupitā hauti, pitā jīvitā vauraupitau hauti, arahaṃ arahā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), arahantau (ka॰) jīvitā vauraupitau hauti, tathāgatassa duṭṭhaina cittaina lauhitaṃ uppāditaṃ hauti, saṅghau bhin‍nau hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti jaḷau aiḷamūgau| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantaupi saddhammaṃ abhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantau saddhammaṃ bhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ| katamaihi chahi? na  mātā jīvitā vauraupitā hauti, na pitā jīvitā vauraupitau hauti, na arahaṃ jīvitā vauraupitau hauti, na tathāgatassa duṭṭhaina cittaina lauhitaṃ uppāditaṃ hauti, na saṅghau bhin‍nau hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti ajaḷau anaiḷamūgau| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau  suṇantau saddhammaṃ bhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammatta’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. sussūsatisuttaṃ

88. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantaupi saddhammaṃ abhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ| katamaihi chahi? tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai daisiyamānai na sussūsati, na sautaṃ audahati, na añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti upaṭṭhapaiti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), anatthaṃ gaṇhāti, atthaṃ riñ‍cati, ananulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi  saman‍nāgatau suṇantaupi saddhammaṃ abhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantau saddhammaṃ bhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ| katamaihi chahi? tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai daisiyamānai sussūsati, sautaṃ audahati, añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, atthaṃ gaṇhāti, anatthaṃ riñ‍cati, anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau suṇantau saddhammaṃ bhabbau niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammatta’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. appahāyasuttaṃ

89. ‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau diṭṭhisampadaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamai cha? sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiṃ, vicikicchaṃ, sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ, apāyagamanīyaṃ rāgaṃ, apāyagamanīyaṃ dausaṃ, apāyagamanīyaṃ mauhaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai appahāya abhabbau diṭṭhisampadaṃ sacchikātuṃ|

‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau diṭṭhisampadaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamai cha? sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiṃ, vicikicchaṃ, sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ, apāyagamanīyaṃ rāgaṃ, apāyagamanīyaṃ dausaṃ, apāyagamanīyaṃ mauhaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai pahāya bhabbau diṭṭhisampadaṃ sacchikātu’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. pahīnasuttaṃ

90. ‘‘chayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa pahīnā| katamai cha? sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi, vicikicchā, sīlabbataparāmāsau, apāyagamanīyau rāgau, apāyagamanīyau dausau, apāyagamanīyau mauhau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammā diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa pahīnā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. abhabbasuttaṃ

91. ‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau uppādaituṃ| katamai cha? sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiṃ, vicikicchaṃ, sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ, apāyagamanīyaṃ rāgaṃ, apāyagamanīyaṃ dausaṃ, apāyagamanīyaṃ mauhaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau uppādaitu’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. paṭhamaabhabbaṭṭhānasuttaṃ

92. ‘‘chayimāni, bhikkhavai, abhabbaṭṭhānāni| katamāni cha? abhabbau  diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau satthari agāravau viharituṃ appatissau, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau dhammai agāravau viharituṃ appatissau, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau saṅghai agāravau viharituṃ appatissau, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau sikkhāya agāravau viharituṃ appatissau, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau anāgamanīyaṃ vatthuṃ pac‍cāgantuṃ, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau aṭṭhamaṃ bhavaṃ nibbattaituṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cha abhabbaṭṭhānānī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyaabhabbaṭṭhānasuttaṃ

93. ‘‘chayimāni, bhikkhavai, abhabbaṭṭhānāni| katamāni cha? abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau kañ‍ci kiñ‍ci (ka॰) vibha॰ 809; ma॰ ni॰ 3.127 saṅkhāraṃ nic‍catau upagantuṃ, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau kañ‍ci saṅkhāraṃ sukhatau upagantuṃ, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau kañ‍ci dhammaṃ attatau upagantuṃ, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau ānantariyaṃ kammaṃ ānantariyakammaṃ (sī॰), anantariyakammaṃ (syā॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 4.162 passitabbaṃ kātuṃ, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau kautūhalamaṅgalaina suddhiṃ pac‍cāgantuṃ, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau itau bahiddhā dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ gavaisituṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cha abhabbaṭṭhānānī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. tatiyaabhabbaṭṭhānasuttaṃ

94. ‘‘chayimāni, bhikkhavai, abhabbaṭṭhānāni| katamāni cha? abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau  puggalau  mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaituṃ, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau pitaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaituṃ, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau arahantaṃ jīvitā vauraupaituṃ, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau tathāgatassa duṭṭhaina cittaina lauhitaṃ uppādaituṃ, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau saṅghaṃ bhindituṃ, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau añ‍ñaṃ satthāraṃ uddisituṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cha abhabbaṭṭhānānī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. catutthaabhabbaṭṭhānasuttaṃ

95. ‘‘chayimāni, bhikkhavai, abhabbaṭṭhānāni| katamāni cha? abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau sayaṃkataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ pac‍cāgantuṃ, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau paraṃkataṃ parakataṃ (sī॰ syā॰) sukhadukkhaṃ pac‍cāgantuṃ, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca sukhadukkhaṃ pac‍cāgantuṃ, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau asayaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ sukhadukkhaṃ pac‍cāgantuṃ, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ sukhadukkhaṃ pac‍cāgantuṃ, abhabbau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau asayaṃkārañ‍ca aparaṃkārañ‍ca adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ sukhadukkhaṃ pac‍cāgantuṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hissa, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa haitu ca sudiṭṭhau haitusamuppan‍nā ca dhammā| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cha abhabbaṭṭhānānī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

sītivaggau navamau| catutthau (syā॰ ka॰)


tassuddānaṃ –

sītibhāvaṃ āvaraṇaṃ, vauraupitā sussūsati|

appahāya pahīnābhabbau, taṭṭhānā caturaupi cāti||


10. ānisaṃsavaggau

1. pātubhāvasuttaṃ

96. ‘‘chan‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| katamaisaṃ chan‍naṃ? tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ, tathāgatappavaiditassa dhammavinayassa daisaitā puggalau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ, ariyāyatanai pac‍cājāti dul‍labhā pac‍cājātau dul‍labhau (syā॰) laukasmiṃ, indriyānaṃ avaikal‍latā dul‍labhā laukasmiṃ, ajaḷatā anaiḷamūgatā dul‍labhā laukasmiṃ, kusalai dhammai chandau kusaladhammacchandau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, chan‍naṃ pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmi’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. ānisaṃsasuttaṃ

97. ‘‘chayimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya| katamai cha? saddhammaniyatau hauti, aparihānadhammau hauti, pariyantakatassa dukkhaṃ hauti dukkhaṃ na hauti (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰), asādhāraṇaina ñāṇaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, haitu cassa sudiṭṭhau, haitusamuppan‍nā ca dhammā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha ānisaṃsā sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāyā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. anic‍casuttaṃ

98. ‘‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kañ‍ci saṅkhāraṃ nic‍catau samanupassantau anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau bhavissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘anulaumikāya khantiyā asaman‍nāgatau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sammattaniyāmaṃ anauk‍kamamānau sautāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaṃ arahattaphalaṃ (ka॰) paṭi॰ ma॰ 3.36 vā sacchikarissatī’ti naitaṃ  ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘‘sau  vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasaṅkhārai sabbasaṅkhāraṃ (sī॰ pī॰) anic‍catau samanupassantau anulaumikāya khantiyā  saman‍nāgatau bhavissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamamānau sautāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ  vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaṃ vā sacchikarissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dukkhasuttaṃ

99. ‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kañ‍ci saṅkhāraṃ sukhatau samanupassantau…pai॰… sabbasaṅkhārai dukkhatau samanupassantau…pai॰… ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati’’| catutthaṃ|

5. anattasuttaṃ

100. ‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kañ‍ci dhammaṃ attatau samanupassantau…pai॰… sabbadhammai sabbadhammaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), kiñ‍cidhammaṃ (ka॰) paṭi॰ ma॰ 3.36 anattatau samanupassantau…pai॰… ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati’’| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. nibbānasuttaṃ

101. ‘‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu nibbānaṃ dukkhatau samanupassantau anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau bhavissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘anulaumikāya khantiyā asaman‍nāgatau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sammattaniyāmaṃ anauk‍kamamānau sautāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaṃ vā sacchikarissatī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘‘sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu nibbānaṃ sukhatau samanupassantau anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau bhavissatī’ti  ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘anulaumikāya khantiyā saman‍nāgatau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sammattaniyāmaṃ  auk‍kamamānau sautāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaṃ vā sacchikarissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. anavatthitasuttaṃ

102. ‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsai sampassamānaina alamaiva bhikkhunā sabbasaṅkhāraisu anaudhiṃ karitvā anic‍casañ‍ñaṃ upaṭṭhāpaituṃ| katamai cha? ‘sabbasaṅkhārā ca mai anavatthitā anavaṭṭhitatau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) khāyissanti, sabbalaukai ca mai manau nābhiramissati  na ramissati (ka॰), sabbalaukā ca mai manau vuṭṭhahissati, nibbānapauṇañ‍ca mai mānasaṃ bhavissati, saṃyaujanā ca mai pahānaṃ gacchissanti gacchanti (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰), paramaina ca sāmañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau bhavissāmī’ti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha ānisaṃsai sampassamānaina alamaiva bhikkhunā sabbasaṅkhāraisu anaudhiṃ karitvā anic‍casañ‍ñaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitu’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. ukkhittāsikasuttaṃ

103. ‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsai sampassamānaina alamaiva bhikkhunā sabbasaṅkhāraisu anaudhiṃ karitvā dukkhasañ‍ñaṃ upaṭṭhāpaituṃ| katamai cha? ‘sabbasaṅkhāraisu ca mai nibbidasañ‍ñā pac‍cupaṭṭhitā bhavissati, saiyyathāpi ukkhittāsikai vadhakai| sabbalaukā ca mai manau vuṭṭhahissati, nibbānai ca santadassāvī bhavissāmi, anusayā ca mai samugghātaṃ gacchissanti gacchanti (pī॰ ka॰), kic‍cakārī ca bhavissāmi, satthā ca mai pariciṇṇau bhavissati maittāvatāyā’ti| imai  khau, bhikkhavai, cha ānisaṃsai sampassamānaina alamaiva bhikkhunā sabbasaṅkhāraisu anaudhiṃ karitvā dukkhasañ‍ñaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitu’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. atammayasuttaṃ

104. ‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsai sampassamānaina alamaiva bhikkhunā sabbadhammaisu anaudhiṃ karitvā anattasañ‍ñaṃ upaṭṭhāpaituṃ| katamai cha? sabbalaukai  ca atammayau bhavissāmi, ahaṅkārā ca mai uparujjhissanti, mamaṅkārā ca mai uparujjhissanti, asādhāraṇaina ca ñāṇaina saman‍nāgatau bhavissāmi, haitu ca mai sudiṭṭhau bhavissati, haitusamuppan‍nā ca dhammā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha ānisaṃsai sampassamānaina alamaiva bhikkhunā sabbadhammaisu anaudhiṃ karitvā anattasañ‍ñaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitu’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. bhavasuttaṃ

105. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, bhavā pahātabbā, tīsu sikkhāsu sikkhitabbaṃ| katamai tayau bhavā pahātabbā? kāmabhavau, rūpabhavau, arūpabhavau  – imai tayau bhavā pahātabbā| katamāsu tīsu sikkhāsu sikkhitabbaṃ? adhisīlasikkhāya, adhicittasikkhāya, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāya – imāsu tīsu sikkhāsu sikkhitabbaṃ| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau imai tayau bhavā pahīnā haunti, imāsu ca tīsu sikkhāsu sikkhitasikkhau hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu acchaicchi taṇhaṃ, vivattayi saṃyaujanaṃ, sammā mānābhisamayā antamakāsi dukkhassā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. taṇhāsuttaṃ

106. ‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhavai, taṇhā pahātabbā, tayau ca mānā| katamā tissau taṇhā pahātabbā? kāmataṇhā, bhavataṇhā, vibhavataṇhā – imā tissau taṇhā pahātabbā| katamai tayau mānā pahātabbā? mānau, aumānau, atimānau – imai tayau mānā pahātabbā| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau imā tissau taṇhā pahīnā haunti, imai ca tayau mānā; ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu acchaicchi taṇhaṃ, vivattayi saṃyaujanaṃ, sammā mānābhisamayā antamakāsi dukkhassā’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

ānisaṃsavaggau dasamau| pañ‍camau (syā॰ ka॰)


tassuddānaṃ –

pātubhāvau  ānisaṃsau, anic‍cadukkhaanattatau|

nibbānaṃ anavatthi, ukkhittāsi atammayau|

bhavā taṇhāyaikā dasāti||

dutiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



11. tikavaggau

1. rāgasuttaṃ

107. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā| katamai tayau? rāgau, dausau, mauhau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai tayau? rāgassa  pahānāya asubhā bhāvaitabbā, dausassa pahānāya maittā bhāvaitabbā, mauhassa pahānāya pañ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya imai tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. duc‍caritasuttaṃ

108. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā| katamai tayau? kāyaduc‍caritaṃ, vacīduc‍caritaṃ, manauduc‍caritaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai tayau? kāyaduc‍caritassa pahānāya kāyasucaritaṃ bhāvaitabbaṃ, vacīduc‍caritassa pahānāya vacīsucaritaṃ bhāvaitabbaṃ, manauduc‍caritassa pahānāya manausucaritaṃ bhāvaitabbaṃ| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya imai tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. vitak‍kasuttaṃ

109. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā| katamai tayau? kāmavitak‍kau, byāpādavitak‍kau, vihiṃsāvitak‍kau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai tayau? kāmavitak‍kassa pahānāya naikkhammavitak‍kau bhāvaitabbau, byāpādavitak‍kassa  pahānāya abyāpādavitak‍kau bhāvaitabbau, vihiṃsāvitak‍kassa pahānāya  avihiṃsāvitak‍kau bhāvaitabbau| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya imai tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. sañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

110. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā| katamai tayau? kāmasañ‍ñā, byāpādasañ‍ñā, vihiṃsāsañ‍ñā| imai  khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai tayau? kāmasañ‍ñāya pahānāya naikkhammasañ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā, byāpādasañ‍ñāya pahānāya abyāpādasañ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā, vihiṃsāsañ‍ñāya pahānāya avihiṃsāsañ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya imai tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dhātusuttaṃ

111. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā| katamai tayau? kāmadhātu, byāpādadhātu, vihiṃsādhātu| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai tayau? kāmadhātuyā pahānāya naikkhammadhātu bhāvaitabbā, byāpādadhātuyā pahānāya abyāpādadhātu bhāvaitabbā, vihiṃsādhātuyā pahānāya avihiṃsādhātu bhāvaitabbā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya imai tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. assādasuttaṃ

112. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā| katamai tayau? assādadiṭṭhi, attānudiṭṭhi, micchādiṭṭhi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai tayau? assādadiṭṭhiyā pahānāya anic‍casañ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā, attānudiṭṭhiyā pahānāya anattasañ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā, micchādiṭṭhiyā pahānāya sammādiṭṭhi bhāvaitabbā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya imai tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. aratisuttaṃ

113. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā| katamai tayau? arati, vihiṃsā vihaisā (ka॰), adhammacariyā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ  dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai tayau? aratiyā pahānāya muditā bhāvaitabbā, vihiṃsāya pahānāya avihiṃsā bhāvaitabbā, adhammacariyāya pahānāya dhammacariyā bhāvaitabbā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya imai tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. santuṭṭhitāsuttaṃ

114. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā| katamai tayau? asantuṭṭhitā, asampajañ‍ñaṃ, mahicchatā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai tayau? asantuṭṭhitāya pahānāya santuṭṭhitā bhāvaitabbā, asampajañ‍ñassa pahānāya sampajañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaitabbaṃ, mahicchatāya pahānāya appicchatā bhāvaitabbā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya imai tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dauvacassatāsuttaṃ

115. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā| katamai tayau? dauvacassatā, pāpamittatā, caitasau vikkhaipau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammā| imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai tayau? dauvacassatāya  pahānāya sauvacassatā bhāvaitabbā, pāpamittatāya pahānāya kalyāṇamittatā bhāvaitabbā, caitasau vikkhaipassa pahānāya ānāpānassati bhāvaitabbā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya imai tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. uddhac‍casuttaṃ

116. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā| katamai tayau? uddhac‍caṃ, asaṃvarau, pamādau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai tayau? uddhac‍cassa pahānāya samathau bhāvaitabbau, asaṃvarassa pahānāya saṃvarau bhāvaitabbau, pamādassa pahānāya appamādau bhāvaitabbau| imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya imai tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

tikavaggau aikādasamau| paṭhamau (syā॰)


tassuddānaṃ –

rāgaduc‍caritavitak‍ka, sañ‍ñā dhātūti vuc‍cati|

assādaaratituṭṭhi, duvai ca uddhac‍caina vaggauti||


12. sāmañ‍ñavaggau

1. kāyānupassīsuttaṃ

117. ‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau kāyai kāyānupassī viharituṃ| katamai cha? kammārāmataṃ, bhassārāmataṃ, niddārāmataṃ, saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ, indriyaisu aguttadvārataṃ, bhaujanai amattañ‍ñutaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai appahāya abhabbau kāyai kāyānupassī viharituṃ|

‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau kāyai kāyānupassī viharituṃ| katamai cha? kammārāmataṃ, bhassārāmataṃ, niddārāmataṃ, saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ, indriyaisu aguttadvārataṃ, bhaujanai amattañ‍ñutaṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai pahāya bhabbau kāyai kāyānupassī viharitu’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dhammānupassīsuttaṃ

118. ‘‘cha, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau ajjhattaṃ kāyai…pai॰… bahiddhā kāyai…pai॰… ajjhattabahiddhā kāyai…pai॰… ajjhattaṃ vaidanāsu…pai॰… bahiddhā vaidanāsu…pai॰… ajjhattabahiddhā vaidanāsu…pai॰… ajjhattaṃ cittai…pai॰… bahiddhā cittai…pai॰… ajjhattabahiddhā cittai…pai॰… ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu…pai॰… bahiddhā dhammaisu…pai॰… ajjhattabahiddhā  dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharituṃ| katamai cha? kammārāmataṃ, bhassārāmataṃ, niddārāmataṃ, saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ, indriyaisu aguttadvārataṃ, bhaujanai amattañ‍ñutaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha dhammai pahāya bhabbau ajjhattabahiddhā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharitu’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. tapussasuttaṃ

119. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau tapussau tapassau (pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 1.248 gahapati tathāgatai niṭṭhaṅgatau amataddasau amataṃ sacchikatvā iriyati| katamaihi chahi? buddhai  avaic‍cappasādaina, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina, saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina, ariyaina sīlaina, ariyaina ñāṇaina, ariyāya vimuttiyā| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau tapussau gahapati tathāgatai niṭṭhaṅgatau amataddasau amataṃ sacchikatvā iriyatī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4-23. bhal‍likādisuttāni

120-139. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhal‍likau gahapati…pai॰… sudattau gahapati anāthapiṇḍikau… cittau gahapati macchikāsaṇḍikau… hatthakau āḷavakau… mahānāmau sak‍kau… uggau gahapati vaisālikau… uggatau gahapati… sūrambaṭṭhau sūrau ambaṭṭhau (ka॰) … jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau… nakulapitā gahapati… tavakaṇṇikau gahapati… pūraṇau gahapati… isidattau gahapati… sandhānau santānau (ka॰) gahapati… vicayau vijayau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) gahapati… vijayamāhikau vaj‍jiyamahitau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) gahapati… maiṇḍakau gahapati … vāsaiṭṭhau upāsakau… ariṭṭhau upāsakau… sāraggau sādattau (syā॰) upāsakau tathāgatai niṭṭhaṅgatau amataddasau amataṃ sacchikatvā iriyati| katamaihi chahi? buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina, saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina, ariyaina sīlaina, ariyaina ñāṇaina, ariyāya vimuttiyā| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sāraggau upāsakau tathāgatai niṭṭhaṅgatau amataddasau amataṃ sacchikatvā iriyatī’’ti| taivīsatimaṃ|

sāmañ‍ñavaggau dvādasamau|



13. rāgapaiyyālaṃ

140. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya cha dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cha? dassanānuttariyaṃ, savanānuttariyaṃ, lābhānuttariyaṃ, sikkhānuttariyaṃ, pāricariyānuttariyaṃ, anussatānuttariyaṃ| rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai cha dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

141. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya cha dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cha? buddhānussati, dhammānussati, saṅghānussati, sīlānussati, cāgānussati, daivatānussati| rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai cha dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

142. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya cha dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cha? anic‍casañ‍ñā, anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā, pahānasañ‍ñā, virāgasañ‍ñā, niraudhasañ‍ñā| rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai cha dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

143-169. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñāya…pai॰… parikkhayāya… pahānāya… khayāya… vayāya… virāgāya… niraudhāya… cāgāya… paṭinissaggāya cha dhammā  bhāvaitabbā’’|

170-649. ‘‘dausassa…pai॰… mauhassa… kaudhassa… upanāhassa… makkhassa… paḷāsassa… issāya… macchariyassa… māyāya… sāṭhaiyyassa… thambhassa… sārambhassa… mānassa… atimānassa… madassa… pamādassa abhiñ‍ñāya…pai॰… pariñ‍ñāya… parikkhayāya… pahanāya… khayāya… vayāya… virāgāya… niraudhāya… cāgāya… paṭinissaggāya imai cha dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

rāgapaiyyālaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|


chak‍kanipātapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
